01 · Astronomy and Astrophysics · 11 月 25 日新收录

位于上升氦巨星支的恒星。II. 一颗偏离正轨的富金属高光度氦 sdO

原始发布日期：2026-11-25

质量高于 sim0.7 M_⊙ 的热亚矮星在核心氦耗尽后膨胀，并在进入白矮星（WD）冷却轨迹之前沿氦巨星支上升。在本系列的第一篇论文中，我们确认高光度富氦热亚矮星 HE 1518-0948（HE 1518）是处于这一演化阶段的大质量 He-WD+He-WD 并合产物。在此，我们对第二颗高光度富氦 O 型热亚矮星（He-sdO）Gaia DR3 5369742867090186624（G5369）进行了详细分析。我们推导出其有效温度为 55 000 K、表面重力为 łog g = 4.85，并得到氢丰度上限 łognHE$>2.5$。其大气参数以及氦和碳丰度与 HE 1518 极为相似，这表明 G5369 也很可能是大质量 He-WD+He-WD 并合的产物。与 HE 1518 不同，G5369 富含金属，其铁丰度约为太阳的两倍。对于 C、N、O 和 Ne 元素，G5369 经金属丰度校正的丰度模式与复合 He-WD 并合模型的核合成预测大体一致，并且类似于观测到的 R CrB 星、极端氦星和 O(He) 星 KPD 0005+5106 的丰度。G5369 的运动学特征使其位于厚盘与晕的边界附近。其轨道近心点为 ∼3.5 kpc，加之其高金属丰度，表明 G5369 最有可能起源于银河系内部。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Stars on the ascending helium giant branch. II. A metal-rich luminous helium sdO gone astray

Hot subdwarf stars with masses above sim0.7 M_⊙ expand after core helium exhaustion and ascend the helium giant branch before entering the white dwarf (WD) cooling track. In the first paper of this series, we identified the luminous helium-rich hot subdwarf HE 1518-0948 (HE 1518) as the product of a massive He-WD+He-WD merger in this evolutionary phase. Here we present a detailed analysis of a second luminous helium-rich O-type hot subdwarf (He-sdO), Gaia DR3 5369742867090186624 (G5369). We derive an effective temperature of 55 000 K, a surface gravity of łog g = 4.85 and an upper limit on the hydrogen abundance of łognHE$>2.5$. The close similarity of its atmospheric parameters and its helium and carbon abundances to those of HE 1518 indicates that G5369 is also likely the product of a massive He-WD+He-WD merger. In contrast to HE 1518, G5369 is metal-rich, with an iron abundance of about twice solar. The metallicity-adjusted abundance pattern of G5369 for the elements C, N, O, and Ne is broadly consistent with the nucleosynthesis predictions of a composite He-WD merger model and is similar to the observed abundances of R CrB stars, extreme helium stars, and the O(He) star KPD 0005+5106. The kinematics of G5369 place it near the boundary between the thick disk and halo. Its orbit, with a pericentre of ∼3.5 kpc, together with its high metallicity suggests that G5369 most likely originated in the inner Galaxy.

02 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 25 日新收录

核系统中的反冲核子效应

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

核激发能通常处于 keV-MeV 范围，但罕见的 eV 尺度跃迁对于核理论而言仍是一个谜。本工作基于经典动量守恒，建立了一个描述 $\beta^-$ 衰变的二体反冲模型。利用具有 keV 尺度激发能的 $\beta^-$ 衰变末态，该模型得到了单个核子的 eV 尺度反冲能。它表明了核系统中的反冲核子效应：束缚在原子核中的单个核子可以通过吸收反冲动量而被激发。在 $^{235}$U 和 $^{229}$Th 中观测到的 eV 尺度同核异能态可归因于反冲核子效应。这种效应能够激发 eV 尺度的能量，并为预测这种极小能量提供坚实的理论基础。这一新发现将使低能核同核异能态研究处于新物理搜寻的前沿，同时为高能加速器实验提供一种清晰且互补的替代方案。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

The recoil nucleon effect in nuclear systems

Nuclear excitation energies are typically in the keV-MeV range, but rare eV-scale transitions remain a puzzle for nuclear theory. This work develops a two-body recoil model to describe $\beta^-$ decay on the basis of classic momentum conservation. Using the final states of $\beta^-$ decay with a keV-scale excitation energy, the model yields eV-scale recoil energy for a single nucleon. It indicates the recoil nucleon effect in nuclear systems: a single nucleon bound in a nucleus can be excited by absorbing the recoil momentum. The observed eV-scale isomers in $^{235}$U and $^{229}$Th can be attributed to the recoil neucleon effect. This effect could spark a eV-scale energy, and provide a solid theoretical foundation for predicting such extremely small energies. The new discovery will place low-energy nuclear isomer research at the cutting edge of new-physics searches, while offers a clean and complementary alternative to high-energy accelerator experiments.

04 · arXiv nucl-ex · 11 月 25 日新收录

快中子辐照 Lu 同位素上隐蔽核反应的观测

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

某些核反应道可能不易识别，但它们仍可能对核反应截面、同核异能态产额等非常重要的特性作出贡献。双中子作为由两个中子组成的束缚原子核，可以发挥隐蔽作用，因为它被认为是涉及镥同位素的快中子诱发核反应所产生的产物。当在输出道中形成时，束缚双中子可以解释反应截面的意外增强。为了理解双中子的形成及后续衰变对核反应产物转变造成的后果，我们利用现有数据和仪器谱深入考察了双中子的作用。结果，我们获得了实验证据，证实 176gLu 的半衰期在某一时间区间内显著缩短，这揭示了对其截面为 10E+11 b 的极高估计值。这实际上是由于 176gLu 的“燃耗”，随后发生 175Lu 与氘核之间的聚变。这引入了中子辐照下新颖的低能反应和聚变路径的存在，进而对基础核物理和高能物理及其应用产生影响。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Observation of hidden nuclear reactions on fast neutron-irradiated Lu isotopes

Some nuclear reaction channels may not be easily identified, but they can still contribute to very important characteristics like nuclear reaction cross-sections, isomeric yields, etc. The dineutron, as a bound nucleus of two neutrons, can play hidden roles, as it is considered a product resulting from fast neutron-induced nuclear reactions involving the isotopes of lutetium. When being formed in the output channels, a bound dineutron allows for an explanation for unexpected enhancement of reaction cross-sections. We used available data and instrumental spectra to deeply consider the role of the dineutron in order to understand the consequences of its formation and subsequent decay upon transformations of nuclear reaction products. As a result, we obtained experimental confirmation that the half-life of 176gLu is significantly diminished within a certain time interval, which reveals an extremely high estimation for its cross-section of 10E+11 b. This is actually due to 176gLu "burnup", followed by the fusion between 175Lu and a deuteron. This introduces the existence of novel, low-energy reactions and fusion pathways under neutron irradiation, which in-turn, leads to implications for both basic nuclear physics and high-energy physics, along with their applications.

05 · arXiv nucl-ex · 11 月 25 日新收录

使用 $^{241}$Pu 测量中微子质量

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

长期以来，氚一直是绝对中微子质量测量的首选同位素。其较低的核质量会导致显著的反冲能，从而限制使用分子氚（$T_2$）和束缚于衬底的原子氚进行的测量。$^{241}$Pu 的 β 衰变端点能和半衰期与氚相近，但核质量大得多，因此可能提供一种有意义的替代方案。其主要挑战是来自 $^{237}$U β 衰变的本底。我们讨论了抑制这种本底的策略，并比较了 $^{241}$Pu 和氚的预期中微子质量灵敏度随本底抑制程度的变化。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Measuring the Neutrino Mass with $^{241}$Pu

Tritium has long been the isotope of choice for measurements of the absolute neutrino mass. Its low nuclear mass leads to substantial recoil energy, limiting measurements with both molecular tritium ($T_2$) and atomic tritium bound to a substrate. With a beta-decay endpoint energy and half-life similar to those of tritium, but a much larger nuclear mass, $^{241}$Pu may offer an interesting alternative. Its principal challenge is background from the beta decay of $^{237}$U. We discuss strategies for suppressing this background and compare the projected neutrino-mass sensitivities of $^{241}$Pu and tritium as a function of the background suppression.

06 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 25 日新收录

能量密度泛函理论中由模拟器赋能的 $\beta$ 衰变计算校准与不确定度量化

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

富中子原子核的 $\beta$ 衰变半衰期预测及其不确定度对我们理解天体物理快速中子俘获过程具有关键影响。为了在核能量密度泛函理论框架内提供量化预测，我们按照惯常做法，对两个耦合常数进行贝叶斯校准：同标量质子-中子配对强度和同矢量自旋密度耦合。我们采用 E-最优实验设计来选择校准集。我们使用带有 Skyrme 能量密度泛函的质子-中子有限振幅方法，并采用半衰期和 Gamow--Teller 共振能量的模拟器。计算得到的 $Q_\beta$ 值经过微调以匹配实验值。在贝叶斯框架中，理论误差、$Q_\beta$ 值和参数不确定度对半衰期不确定度的贡献均已计入。我们对偶偶 $N=126$ 等中子素的预测给出了现有理论模型中富中子原子核最短的半衰期，这将影响重元素核合成的流动。所提出的框架允许将量化的理论不确定度传播至丰度模式和电磁辐射等天体物理观测量。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Emulator-enabled calibration and uncertainty quantification of $\beta$-decay calculations within the energy density functional theory

Predictions of $\beta$-decay half-lives of neutron-rich nuclei and their uncertainties critically impact our understanding of the astrophysical rapid neutron capture process. To provide quantified predictions within nuclear energy density functional theory, as customarily done, we perform Bayesian calibrations of two coupling constants: the isoscalar proton-neutron pairing strength and the isovector spin-density coupling. We apply an E-optimal experimental design for choosing the calibration sets. We employ the proton-neutron finite-amplitude method with Skyrme energy density functionals using emulators of half-lives and Gamow--Teller resonance energies. The calculated $Q_\beta$ values are fine-tuned to experimental values. The contributions to the half-life uncertainty from the theory error, $Q_\beta$ values, and the parameter uncertainty are all accounted for in the Bayesian framework. Our predictions for the even-even $N=126$ isotones provide the shortest half-lives for neutron-rich nuclei among the existing theoretical models, which would affect the flow of heavy-element nucleosynthesis. The proposed framework allows quantified theoretical uncertainties to be propagated to astrophysical observables such as abundance patterns and electromagnetic emission.

07 · Physical Review C · 源文更新（相对 11 月 22 日）

富中子 O 离子与 12 C 聚变中的中子动力学

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

首次测量了 $^{21}$O + $^{12}$C 的聚变激发函数，并将其与中子丰度较低的同位素的聚变进行了比较。研究了 d$_{5/2}$ 壳层中的价中子对聚变激发函数的影响。实验数据表明，提取的势垒高度 V$_B$ 和势垒位置 R$_B$ 对中子过剩具有明确的依赖性。为了评估动力学的作用，将实验数据与密度约束冻结 Hartree Fock (DCFHF) 理论和密度约束含时 Hartree Fock (DCTDHF) 理论进行了比较。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Neutron dynamics in the fusion of neutron-rich O ions with 12 C

The fusion excitation function for $^{21}$O + $^{12}$C was measured for the first time and compared to the fusion of less neutron-rich isotopes. The impact of valence neutrons in the d$_{5/2}$ shell on the fusion excitation function is examined. The experimental data manifest a clear dependence of the extracted barrier height, V$_B$, and barrier position, R$_B$ on neutron excess. To assess the role of dynamics the experimental data are compared with both density constrained frozen Hartree Fock (DCFHF) and density constrained time-dependent Hartree Fock (DCTDHF) theories.

09 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 25 日新收录

通过已鉴别强子流退相关揭示核形状

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

相对论核碰撞将核形状编码在集体流中，然而利用已鉴别强子区分形变模式的潜力在很大程度上仍未得到探索。我们采用逐事例黏性流体动力学并与强子输运耦合，研究了 193 GeV 下中心 $^{238}$U+$^{238}$U 碰撞以及 5.36 TeV 下 $^{20}$Ne+$^{20}$Ne 碰撞中 π 介子、K 介子和质子的动量依赖椭圆流退相关。不同的已鉴别粒子种类表现出各异的动量依赖和形变依赖响应；即使单独的形变效应较弱，核形状信息仍保留在它们的相对关联中。我们引入交叉坐标 $(P_{\rm cross},R_{\rm cross})$，其由 π 介子-K 介子与 π 介子-质子流因子化比值的交点定义。四极形变和三轴性的变化在交叉平面中产生不同的轨迹，为两个核形状参数提供互补约束。相应行为在高阶矩关联中依然存在，而 Ne+Ne 中的能量比较揭示了碰撞动力学的影响。我们的发现确立了已鉴别粒子流退相关作为探测和约束核固有几何结构的一种互补方法。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Revealing Nuclear Shape through Identified-Hadron Flow Decorrelations

Relativistic nuclear collisions encode nuclear shape in collective flow, yet the potential of identified hadrons to disentangle deformation modes remains largely unexplored. Using event-by-event viscous hydrodynamics coupled to hadronic transport, we investigate momentum-dependent elliptic-flow decorrelations of pions, kaons, and protons in central $^{238}$U+$^{238}$U collisions at 193 GeV and $^{20}$Ne+$^{20}$Ne collisions at 5.36 TeV. The identified species exhibit distinct momentum- and deformation-dependent responses, with nuclear-shape information persisting in their relative correlations even when individual deformation effects are weak. We introduce crossing coordinates $(P_{\rm cross},R_{\rm cross})$, defined by the intersection of pion-kaon and pion-proton flow-factorization ratios. Variations of quadrupole deformation and triaxiality generate distinct trajectories in the crossing plane, offering complementary constraints on both nuclear-shape parameters. The corresponding behavior persists in higher-moment correlations, while an energy comparison in Ne+Ne reveals the influence of collision dynamics. Our findings establish identified-particle flow decorrelations as a complementary approach to probing and constraining intrinsic nuclear geometry.

10 · arXiv nucl-ex · 11 月 25 日新收录

$\sqrt{s} = 200$ GeV 下 $p^{\uparrow}+ p$ 碰撞中前向 $\eta$ 介子的横向单自旋不对称性

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

利用布鲁克海文国家实验室相对论重离子对撞机 2012 年的横向极化质子数据，测量了 $\sqrt{s}=200$ GeV 下 $p^{\uparrow}+p$ 碰撞中前向 $\eta$ 介子的横向单自旋不对称性（$A_N$）随 Feynman-x（$x_F$）的变化，结果为 $0.2 =0.086 \pm 0.019$），与先前对 $\pi^{0}$ 和 $\eta$ 的 $A_N$ 测量结果高度一致，但覆盖了更高的 $x_F$ 和 $p_T$。在共线 twist-3 框架中计算得到的初态自旋-动量关联对该不对称性的贡献似乎不足以描述数据，并表明碎裂对该不对称性具有显著影响。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Transverse single-spin asymmetry of forward $\eta$ mesons in $p^{\uparrow}+ p$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 200$ GeV

Utilizing the 2012 transversely polarized proton data from the Relativistic Heavy Ion Collider at Brookhaven National Laboratory, the forward $\eta$-meson transverse single-spin asymmetry ($A_N$) was measured for $p^{\uparrow}+p$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=200$ GeV as a function of Feynman-x ($x_F$) for $0.2 =0.086 \pm 0.019$), agreeing well with previous measurements of $\pi^{0}$ and $\eta$ $A_N$, but with reach to higher $x_F$ and $p_T$. The contribution of initial-state spin-momentum correlations to the asymmetry, as calculated in the collinear twist-3 framework, appears insufficient to describe the data and suggests a significant impact on the asymmetry from fragmentation.

11 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 25 日新收录

迈向 Wigner 超多重态对称性百年：从头算视角

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

随着 Wigner 超多重态理论于 2037 年迎来百年纪念，本文通过近期取得进展的从头算对称性适配模型（SAM）提供了一种现代视角。我们从该对称性在双核子数据和电弱过程中的经验基础出发，并追溯其如何通过有效场论、QCD 大-$N_c$ 极限、相似性重整化群和晶格计算从强相互作用中涌现。随后，我们沿其历史轨迹展开论述，从早期有限的关注，到重新引发兴趣，再到现代代数方法和第一性原理方法的复兴。这一复兴的核心是用于 U(3) 和 U(4) 耦合与再耦合系数的基于零空间的新算法，它们消除了一个延续近九十年的瓶颈，并作为开源库 u3lib 和 u4lib 发布。在此基础上，SAM 是首个同时编码 Elliott 空间 U(3) 对称性和 Wigner 自旋-同位旋 U(4) 对称性的大规模从头算壳模型，能够分辨具有相同二次 Casimir 本征值的各个超多重态。早期结果表明，自旋-同位旋对称性在高保真手征相互作用中占据显著主导地位，并抑制轻原子核中的自旋-同位旋极化率。最后，我们讨论 SAM 有望在未来十年推进的潜在方向。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Toward the Centennial of Wigner's Supermultiplet Symmetry: an Ab Initio Perspective

As the centennial of Wigner's supermultiplet theory approaches in 2037, this review offers a modern perspective through the recently advanced ab initio Symmetry Adapted Model (SAM). We begin with the symmetry's empirical foundations in two-nucleon data and electroweak processes, and trace its emergence from the strong force through effective field theories, the QCD large-$N_c$ limit, the similarity renormalization group, and lattice calculations. We then follow its historical arc, from early limited engagement, through its revived interest, to the modern algebraic and first-principles resurgence. Central to this renaissance are new nullspace-based algorithms for the U(3) and U(4) coupling and recoupling coefficients, which remove a bottleneck spanning nearly nine decades and are released as the open-source libraries u3lib and u4lib. Building on them, the SAM is the first large-scale ab initio shell model to encode both Elliott's spatial U(3) and Wigner's spin-isospin U(4) symmetries, resolving individual supermultiplets that share the same quadratic Casimir eigenvalue. Early results show that the spin-isospin symmetry strongly dominates high-fidelity chiral interactions and suppresses spin-isospin polarizability in light nuclei. We close with potential directions that the SAM is poised to advance within the next decade.

12 · arXiv nucl-th · 11 月 25 日新收录

基于 Weizsacker-Skyrme 4 模型的 $\alpha$ 衰变能残差校正的表格先验数据拟合网络（TabPFN）优化

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

构建了一种物理引导的混合框架，将 Weizsacker-Skyrme-4（WS4）宏观-微观质量模型与表格先验数据拟合网络（TabPFN）相结合，以提高超重核 α 衰变能预测的准确度。TabPFN 利用核结构特征学习实验数据与 WS4 预测之间的残差。通过对 18 种特征组合进行消融研究，确定了最优特征集，将总体均方根误差（RMSE）从 0.2743 降至 0.1442 MeV，改善了 47.43%。校正后的模型提高了所有四种奇偶原子核组态的预测准确度，同时保留了沿 Po、Rn 和 U 同位素链的壳效应和奇偶交错模式。该框架还进一步用于预测沿 Z = 118-120 同位素链的实验未测原子核的衰变能值。外推结果保留了 WS4 的总体趋势，并呈现出与预测的 N = 184 中子壳闭合相关的特征。这些发现凸显了物理引导残差学习在预测超重核衰变性质方面的潜力。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) optimization of residual correction of $\alpha$-decay energy based on Weizsacker-Skyrme 4 model

A physics-guided hybrid framework combining the Weizsacker-Skyrme-4 (WS4) macroscopic-microscopic mass model with the Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) is developed to improve the accuracy of alpha-decay energy predictions for superheavy nuclei. TabPFN learns the residuals between experimental data and WS4 predictions using nuclear-structure features. Through ablation studies of 18 feature combinations, the optimal feature set is identified, reducing the overall root-mean-square error (RMSE) from 0.2743 to 0.1442 MeV, an improvement of 47.43%. The corrected model improves prediction accuracy across all four odd-even nuclear configurations while preserving shell effects and odd-even staggering patterns along the Po, Rn, and U isotopic chains. The framework is further applied to predict decay energy values for experimentally unmeasured nuclei along the Z = 118-120 isotopic chains. The extrapolated results retain the overall trends of WS4 and exhibit signatures associated with the predicted neutron shell closure at N = 184. These findings highlight the potential of physics-guided residual learning for predicting the decay properties of superheavy nuclei.

New $\gamma$-softness in $^{196}$Pt from $\gamma$-rigid triaxiality

The interplay between the $\gamma$-softness and the $\gamma$-rigidity is a key aspect in understanding nuclear collectivity. In this paper, the $\gamma$-softness emerging from the $\gamma$-rigid triaxiality within the framework of the SU3-IBM is applied to investigate the $^{196}$Pt nucleus. 非相对论 Skyrme RPA 框架内弱磁性效应对热核物质中中微子-核子相互作用的影响

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

中微子的产生、吸收和散射对于解释中微子驱动的核心坍缩超新星十分重要。在高密度或高温下，相对论效应，特别是弱磁性（WM），可能变得愈发重要。因此，我们通过将 WM 项纳入核子弱流，扩展了用于研究热核物质中中微子-核子相互作用的非相对论随机相位近似（RPA）框架。在采用我们近期的 Skyrme 泛函 Sky3s 进行的计算中，我们确认 WM 提高了超新星中最重要的带电流中微子过程的相互作用率，并降低了反中微子的相互作用率。然而，通过 $\beta$ 衰变产生反中微子的情况并非如此，其产生率也有所提高。在我们的计算中，在高密度下，WM 可使中子星物质通过直接 Urca 过程产生的中微子发射率增至接近原来的两倍。我们还发现，WM 对散射率的修正随散射角和能量转移的增大而愈发重要，从而导致中微子与反中微子的双微分散射率之间存在显著差异。我们的公式可帮助改进超新星模拟所需的中微子反应率。

查看原文标题与摘要

Weak magnetism effects on neutrino-nucleon interactions in hot nuclear matter within nonrelativistic Skyrme RPA

Neutrino production, absorption, and scattering are important in explaining neutrino-driven core-collapse supernovae. At high densities or temperatures, relativistic effects, in particular weak magnetism (WM), may become increasingly important. Therefore, we extend the nonrelativistic random-phase approximation (RPA) framework for the study of neutrino-nucleon interactions in hot nuclear matter by incorporating the WM term into the nucleon weak current. In calculations with our recent Skyrme functional Sky3s, we confirm that WM increases interaction rates for the most important charged-current neutrino processes in supernovae and reduces those of antineutrinos. However, this is not the case for the antineutrino production via $\beta$ decay, which is also increased. In our calculations, WM can almost double the neutrino emissivities of neutron-star matter by direct Urca processes at high density. We also find that WM corrections to the scattering rates are increasingly important with increasing scattering angle and energy transfer, leading to a significant difference between double-differential neutrino and antineutrino scattering rates. Our formulas can help improve neutrino rates needed in supernova simulations.

中子寿命谜题与自发衰变中的 Dicke 相干性

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

自由中子寿命的束流测量与瓶式测量之间长期存在的差异——即中子寿命谜题——仍是核物理和粒子物理中的一个未解反常现象。在此，我们研究了通过宏观 Dicke 超辐射实现的集体量子同步是否会影响自由中子衰变，或介导假想激发中子态的弛豫。我们表明，在标准三体弱 β 衰变中，连续的末态相空间和费米子反对称化从根本上阻碍了超辐射；即使在 Koch--Hummel 激发中子模型中，由于粒子密度低，超辐射在地面超冷中子阱中仍不起作用。将此分析扩展至大爆炸核合成之前的原初等离子体，我们证明，快速的 $e^\pm$ 碰撞退相干和强烈的热光子背景会阻止超辐射清除，转而强制维持热细致平衡。因此，在不受地面实验约束的整个参数空间内，激发中子态都不可避免地在弱相互作用冻结之前达到热化，从而增加中子的有效统计权重，并严重过量产生原初 $^4\text{He}$，由此排除了 Koch--Hummel 假说的标准实现形式。

查看原文标题与摘要

Neutron lifetime puzzle and Dicke coherence in spontaneous decays

The long-standing discrepancy between beam and bottle measurements of the free-neutron lifetime---known as the neutron lifetime puzzle---remains an open anomaly in nuclear and particle physics. Here, we investigate whether collective quantum synchronization via macroscopic Dicke superradiance can influence free-neutron decay or mediate the relaxation of hypothetical excited neutron states. We show that superradiance is fundamentally obstructed in standard three-body weak beta decay by continuous final-state phase space and fermionic antisymmetrization, and remains inactive in terrestrial ultracold neutron traps due to low particle densities, even within the Koch--Hummel excited-neutron model. Extending this analysis to the primordial plasma prior to Big Bang nucleosynthesis, we demonstrate that rapid $e^\pm$ collisional dephasing and intense thermal photon backgrounds prevent a superradiant purge and instead enforce thermal detailed balance. Consequently, across the entire parameter space unconstrained by terrestrial experiments, an excited neutron state inevitably thermalizes before weak freeze-out, increasing the effective neutron statistical weight and severely overproducing primordial $^4\text{He}$, thereby excluding the standard realization of the Koch--Hummel hypothesis.

ICRH 驱动的 beta 诱发 Alfvén 本征模对 EAST 等离子体中快离子再分布和离子加热的影响

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

摘要 在磁约束等离子体中，高能粒子 (EP) 驱动的不稳定性可引起高能离子的再分布和损失，从而影响等离子体加热和约束。在本工作中，使用实验重建的平衡态和剖面，研究了低密度 EAST 等离子体中由离子回旋共振加热 (ICRH) 激发的快离子驱动 beta 诱发 Alfvén 本征模 (BAE)。实验上，强 ICRH 导致离子温度和等离子体储能显著增加，同时在 1.6 MW 加热功率下激发了频率为 ∼ 64 kHz 的 Alfvén 本征模，而在降低功率时未观察到这种模式。连续谱分析将该模式鉴定为芯部局域化的 BAE。为解释这些观测结果，开展了结合 TORIC、ASCOT 和 GTC 的集成模拟。采用了修正的快离子分布函数，以刻画 ICRH 产生的快离子在速度空间中的强各向异性。线性回旋动理学模拟再现了观测到的 BAE 频率，并确定了模式激发所需的快离子压强阈值。采用真实壁面几何结构的轨道跟踪模拟表明，BAE 会引起显著的相空间再分布，并增强高能快离子（能量 > 600 keV）的损失。另外，ASCOT 功率沉积分析表明，强 BAE 减少了从快离子碰撞传递至热离子的功率，从而抑制快离子对离子加热的贡献，并限制离子温度的升高。随着测得的 BAE 活动在阶段 B 中减弱，该离子加热通道的恢复解释了随后离子温度的升高。这些结果阐明了 ICRH 产生的快离子、BAE 活动与离子加热之间的相互作用，并为优化未来燃烧等离子体装置中的快离子约束和加热效率提供了见解。

查看原文标题与摘要

Impact of ICRH-driven beta-induced Alfvén eigenmode on fast-ion redistribution and ion heating in EAST plasmas

Abstract In magnetically confined plasmas, energetic-particle (EP)-driven instabilities can induce redistribution and losses of energetic ions, thereby affecting plasma heating and confinement. In this work, fast-ion-driven beta-induced Alfvén eigenmode (BAE) excited by ion cyclotron resonance heating (ICRH) was investigated in low-density EAST plasmas using experimentally reconstructed equilibria and profiles. Experimentally, strong ICRH led to a significant increase in ion temperature and plasma stored energy, accompanied by the excitation of an Alfvén eigenmode at ∼ 64 kHz under 1.6 MW heating power, while no such mode was observed at reduced power. Continuum analysis identified this mode as a core-localized BAE. To interpret these observations, integrated simulations combining TORIC, ASCOT, and GTC were performed. A modified fast-ion distribution function was implemented to capture the strong velocity-space anisotropy of ICRH-generated fast ions. Linear gyrokinetic simulations reproduced the observed BAE frequency and identified a threshold in fast-ion pressure for mode excitation. Orbit-following simulations with realistic wall geometry demonstrated that the BAE induces significant phase-space redistribution and enhances the loss of high-energy fast ions (energy > 600 keV ). Separately, the ASCOT power-deposition analysis shows that the strong BAE reduces the collisional power transferred from fast ions to thermal ions, thereby suppressing the fast-ion contribution to ion heating and limiting the increase in ion temperature. As the measured BAE activity decreases in Phase B, recovery of this ion-heating channel explains the subsequent rise in ion temperature. These results clarify the interplay between ICRH-generated fast ions, BAE activity, and ion heating, and provide insights into optimizing fast-ion confinement and heating efficiency in future burning plasma devices.

Exploring the phase diagram of dense and hot matter with HBT interferometry

We investigate the HBT radii of pions produced in Au+Au collisions at selected energies from the STAR-BES program, using an event-by-event hybrid model that combines AMPT initial conditions, 3+1D viscous hydrodynamic evolution with a QCD equation of state at finite baryon chemical potential, and hadronic transport with SMASH. The goal is to explore the source's space-time structure and its possible connection to the structure of the QCD phase diagram. The hybrid chain was first validated by comparing identified-particle spectra and anisotropic flow, yielding a globally consistent description of the experimental data, with more evident discrepancies at the lowest energies.

查看原文标题与摘要

Chiral QCD phase in equilibrium with Hadron Gas and the location of the critical point

We develop a description of the equation of state of QCD matter with restored chiral symmetry, which is in thermal and chemical equilibrium with the hadronic phase. The hadron gas is described with thermodynamically consistent volume corrections. The chiral phase is composed of a set of few quark condensates, each of which corresponds to a family of hadrons with specific quark content. On the boundary between the two phases we apply the requirement of conservation of particle numbers per family. We use lattice calculations for temperatures below the transition curve to determine hadronic volumes. We find that the pion system plays decisive role in the shift of the transition from higher order (crossover) to first order. For four volume models and five hadron sets we calculate the location of the critical point as function of critical temperature $T_c$ at vanishing baryon density. Among other acceptable solutions, if we additionally impose the equality between the densities of quarks contained in mesons and baryons, we find a critical point residing in the interval of baryon chemical potential $\mu_B \simeq$ 234-279 MeV and of temperature $T \simeq$ 148-156 MeV. We propose as signal of approach to the critical point the determination of the chiral pion mass through experimentally measured pion multiplicities in heavy-ion collisions.

Analysis of CONUS+ run 2: Detector performance, background model and CE$\nu$NS measurement

Coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$\nu$NS) provides a uniquely sensitive probe of neutrino properties, but its detection requires pushing energy thresholds to the sub-keV scale while controlling backgrounds. The CONUS+ experiment, located at the Leibstadt nuclear power plant, reported the first observation of CE$\nu$NS using reactor antineutrinos in 2025. Following a detector upgrade that replaced three of the original 1 kg high-purity germanium detectors with larger 2.42 kg diodes, this paper presents the analysis of the CONUS+ run 2 dataset, including the detector characterization, the background model, the signal prediction and expected sensitivity, and the extraction of the CE$\nu$NS signal. The new detectors reach a background level per unit mass reduced by approximately a factor of two relative to the original detectors, while reaching energy thresholds between 140 and 170 eV$_{ee}$. With an exposure of 1296 kg$\cdot$days reactor-on and 86 kg$\cdot$days reactor-off, a blind likelihood analysis fitting directly the number of signal counts yields $N_s = 1565 \pm 155$ CE$\nu$NS events and a relative precision of 9.9\%. The result is in agreement with the Standard Model expectation of $1701 \pm 198$ events, with $N_s/N_{\mathrm{SM}} = 0.92 \pm 0.14$.

Simulation and Rejection of Radon Progeny Backgrounds in nEXO

Radon-induced backgrounds from $^{214}$Bi decays remain a significant challenge for liquid xenon (LXe) detectors searching for neutrinoless double beta decay ($0\nu\beta\beta$). We present a simulation-based study of the $^{214}$Bi background in nEXO, incorporating a physically motivated model of the transport and implantation of radon progeny into the detector cathode. We show that the spatial distribution of scintillation light provides an additional method for background discrimination for events surviving standard energy, event topology, and fiducial volume selections. A simple discriminator based on the scintillation light detected below the cathode rejects 41% of the remaining $^{214}$Bi backgrounds while retaining 95% of $0\nu\beta\beta$ signal events, and a convolutional neural network classifier improves this rejection to 57% at the same signal efficiency. An updated optical model provides a more accurate estimate of the light collection efficiency below the cathode than used in previous work, increasing the expected $^{214}$Bi-$^{214}$Po (Bi-Po) coincidence-tagging efficiency for bottom-surface $^{214}$Bi decays from less than 1% to approximately 50%. Together, these results demonstrate improved rejection of cathode-implanted $^{214}$Bi backgrounds in nEXO through complementary prompt scintillation-based discrimination and delayed coincidence tagging.

利用 TRON 挖掘时间轴。IV. MeerKAT 探测到来自附近一颗 M 矮星的射电暴

摘要 我们报告探测到一个来自活动 M 矮星（光谱型 M1Ve）PM J14111−6155 的分钟时间尺度、高度圆偏振（∼100%）射电暴，该星距太阳 19.6 pc。该射电暴是在 1.28 GHz SMGPS 中利用 TRON-pipeline 的 8-second 积分识别出的，峰值达到 7.7 mJy。基于可见度的动态谱揭示了一个漂移的、带限的发射特征（∼300 MHz FWHM），其最大截止频率接近 1.65 GHz；该特征与电子回旋脉泽不稳定性（ECMI）相关，意味着存在大尺度磁拓扑结构（B* ≈ 0.59 kG）。四小时后的未探测结果（3σ 上限）指向间歇性或受转动调制的束射发射。对 TESS 和地基巡天数据的分析均未能为其光学对应体确定明确无误的转动周期。后续 SALT 光谱观测证实其色球层处于活动状态，表现出显著的 Ca ii 红外三重线、Balmer 线，以及由光学厚自吸收引起的持续双峰 Hα 发射（44.6 km s−1 峰间距）。这一发现表明，协同使用时间序列光学测光、高分辨率光谱和高时间采样率光谱偏振射电成像，能够直接探测附近 M 矮星磁活动大气中的相干射电发射。

Mining the time axis with TRON. IV. MeerKAT detects a radio burst from a nearby M dwarf

Abstract We report the detection of a minute-timescale, highly circularly polarised (∼100%) radio burst from an active M dwarf (spectral type M1Ve), PM J14111−6155, located 19.6 pc from the Sun. Identified in the 1.28 GHz SMGPS using 8-second integrations from the TRON-pipeline, the burst peaked at 7.7 mJy. Visibility-based dynamic spectra reveal a drifting, band-limited emission feature (∼300 MHz FWHM) with a maximum cut-off near 1.65 GHz, associated with electron cyclotron maser instability (ECMI), implying the existence of large-scale magnetic topologies (B* ≈ 0.59 kG). A non-detection (3σ upper limit) four hours later points to intermittent or rotationally modulated beamed emission. An analysis of both TESS and ground-based survey data failed to recover an unambiguous rotational period for the optical counterpart. Follow-up SALT spectroscopy confirms its active chromospheric state, exhibiting prominent emission in the Ca ii infrared triplet, Balmer lines, and persistent double-peaked Hα emission (44.6 km s−1 peak separation) attributable to optically thick self-absorption. This discovery demonstrates that the coordinated use of time-series optical photometry, high-resolution spectroscopy, and high-cadence spectropolarimetric radio imaging enables direct probing of coherent radio emission from the magnetically active atmospheres of nearby M dwarfs.

通过突然自旋加速事件形成双小行星

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们研究突然自旋加速事件是否为双小行星形成提供了一条动力学上可行的途径，并评估由此形成的系统的轨道、结构和形态性质。我们进一步考察这条途径对近期由盘和环带介导的卫星形成模型以及经典的雅科夫斯基--奥基夫--拉杰夫斯基--帕达克（YORP）驱动形成情景的影响。采用软球离散元法进行了高分辨率 N 体模拟。通过结合实验室破碎实验、摄影测量重建和自引力再聚积的流程，生成了真实的引力聚集体，从而产生由不规则形状碎片构成的聚集体。突然自旋加速被建模为施加于具有不同初始形状、内部结构和表面结构的引力聚集体的角动量脉冲式增加。PKDGRAV 突然自旋加速可产生快速的赤道质量脱落，并可在数十小时内将足够的物质注入轨道，从而形成有利于引力再聚积的碎屑环境。成功形成双星的模拟经历了瞬态多体演化，并产生了质量比 M_s/M_ ̊m tot 和轨道间距 a_s/R_p 与观测到的小型双星系统一致的次级天体。模拟还产生了多种裂变后结果，包括接触双星卫星、瞬态三星系统、卫星再撞击和逃逸碎片。次级天体的形态和内部来源可能对碎片尺寸分布和表面结构敏感。在本文研究的双星形成情形中，卫星的成功形成主要与是否存在质量足够大（sim10% 的系统质量）的轨道碎屑储库有关，而非与具体的自旋加速机制本身有关。结果还表明，纯粹渐进的 YORP 演化效率可能低于通常的假设，因为真实的 YORP 自旋加速会产生局部、有限且间歇性的质量脱落。相比之下，突然自旋加速会自然地产生碎屑环境，并提供一条合理的上游途径，将撞击诱发或潮汐诱发的旋转激发与近期研究中确定的由盘和环带介导的卫星形成过程联系起来。

查看原文标题与摘要

Formation of binary asteroids through sudden spin-up events

We investigate whether sudden spin-up events provide a dynamically viable pathway for binary asteroid formation and assess the orbital, structural, and morphological properties of the resulting systems. We further examine the implications of this pathway for recent disc- and annulus-mediated satellite formation models and the classical Yarkovsky--O’Keefe--Radzievskii--Paddack (YORP)-driven formation scenario. High-resolution N-body simulations were performed using the soft-sphere discrete element method. Realistic gravitational aggregates were generated through a pipeline combining laboratory fragmentation experiments, photogrammetry reconstruction, and self-gravitational re-accumulation, to produce aggregates made of irregularly shaped fragments. Sudden spin-up was modelled as an impulsive increase in angular momentum applied to gravitational aggregates with different initial shapes, internal and surface structures. PKDGRAV Sudden spin-up can produce rapid equatorial mass shedding and can inject sufficient material into orbit within tens of hours, creating debris environments favourable for gravitational re-accumulation. The successful binary-forming simulations undergo transient multi-body evolution and produce secondaries with mass ratios of M_s/M_ ̊m tot and orbital separations of a_s/R_p consistent with observed small binary systems. Simulations further produce a diversity of post-fission outcomes, including contact-binary satellites, transient triple systems, satellite re-impacts, and escaping fragments. The secondary morphology and internal provenance can be sensitive to the fragment size distribution and surface structure. In the binary-forming cases investigated here, successful satellite formation is primarily associated with the availability of a sufficiently massive (sim10% of the system mass) orbital debris reservoir rather than with the specific spin-up mechanism itself. The results further suggest that purely gradual YORP evolution may be less efficient than often assumed, as realistic YORP spin-up produces localised, limited, and intermittent mass shedding. In contrast, sudden spin-up naturally generates debris environments and provides a plausible upstream pathway connecting impact-induced or tidally induced rotational excitation to the disc- and annulus-mediated satellite formation processes identified in recent studies.

格点上的 QCD+QED 热力学

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

目前，QCD 中可观测量的 QED 修正在零温下已得到充分理解，在此条件下，QED 效应是微扰性的，并且是获得精密物理可观测量所必需的，例如中子-质子质量差或μ子反常磁矩等。然而，文献中有迹象表明，在有限温度下引入 QED 可能导致非微扰效应，从而影响相图的结构，因为组合理论具有严格的中心对称性。在这篇会议论文中，我们分析了组合理论的结构以及在格点上模拟该理论的不同策略，并给出了有限温度下格点 QCD+QED 模拟的新结果，重点在于理解电磁耦合在该系统热力学中的作用。我们还讨论了组合理论中出现的新特征以及这些模拟所带来的挑战；为了从第一性原理理解 QCD+QED 的相图，这些挑战要求开发新算法。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Thermodynamics of QCD+QED from the lattice

QED corrections to observables in QCD are by now well-understood at zero temperature, where QED effects are perturbative and required for precision physics observables, such as the neutron-proton mass difference or the muon anomalous magnetic moment, among many others. However, there are indications in the literature that at finite temperature the introduction of QED can lead to non-perturbative effects, affecting the structure of the phase diagram, since the combined theory is exactly center-symmetric. In this proceedings article, we analyze the structure of the combined theory and the different strategies to simulate it on the lattice, as well as presenting novel results of lattice QCD+QED simulations at finite temperature, focusing on understanding the role of the electromagnetic coupling in the thermodynamics of this system. We also discuss new features that arise in the combined theory and the challenges that these simulations entail, which require the development of new algorithms in order to understand the phase diagram of QCD+QED from first principles.

用于 Au+Au 碰撞模拟的坐标空间和动量空间核子短程关联采样

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们开发了一种联合纳入空间核子关联、高动量分量、以质子-中子配对指定为主以及关联对动量的原子核初始化方案，并将其实现于 SMASH 输运模型中。这一构造使得能够对短程关联（SRCs）的不同组成因素对重离子可观测量的影响进行受控比较。对于 $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=2.4$ GeV 的 Au+Au 碰撞，在使用相同平均场参数时，相对于具有费米动量的 Woods-Saxon 参考，SRC 初始化提高了两种带电π介子的产额。$\pi^+$ 产生的相对增强更大，从而降低了 $\pi^-/\pi^+$ 比率。受控比较表明，修正后的单核子动量分布是这种增强的主要来源，所测试的空间变化和核子对同位旋变化的影响较小，并且在不施加核子对动量结构时，产生增强仍然存在。反应历程将这种增强与重子共振态产生的增加联系起来。更强的高动量排斥通过减少核子-核子碰撞中的可用能量并抑制共振态产生，抵消了 SRC 引起的π介子增强。SRC 初始化对 $K^+$ 产生造成的类似增强，以及重新拟合平均场参数后该增强的减弱，表明关联初始化与平均场动力学的竞争效应并不限于π介子产生。

查看原文标题与摘要

Sampling nucleon short-range correlations in coordinate and momentum space for Au+Au collision simulations

We develop a nuclear initialization scheme that jointly incorporates spatial nucleon correlations, high-momentum components, predominantly proton-neutron pair assignments, and correlated pair momenta, and implement it in the SMASH transport model. This construction enables controlled comparisons of the effects of different ingredients of short-range correlations (SRCs) on heavy-ion observables. For Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=2.4$ GeV, SRC initialization increases both charged-pion yields relative to a Woods-Saxon reference with Fermi momenta when the same mean-field parameters are used. The larger relative enhancement of $\pi^+$ production reduces the $\pi^-/\pi^+$ ratio. Controlled comparisons identify the modified single-nucleon momentum distribution as the dominant source of the enhancement, with smaller effects from the tested spatial and pair-isospin variations and enhanced production persisting without the imposed pair-momentum structure. Reaction histories link this enhancement to increased baryon-resonance production. Stronger high-momentum repulsion counteracts the SRC-induced pion enhancement by reducing the energy available in nucleon-nucleon collisions and suppressing resonance production. The similar enhancement of $K^+$ production with SRC initialization and its reduction after refitting the mean-field parameters show that the competing effects of correlated initialization and mean-field dynamics extend beyond pion production.

利用 CMS 开放数据通过微分流系数 $v_0(p_T)$ 探测径向流

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们利用 CMS 开放数据，对 $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 2.76~\mathrm{TeV}$ 的 Pb+Pb 碰撞中微分径向流系数 $v_0(p_T)$ 及其经 $p_T$ 积分的对应量 $v_0$ 进行了全面研究，所用数据来自 50--60\% 和 60--70\% 的中外周中心度区间。将测得的 $v_0(p_T)$ 分布与 ATLAS 在 $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$ 下的测量结果进行比较，揭示了径向流涨落总体幅度明确的能量依赖性。值得注意的是，标度比率 $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ 在低 $p_T$ 下表现出近似普适性，基本不依赖于碰撞能量和中心度，并持续至 $p_T\sim3~\mathrm{GeV}$，这表明积分 $v_0$ 编码了大部分与能量有关的信息。我们将数据与 \textsc{Jetscape} 多阶段混合模型进行比较，并使用基于模型输出主成分分析训练的高斯过程仿真器来探索完整的 17维参数空间。使用 Sobol 指数进行的全局方差敏感性分析表明，对于 Grad 黏性修正，核子涨落参数 $\sigma_k$ 是 $v_0$、$v_0(p_T)$ 和 $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ 方差的主导来源，其次是自由流参数 $\tau_R$ 和 $\alpha$ 以及核子宽度 $w$。$v_0$ 对体黏度峰值 $(\zeta/s)_{\max}$ 的敏感性较弱，但对于 $v_0(p_T)$ 和标度比率，这种敏感性有所增强。在经 $p_T$ 积分的可观测量和标度谱上校准的仿真器预测，在低和中等 $p_T$ 下能够相当好地描述数据。在 $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 2.76~\mathrm{TeV}$ 下校准的参数也能描述 ATLAS 在 $5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$ 下的 $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ 数据，支持径向流涨落的动量依赖性在不同碰撞能量下具有近似普适性。

查看原文标题与摘要

Probing radial flow via the differential flow coefficient $v_0(p_T)$ using CMS open data

We present a comprehensive study of the differential radial flow coefficient $v_0(p_T)$ and its $p_T$-integrated counterpart $v_0$ in Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 2.76~\mathrm{TeV}$, using CMS open data for mid-peripheral centrality classes of 50--60\% and 60--70\%. The measured $v_0(p_T)$ distributions are compared with ATLAS measurements at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$, revealing a clear energy dependence in the overall magnitude of radial flow fluctuations. Notably, the scaled ratio $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ exhibits an approximate universality that is largely independent of both collision energy and centrality at low $p_T$, persisting up to $p_T\sim3~\mathrm{GeV}$, suggesting that the integrated $v_0$ encodes most of the energy-dependent information. We compare the data with the \textsc{Jetscape} multi-stage hybrid model, using Gaussian process emulators trained on a principal component analysis of the model output to explore the full 17-dimensional parameter space. A global variance-based sensitivity analysis using Sobol indices shows that, for the Grad viscous correction, the nucleon fluctuation parameter $\sigma_k$ is the dominant source of variance for $v_0$, $v_0(p_T)$, and $v_0(p_T)/v_0$, followed by the free-streaming parameters $\tau_R$ and $\alpha$ and the nucleon width $w$. The sensitivity to the bulk viscosity peak $(\zeta/s)_{\max}$ is weak for $v_0$ but increases for $v_0(p_T)$ and for the scaled ratio. Emulator predictions, calibrated on $p_T$-integrated observables and on scaled spectra, describe the data reasonably well at low and intermediate $p_T$. Parameters calibrated at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 2.76~\mathrm{TeV}$ also describe the ATLAS $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ data at $5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$, supporting a near universal momentum dependence of radial flow fluctuations across collision energies.

量子处理器上的中子-alpha 散射：自旋-轨道劈裂共振与弱非共振道

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们利用在 IBM 的 Boston 量子计算机上测得的囚禁谱，重建了直至 $l=2$ 的全部五个道中的弹性中子--alpha 相移。通过采用因式分解几何时间 RODEO 算法的四量子比特实现，我们分辨出 $^5$He 的自旋--轨道劈裂 $p_{3/2}$ 和 $p_{1/2}$ 共振以及较小的非共振 $d$ 波相移。经典构建的八维有效哈密顿量被编码到三个系统量子比特中，并使用一个重复利用的辅助量子比特进行探测。我们在五种约束强度下对每个道的 40 个囚禁能量（总计 200 个能量）进行拟合，其中对 $d$ 波使用倒数有效力程函数。拟合给出了自由空间相移、弹性截面以及两个 $p$ 波极点。宽的 $p_{1/2}$ 极点对拟合阶数更为敏感。在 $0.05\leq E_{\rm cm}\leq6$ MeV 网格上，针对同一校准相互作用，重建的相移与独立径向计算结果之间的差异小于 $0.073^\circ$。$l\leq2$ 截面的最大相对差异为 $0.185\%$。在对三个时间分隔区块中的十个低能级进行的独立测量中，反转演化时间和查询相位会改变拟合能量，但理想的辅助量子比特历史概率保持不变。在两次回顾性等采样数比较中，对正向和反向估计取平均，将仅采用正向演化时的合并均方根能量误差从 $49.59$ eV 分别降低至 $20.91$ 和 $16.37$ eV。该效应因道而异。直方图检验拒绝了大多数硬件扫描所采用的工作噪声模型，因此条件拟合误差并未量化总硬件不确定性。

查看原文标题与摘要

Neutron-alpha scattering on a quantum processor: Spin-orbit-split resonances and weak nonresonant channels

We reconstruct elastic neutron--alpha phase shifts in all five channels through $l=2$ from trapped spectra measured on IBM's Boston quantum computer. Using a four-qubit implementation of the factorized geometric-time RODEO algorithm, we resolve the spin--orbit-split $p_{3/2}$ and $p_{1/2}$ resonances of $^5$He and the small nonresonant $d$-wave phase shifts. Classically constructed eight-dimensional effective Hamiltonians are encoded in three system qubits and probed with a recycled ancilla. We fit 40 trapped energies per channel at five confinement strengths (200 energies in total), using a reciprocal effective-range function for the $d$ waves. The fits yield free-space phase shifts, the elastic cross section, and both $p$-wave poles. The broad $p_{1/2}$ pole is more sensitive to the fit order. On the $0.05\leq E_{\rm cm}\leq6$ MeV grid, the reconstructed phase shifts differ by less than $0.073^\circ$ from an independent radial calculation for the same calibrated interaction. The maximum relative difference in the $l\leq2$ cross section is $0.185\%$. In separate measurements of ten low-lying levels in three time-separated blocks, reversing the evolution times and query phase changes the fitted energies but leaves the ideal ancilla-history probabilities unchanged. Averaging forward and reverse estimates reduces the pooled root-mean-square energy error from $49.59$ eV for forward evolution alone to $20.91$ and $16.37$ eV in two retrospective equal-shot comparisons. The effect varies by channel. Histogram tests reject the working noise model for most hardware scans, so the conditional fit errors do not quantify the total hardware uncertainty.

有限密度下 QC2D 的强子谱学

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们使用格点蒙特卡罗模拟，研究低温下双色 QCD (QC$_2$D) 中强子质量对化学势的依赖性。我们在 $T\approx40\,\mathrm{MeV}$ 的 $32^4$ 格点上计算了所有可能的介子和双夸克关联函数，包括非连通图。在强子相中，我们得到与三色 QCD 类似的介子质量层级。在超流跃迁之后，排序发生变化：$\sigma$ 和 $a_1$ 通道变得更轻，而π介子质量随密度增加。我们还考察了 $U(1)_B$ 对称性破缺的 Nambu--Goldstone 模式对双夸克源的依赖性，并发现在外推至零源后，其在超流相中的质量几乎为零。最后，我们比较了手征伙伴的关联函数，并讨论了手征对称性部分恢复的迹象。本报告基于我们近期对致密 QC$_2$D 中强子谱的研究~\cite{Iida:2026dmw}。

Hadron spectroscopy of QC2D at finite density

We investigate the chemical-potential dependence of hadron masses in two-color QCD (QC$_2$D) at low temperature using lattice Monte Carlo simulations. We calculate all possible meson and diquark correlation functions, including disconnected diagrams, on a $32^4$ lattice at $T\approx40\,\mathrm{MeV}$. In the hadronic phase, we obtain a meson mass hierarchy similar to that of three-color QCD. After the superfluid transition, the ordering changes: the $\sigma$ and $a_1$ channels become lighter, while the pion mass increases with density. We also examine the diquark source dependence of the Nambu--Goldstone mode of $U(1)_B$ symmetry breaking and find an almost vanishing mass in the superfluid phase after extrapolation to zero source. Finally, we compare correlation functions for chiral partners and discuss indications of partial chiral-symmetry restoration. This contribution is based on our recent study of hadron spectra in dense QC$_2$D~\cite{Iida:2026dmw}.

基于相对论 Brueckner 理论、采用 Chebyshev 展开耦合的协变密度泛函

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们根据全 Dirac 空间相对论 Brueckner--Hartree--Fock (RBHF) 自能及相应的状态方程，构建了一个密度依赖的协变能量密度泛函 (CE-ME1)。矢量自能的类空间分量在 Fermi 面上通过约化的标量自能和类时间自能加以考虑，并将其映射到局域 Hartree $\sigma$、$\omega$、$\delta$ 和 $\rho$ 通道。密度依赖耦合在密度范围 $0.08\leq\rho\leq0.48~{\rm fm}^{-3}$ 内由三次 Chebyshev 多项式表示。受约束的能量与饱和性质标定采用了已发表的微观计算中六种同位旋不对称度下的 199 个 RBHF 单粒子能量值，同时保持耦合为正且单调。再现了 Bonn-A RBHF 饱和点、不可压缩系数 $K_\infty=258$ MeV、对称能 $J=33.1$ MeV 和斜率 $L=65.2$ MeV。预测的曲率 $K_{\rm sym}=33$ MeV 不同于已发表的 RBHF 值 $-67$ MeV，说明了局域约化对导数的敏感性。综合能量 RMS 为 $0.613$ MeV，它衡量的是模型偏差，而非统计不确定度。在标定范围内，均匀物质在饱和密度以上仍保持力学稳定且满足因果性。在冷的电荷中性 $npe\mu$ 物质中，CE-ME1 在大部分区间内合理地再现了插值得到的 RBHF 压强和质子分数，但压强偏差在接近区间上边界时增大。DD-ME2 基准更硬，并预测高密度下的质子分数更低。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Covariant density functional from relativistic Brueckner theory with Chebyshev-expanded couplings

We construct a density-dependent covariant energy density functional (CE-ME1) from full-Dirac-space relativistic Brueckner--Hartree--Fock (RBHF) self-energies and the corresponding equation of state. The space-like component of the vector self-energy is accounted for at the Fermi surface through reduced scalar and time-like self-energies, which are mapped onto local Hartree $\sigma$, $\omega$, $\delta$, and $\rho$ channels. The density-dependent couplings are represented by cubic Chebyshev polynomials over the density range $0.08\leq\rho\leq0.48~{\rm fm}^{-3}$. A constrained energy-and-saturation calibration uses 199 RBHF energy-per-particle values from published microscopic computations at six isospin asymmetries, while maintaining positive, monotonic couplings. The Bonn-A RBHF saturation point, incompressibility $K_\infty=258$ MeV, symmetry energy $J=33.1$ MeV, and slope $L=65.2$ MeV are reproduced. The predicted curvature $K_{\rm sym}=33$ MeV differs from the published RBHF value of $-67$ MeV, illustrating the derivative sensitivity of the local reduction. The combined energy RMS is $0.613$ MeV, measuring model discrepancy rather than statistical uncertainty. Uniform matter remains mechanically stable and causal above saturation within the calibrated range. In cold charge-neutral $npe\mu$ matter, CE-ME1 reasonably reproduces the interpolated RBHF pressure and proton fraction over most of the interval, although the pressure discrepancy increases near its upper edge. The DD-ME2 benchmark is stiffer and predicts a lower proton fraction at high density.

具有自洽纵向加速的 1+1 维相对论黏性磁流体动力学

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们研究在共形状态方程 $\varepsilon=3p$ 下具有自洽纵向加速的 $(1+1)$ 维相对论黏性磁流体动力学。我们利用能量守恒、纵向动量守恒以及给定横向磁应力中的剪切弛豫定律，对温度、流体快度和剪切应力进行演化。超越纵向推动不变性后，我们在 Navier--Stokes 近似下推导了微扰解析表达式，并获得具有剪切弛豫的非线性解，从相同初始条件出发比较动力学加速流与给定加速流。初始温度曲率产生纵向压力梯度，从而改变快度斜率、修正纵向膨胀功并产生不同的冷却历程。对于参考参数，剪切黏度对所得中心温度差的增强远强于给定磁应力，而中心温度相等的条件随固有时和初始剪切而变化。在 $\chi=\lambda^*=0.03$ 时，尽管给定流中存在持续的动量残差，但在 $0.6$--$6$~fm/$c$ 内中心温度的最大相对差异为 $0.18\%$。由于速度映射也会进入单位流体快度的熵，即使中心温度非常接近，快度分布仍对流动演化敏感。用理想感应取代给定场，会消除直接磁功，同时在纵向动量平衡中保留磁压和惯性。

1+1 dimensional relativistic viscous magnetohydrodynamics with self-consistent longitudinal acceleration

We study $(1+1)$-dimensional relativistic viscous magnetohydrodynamics with self-consistent longitudinal acceleration under the conformal equation of state $\varepsilon=3p$. We evolve temperature, fluid rapidity, and shear stress using energy conservation, longitudinal momentum conservation, and a shear-relaxation law in a prescribed transverse magnetic stress. Moving beyond longitudinal boost invariance, we derive perturbative analytical expressions in the Navier--Stokes approximation and obtain nonlinear solutions with shear relaxation, comparing dynamical and prescribed accelerating flows from identical initial conditions. The initial temperature curvature generates a longitudinal pressure gradient that alters the rapidity slope, modifies longitudinal expansion work, and produces a distinct cooling history. For the reference parameters, shear viscosity enhances the resulting central temperature difference much more strongly than the prescribed magnetic stress, while the condition for equal central temperatures shifts with proper time and initial shear. At $\chi=\lambda^*=0.03$, the maximum relative difference in central temperature is $0.18\%$ over $0.6$--$6$~fm/$c$, despite a persistent momentum residual in the prescribed flow. Because the velocity mapping also enters the entropy per unit fluid rapidity, the rapidity distribution remains sensitive to the flow evolution even when central temperatures closely agree. Replacing the prescribed field with ideal induction removes direct magnetic work while retaining magnetic pressure and inertia in the longitudinal momentum balance.

Understanding the Origin of Silver in Metal-poor Stars Based on the Non–Local Thermodynamic Equilibrium Analysis of Ag I Lines*

Abstract Silver is a poorly observed neutron-capture element, and understanding its Galactic production history requires accurate abundances for stellar samples covering a broad metallicity range. We refined the method for calculating stellar Ag abundances by considering the non–local thermodynamic equilibrium (NLTE) line formation for Ag i . A comprehensive model atom of silver was built from the atomic data available in the literature, including recent data on inelastic Ag i + H i collisions. We derived abundances using the high-resolution spectra and the synthetic spectrum method with the one-dimensional model atmospheres. From two Ag i lines in the solar spectrum we obtained the NLTE abundances log ε ⊙,Ag = 1.15 and 1.10 for different treatments of collisions with hydrogen atoms. The NLTE abundances of Ag were determined for the first time for 41 stars in the −2.74 ≤ [Fe/H] ≤ −0.56 metallicity range. The NLTE abundance corrections for Ag i 3382 Å were found to be larger for the giants (up to 0.92 dex) than the dwarfs (up to 0.51 dex). In NLTE, Ag in our sample stars is enhanced relatively to Fe and the [Ag/Fe] Galactic trend is very similar to that for [Eu/Fe]; the [Ag/Eu], [Ag/Ba], and [Ag/Sr] abundance ratios of the r-II stars CS22892-052 and CS31082-001 agree very well with the r -process model of Prantzos et al., and the −1.6 ≲ [Fe/H] ≤ −0.56 stars have close-to-solar [Ag/Eu] ratios. We conclude that production of silver was dominated by the main r- process throughout the metallicity range we investigated, with the main s- process contribution not larger than 20% for [Fe/H] ≳ −1.6.

利用深度巡天阵列优化引力波事件的即时射电对应体搜索

摘要 有人提出，持续时间为毫秒量级的即时相干射电辐射可作为含中子星致密天体并合的对应体，并产生于聚合之前、期间或之后不久。对其进行探测将可研究这些并合事件的等离子体环境、磁场、并合遗迹演化和辐射物理。在 GHz 频率下，色散延迟的范围可从几分之一秒到数秒，因此要求望远镜在聚合之前或之后不久就对准源。为此，负延迟或低延迟警报、快速响应，以及在不确定且不断演化的定位条件下优化指向，均至关重要。我们提出了一个使用即将建成的深度巡天阵列 (DSA) 对引力波开展即时后随观测的框架；DSA 是一个高灵敏度、快速转向、宽视场的 GHz 射电阵列。我们模拟低延迟定位序列，并将其嵌入一个具有仰角、视场和转向约束的后随观测环境中。我们将一种强化学习指向策略与一个基线方案进行比较，后者在每一步都转向概率最高的可观测区域。这种峰值跟踪规则是适合初始部署的强健且可解释的基线，而学习得到的策略则针对一部分宽广、多峰或弧形定位区域提供了互补策略。在随机并合时刻，37% 的事件可在 20 ∘ 仰角以上观测；其中，使用零噪声定位图时，∼90% 的双中子星事件可被成功到达并跟踪，而使用高斯噪声图时，这一比例降至 ∼75%。我们在 DSA 原型天线上验证了优化后的指向轨迹。在具有代表性的 700–1025 MHz 子频带内，DSA 后随观测可达到 ∼20 mJy ms 的流量灵敏度，从而探测光度为 ∼10 38 –10 39 erg s −1 的毫秒爆发；该光度处于或低于若干中子星并合相干辐射预测的下限。

Optimizing Prompt Radio Counterpart Searches for Gravitational-wave Events with the Deep Synoptic Array

Abstract Prompt millisecond-duration coherent radio emission is proposed as a counterpart to neutron-star-containing compact mergers, produced before, during, or shortly after coalescence. Its detection would probe the plasma environment, magnetic fields, remnant evolution, and emission physics of these mergers. At GHz frequencies, dispersive delays can range from fractions of a second to several seconds, requiring the telescope to be on source before or soon after coalescence. Negative- or low-latency alerts, rapid response, and optimized pointing under uncertain, evolving localizations are therefore essential. We present a framework for prompt gravitational-wave follow-up with the upcoming Deep Synoptic Array (DSA), a sensitive, fast-slewing, wide-field GHz radio array. We simulate low-latency localization sequences and embed them in a follow-up environment with elevation, field of view, and slew constraints. We compare a reinforcement learning pointing policy with a baseline that slews toward the highest-probability accessible region at each step. This peak-tracking rule is a strong, interpretable baseline for initial deployment, while the learned policy provides a complementary strategy for a subset of broad, multimodal, or arc-shaped localizations. At a random merger time, 37% of events are observable above 20 ∘ elevation; among these, ∼90% of binary neutron star events are successfully reached and tracked using zero-noise localization maps, decreasing to ∼75% with Gaussian-noise maps. We validate the optimized pointing trajectories on prototype DSA antennas. DSA follow-up can reach a fluence sensitivity of ∼20 mJy ms over a representative 700–1025 MHz subband, probing millisecond bursts with luminosities of ∼10 38 –10 39 erg s −1 , at or below the lower end of several coherent-emission predictions for neutron star mergers.

Finite Temperature Effects on g -Modes of Warm Neutron Stars

Abstract We study the effect of temperature on composition-driven g -modes in the cores of warm neutron stars. Using a chiral SU (2) f sigma model, we construct isentropic equations of state and stellar configurations for hot and dense matter. Weak-interaction rates are incorporated into a complex, frequency-dependent dynamical sound speed that approaches the frozen-composition limit for slow reactions and the beta-equilibrated limit for fast reactions. We solve self-consistently for the complex g -mode eigenfrequency and find that its real part depends nonmonotonically on the nuclear symmetry-energy slope parameter L , reaching its largest values near L ≈ 100–110 MeV. Increasing the entropy per baryon accelerates chemical reequilibration and suppresses the oscillation frequency. The associated damping rate reaches a maximum when the weak-interaction relaxation and mode timescales become comparable, signifying that dissipative effects also depend on L . Our results demonstrate that the thermal weak-interaction response and the density dependence of the symmetry energy jointly determine the g -mode spectrum of warm neutron stars.

Euclid. XI. 采用两点相关方法诊断光谱巡天中与恒星有关的系统效应

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

Euclid 光谱巡天将以前所未有的精度测量星系成团性，这要求对观测和仪器系统效应进行严格控制。其中，与恒星有关的效应可能通过光度持久效应以及恒星图像掩膜不完善而污染光谱图像。我们表征了光谱曝光中与恒星有关的效应留下的印记，并量化了它们对星系成团分析的影响。如果这些效应产生特征性角向结构，就可以在数据中识别并表征它们，从而能够评估它们对宇宙学测量的影响。我们使用角向和空间自相关及互相关统计量来量化这些效应。对于星系，我们使用从 EuclidLargeMocks 中提取的模拟光谱星表。我们重点研究了 Euclid 宽域巡天 (EWS) 的一个 330区域。对于恒星，我们考虑了同一区域内真实的 Gaia 星表和恒星星表。为模拟光度持久效应，我们实现了一个依据光谱测量校准的简化探测器级模型，并调节该效应的强度，以向模拟星系星表中引入不同比例的“闯入者”。对于恒星掩膜，我们对数据中用于剔除恒星周围区域的掩膜与用于量化巡天选择函数的随机星表所应用掩膜之间的潜在不一致进行了建模。我们使用 Landy--Szalay 估计量测量了恒星--恒星、星系--星系和恒星--星系角相关函数，并使用一个专用度量量化了相对于预期为零的恒星–星系相关的偏离。最后，我们使用 3D 两点相关函数 (2PCF) 评估了这些系统效应在大尺度上的影响。我们的结果表明，光度持久效应在约 100 尺度处的恒星--星系角互相关中产生一个特征结构，这与 Euclid 抖动模式和棱栅色散几何所设定的尺度一致。它还会引入一个虚假的正互相关信号，其振幅在直至 1^̧irc 的范围内保持近似恒定。我们发现，恒星–星系互相关分析是探测残余持久效应的灵敏工具，能够在类似 Data Release 1 的光谱星表中揭示低至 10% 的虚假污染水平。相比之下，恒星掩膜中的失配会产生强烈的小尺度角向特征，但在现实的 EWS 条件下不会显著影响大尺度 2PCF。

Euclid. XI. A two-point correlation approach to diagnosing star-related systematics in the spectroscopic survey

The Euclid spectroscopic survey will measure galaxy clustering with unprecedented precision, which demands stringent control over observational and instrumental systematics. Among these, star-related effects may contaminate the spectroscopic images through photometric persistence and through imperfect masking of stellar images. We characterized the imprint of star-related effects in the spectroscopic exposures and quantified their impact on galaxy clustering analyses. If these effects produce characteristic angular features, they can be identified and characterized in the data, thereby enabling an assessment of their impact on cosmological measurements. We used angular and spatial auto- and cross-correlation statistics to quantify these effects. For the galaxies we used mock spectroscopic catalogues extracted from EuclidLargeMocks. We focused on a 330 region of the Euclid Wide Survey (EWS). For the stars, we considered the real Gaia and star catalogues in the same area. To simulate photometric persistence, we implemented a simplified detector-level model calibrated on spectroscopic measurements and modulated the strength of the effect to introduce varying fractions of `interlopers' into the mock galaxy catalogues. For the stellar mask, we modelled potential inconsistencies between the mask used to remove regions around stars in the data and the mask applied to the random catalogue used to quantify the survey selection function. We measured the star--star, galaxy--galaxy, and star--galaxy angular correlation functions using the Landy--Szalay estimator and quantified deviations from the expected null star–galaxy correlation with a dedicated metric. Finally, we evaluated the impact of these systematics on large scales using the 3D two-point correlation function (2PCF). Our results show that photometric persistence produces a characteristic feature in the star--galaxy angular cross-correlation at scales of order 100 consistent with the scale set by the Euclid dithering pattern and grism dispersion geometry. It also induces a spurious, positive cross-correlation signal that remains approximately constant in amplitude up to 1^̧irc. We find that star–galaxy cross-correlation analysis is a sensitive tool for detecting residual persistence and is capable of revealing spurious contamination levels as low as 10% in a Data Release 1-like spectroscopic catalogue. In contrast, mismatches in stellar masking produce strong small-scale angular signatures but do not significantly impact the large-scale 2PCF under realistic EWS conditions.

同位旋密度下 Nambu--Jona-Lasinio 模型的闭合形式：正则化能改变什么

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

在零温下，具有同位旋化学势的双味 Nambu-Jona-Lasinio 模型的平均场热力学势归结为两个准粒子能级的发散动量积分。在实际计算中，该积分在不远高于所探测化学势的动量处被截断。我们使用 Hermite 约化，在任意截断下对其进行精确求值。结果是一个代数边界项和三个不完全椭圆积分，每一类各一个。它们在路径终点处的模数、参数和振幅由两个能级在零动量处构成的三角形确定。幂次发散存在于边界项中，而其余发散为对数发散：动量轴是椭圆曲线上的一条开放路径，紫外区是该路径终止处的穿孔。闭合形式使正则化的作用变得明确。随着截断被移除，仅作用于动量的硬截断和光滑截断之间仅相差一个加法常数以及由真空输入确定的两项。介质分离方案复现了所得势，至多相差一个加法常数。相比之下，分别应用于两个能级的截断会在凝聚起始点以下产生同位旋密度，并违反 Silver Blaze 性质。该闭合形式给出了沿同位旋轴由真空延拓而来的状态，无需进行数值动量积分。在采用相同真空拟合的情况下，保持有限截断会给出明显不同的状态。两者之差由仅在截断被移除时才消失的显式项构成：它们是不同的模型，而不是同一个模型的两种正则化。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

The Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model at isospin density in closed form: what a regularization can change

At zero temperature, the mean-field thermodynamic potential of the two-flavour Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model at isospin chemical potential reduces to a divergent momentum integral of two quasiparticle levels. In practice this integral is cut off at momenta not far above the chemical potentials being probed. We evaluate it exactly, at an arbitrary cutoff, using the Hermite reduction. The result is an algebraic boundary term and three incomplete elliptic integrals, one of each kind. Their modulus, parameter and amplitude at the end of the path are fixed by the triangle formed by the two levels at zero momentum. The power divergences reside in the boundary term, while the remaining divergence is logarithmic: the momentum axis is an open path on an elliptic curve, and the ultraviolet is the puncture at which it ends. The closed form makes the role of regularization explicit. As the cutoff is removed, sharp and smooth cutoffs acting on the momentum alone differ only by an additive constant and by two terms that the vacuum inputs fix. The medium separation scheme reproduces the resulting potential up to an additive constant. By contrast, a cutoff applied separately to the two levels generates an isospin density below the onset of condensation and violates the Silver Blaze property. The closed form gives the state that continues the vacuum along the isospin axis without numerical momentum integration. With the same vacuum fit, a cutoff kept finite gives a visibly different state. The two differ by explicit terms that vanish only as the cutoff is removed: they are different models, not two regularizations of one.

Real-time event-level zero-shot learning at 150 ns: towards a foundation model for particle physics experiment triggering

The hardware-based Level-1 triggers of the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) experiments select collisions with algorithms designed for one signature each, acting on a coarse, low-latency view of every event. We present a learned event embedding for several trigger tasks and evaluate its transfer to processes absent from training. Using paired offline and Level-1 views of the same simulated collisions, we train an offline transformer with a supervised metric loss and distil it, event by event, into a quantised Level-1 transformer whose bit widths are learned under a fixed resource budget. Trained on 25 Standard Model processes, the embedding places 28 held-out processes next to their physical relatives. A nearest-neighbour selection against background collisions, which never enter encoder training, rejects 99% of them. It retains a median of 90% of seen and 96% of unseen Standard Model events and 88% of a set of 52 beyond-the-Standard-Model benchmark samples, the best-performing score across the three groups among the baselines evaluated on the same events. A second gate against the seen processes isolates high-mass dilepton signals into label-free clusters, and long-lived signals too when displaced-track information is available. With 7512 parameters and an FPGA latency of 147 ns, the encoder uses one event representation, learned once, to derive several selections, opening the door to a foundation model for the LHC's hardware-based triggers.

EIC 上的极化核 DVCS

Polarized Nuclear DVCS at the EIC

The Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) will enable a series of measurements at unprecedented energies and luminosities, providing new opportunities to investigate the microscopic structure of nucleons and nuclei at small $x_B$. Exclusive processes such as Deeply Virtual Compton Scattering (DVCS) offer unique access to the three-dimensional structure of hadrons through Generalized Parton Distributions (GPDs), while polarized electron and ion beams further enable detailed studies of spin-dependent structure. A model for coherent DVCS on polarized $^3$Heis developed and applied to simulations of for $9\times166$-GeV $e^3$He collisions at the EIC. Using this framework, the statistical precision achievable is estimated for measurements of beam-spin asymmetries and for the extraction of the Compton Form Factors (CFFs) $\mathcal H_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ and $\tilde{\mathcal H}_{^3\mathrm{He}}$. Early EIC data are found to enable precise differential measurements of the unpolarized CFF $\mathcal H_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ and to provide significant constraints on its real and imaginary components. By contrast, meaningful constraints on the polarized CFF $\tilde{\mathcal H}_{^3\mathrm{He}}$ require substantially larger integrated luminosities. The kinematics of the recoil $^3$He nucleus are also examined, and the advanced far-forward detector capabilities at the EIC required to tag the intact nucleus and perform fully exclusive measurements of coherent nuclear DVCS are discussed.

Hint of universality in $\alpha$-cluster nuclei

The empirical ground-state energies of the $\alpha$-cluster nuclei are placed on the universal function that governs $N$-boson systems with a shallow state. The data fall on the finite-range-corrected universal curve, suggesting Efimov-type universality. No excited states, transition strengths, radii, or cluster geometries enter the analysis; the nuclei are treated as $N$ structureless bosons. A zero-shape $\alpha$-$\alpha$ Bargmann potential, describing the $^{8}$Be resonance, provides the pairwise interaction with no shape freedom. Simple three- and four-body forces, each a single Gaussian in the hyperradius, are fitted to $^{12}$C and $^{16}$O; microscopic calculations for $^{20}$Ne and $^{24}$Mg ($N=5,6$) follow as parameter-free predictions. A compact $0^+_2$ state near the Hoyle energy emerges in $^{12}$C without being targeted by the fit.

We find that the cross sections are sizable, and that future measurements in this channel can help constrain BFKL dynamics and improve our understanding of high-energy QCD.

查看原文标题与摘要

Construction of a conserved energy-momentum tensor of the Skyrmion coupled to additional dynamical degrees in the large-$N_c$ expansion

We formulate an order-by-order construction of a conserved energy-momentum tensor (EMT) in the large-$N_c$ expansion of the model where the Skyrmion is coupled to additional dynamical fields. We show that, expanding the equations of motion and the EMT in powers of $1/N_c$, the inclusion of the backreaction to the Skyrmion from the additional fields is essential for achieving the conservation law of the EMT, which is contrasted with the fixed-background approximation where the effects of backreaction are neglected. We apply the construction to the model for hyperons based on the Callan-Klebanov bound-state approach, where the antikaon is bound to the Skyrmion, to obtain the EMT up to next-to-leading order of the $1/N_c$ expansion. We explicitly show that the divergence of the EMT from the backreaction for the Skyrmion exactly cancels that of the kaon to achieve the conservation of the total EMT. We find that the conserved EMT gives a negative hyperon D-term whose magnitude is smaller than that of the nucleon D-term and radii of gravitational form factors for the hyperon smaller than those for the nucleon, which implies that the hyperon is more compact than the nucleon.

Chiral crossover responses in a magnetic field: Finite-$\mu_5$ matching in the NJL model

We investigate how independent information at finite chiral chemical potential $\mu_5$ constrains pseudocritical-temperature responses in the Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model under a magnetic field. Vacuum calibration alone leaves the coefficient $c_r$ of the additional interaction $\delta\Omega_r=c_r\mu_5^2(M-m_0)^2$ undetermined. Binding energy formulas for Skyrme energy density functional and two-parameter density distribution

Abstract The formulas for the binding energy of nuclei for the Skyrme energy density functional and two-parameter distribution of proton and neutron densities are obtained. The kinetic energy density functional is taken in the extended Thomas-Fermi approximation with accuracy included ℏ 2 terms, and with the effective mass equal to the nucleon mass. It is shown that the binding energy of the nucleus is presented in series on A 1/3 and Y 2 , where A is the number of nucleons in the nucleus and Y = (N -Z)/A is the asymmetry parameter dependent on the number of protons Z and neutrons N in the nucleus. Expressions for the coefficients of the binding energy formula proportional to A k Y m , where k = 0, 1/3, 2/3, 1 and m = 0, 2, 4, 6, were obtained. The volume coefficients of these formulas, i.e., which are proportional to A 1 , are connected to the Skyrme force parameters and the density of the nucleus in the center. The surface (∝ A 2/3 ) and curvature (∝ A 1/3 ) terms, as well as the terms proportional to the A 0 , depend on the Skyrme force parameters, the density of the nucleus in the center, and the diffuseness of the density distributions.

协变手征有效场论中次次领阶的反核子--核子相互作用

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

Antinucleon--nucleon interaction at next-to-next-to-leading order in covariant chiral effective field theory

We construct antinucleon--nucleon ($\bar NN$) interactions at next-to-leading order (NLO) and next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in covariant chiral effective field theory. The short-range interaction is represented by complex covariant contact terms, while the long-range part is obtained from the corresponding nucleon--nucleon interaction through $G$-parity transformation. The low-energy constants are determined from the Nijmegen partial-wave analysis, and phase shifts and mixing angles are calculated for all partial waves with $J\leq2$. Compared with NLO, NNLO improves the overall description of the phase shifts and mixing angles, particularly in several $J=2$ channels, although the improvement is not uniform across all partial waves or at higher energies. We estimate Bayesian truncation uncertainties and compare the results with heavy-baryon chiral interactions. We also revisit the pole candidates identified in our leading-order study and calculate the corresponding threshold parameters. The isoscalar ${}^{13}P_0$ pole remains a few MeV below the $\bar NN$ threshold with a width of about 40 MeV at both NLO and NNLO, whereas the $S$-wave candidates show stronger sensitivity to chiral orders. The NNLO covariant $\bar NN$ interaction therefore provides a useful basis for studies of antinucleon--nucleon scattering and near-threshold phenomena.

基于运动方程耦合簇理论响应的自能及其到 GW、GW+SOSEX 和 GF2 的约化

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们通过将参考格林函数与由含单激发和双激发的运动方程耦合簇理论（EOM-CCSD）得到的中性激发响应相耦合，构建频率依赖的单粒子关联自能。外部相互作用顶角的直接、混合直接-反对称化和完全反对称化选择定义了三种近似，它们均具有无需数值频率积分的显式谱表示。在裸态极限下，混合形式和完全反对称化形式恢复二阶格林函数理论（GF2），而直接形式仅保留其环图贡献。采用直接随机相位近似（RPA）响应时，直接形式和混合形式分别约化为 $G_0W_0$ 以及 $G_0W_0$ 加二阶屏蔽交换（SOSEX）。对于分子电离势，以 EOM-CCSD 响应取代 RPA 响应通常会降低电离能，而在一个外部顶角引入交换则会提高电离能。这些显著的相反效应会部分抵消，尽管无论以 IP-EOM-CCSD 还是 QUESTDB 参考能量为基准，混合近似相较于常规 $G_0W_0$ 都未表现出系统性改进（相对于 QUESTDB 理论最佳估计的 aug-cc-pVDZ 平均绝对误差：0.45 对 0.36~eV）。三阶微扰分析表明，这三种近似保留了粒子-空穴图的不同贡献和权重，同时省略了显式的粒子-粒子与空穴-空穴梯形图贡献以及静态自能。特别是，完全反对称化形式在二阶恢复 GF2，但对其保留的三阶图赋予了不正确的权重。这些发现凸显了在构建精确的自能近似时平衡处理屏蔽和顶角修正的重要性。

查看原文标题与摘要

A response-based self-energy from equation-of-motion coupled-cluster theory and its reduction to GW, GW+SOSEX, and GF2

We construct a frequency-dependent one-particle correlation self-energy by coupling a reference Green's function to the neutral excitation response obtained from equation-of-motion coupled-cluster theory with singles and doubles (EOM-CCSD). Direct, mixed direct-antisymmetrized, and fully antisymmetrized choices of the external interaction vertices define three approximations, all admitting an explicit spectral representation without numerical frequency integration. In the bare-state limit, the mixed and fully antisymmetrized forms recover second-order Green's-function theory (GF2), whereas the direct form retains only its ring contribution. With a direct random-phase approximation (RPA) response, the direct and mixed forms reduce to $G_0W_0$ and $G_0W_0$ plus second-order screened exchange (SOSEX), respectively. For molecular ionization potentials, replacing the RPA response by EOM-CCSD generally lowers the ionization energies, whereas introducing exchange at one external vertex raises them. These substantial opposing effects partially compensate, although the mixed approximation does not systematically improve upon conventional $G_0W_0$, either against IP-EOM-CCSD or QUESTDB reference energies (aug-cc-pVDZ mean absolute errors relative to the QUESTDB theoretical best estimates: 0.45 vs 0.36~eV). A third-order perturbative analysis reveals that the three approximations retain different contributions and weights of particle-hole diagrams while omitting explicit particle-particle and hole-hole ladder contributions and the static self-energy. In particular, the fully antisymmetrized form recovers GF2 at second order but assigns incorrect weights to its retained third-order diagrams. These findings highlight the importance of a balanced treatment of screening and vertex corrections in constructing accurate self-energy approximations.

核子上双介子电磁产生的一般形式体系

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们为核子上的双介子电磁产生 $\gamma_{\rm v}N\to\Phi_1\Phi_2B$ 建立了一般形式体系，该体系可用于广泛能区内的单道和耦合道分析。本着 Berends 等人为单介子电产生所引入分解方法的思路，将强子流展开为一组 16 个规范不变和洛伦兹不变的矩阵，其中四个对于实光子为零。相应的洛伦兹不变（Dirac）振幅可从任意反应模型获得，并映射为 16 个类 CGLN Pauli 振幅，由此可直接得到五重微分截面及其横向、纵向和干涉响应函数。尽管这些矩阵并非全都线性无关，但它们直接源于流的协变分解，因此可以从 Feynman 图中直接读出振幅，并且观测量具有更简单、更对称的形式。作为首个应用，我们根据树级核子 Born 项计算阈值附近的 $\gamma p\to\pi^0\pi^0p$ 和 $\gamma p\to\pi^+\pi^0n$ 反应。该示例明确展示了如何从 Feynman 图中读出 Dirac 振幅，以及各个图所破坏的规范不变性如何在总振幅中得到恢复，并在纳入强子形状因子时得到保持。因此，该形式体系为未来双介子光产生和电产生的理论与唯象研究提供了灵活的基础。论文中提供了更完整的摘要。

查看原文标题与摘要

A general formalism for electromagnetic production of two mesons on the nucleon

We develop a general formalism for the electromagnetic production of two mesons on the nucleon, $\gamma_{\rm v}N\to\Phi_1\Phi_2B$, that can be used in single-channel as well as coupled-channel analyses over a wide range of energies. In the spirit of the decomposition introduced by Berends et al. for single-meson electroproduction, the hadronic current is expanded in a set of 16 gauge- and Lorentz-invariant matrices, four of which vanish for real photons. The corresponding Lorentz-invariant (Dirac) amplitudes can be obtained from any reaction model and are mapped onto 16 CGLN-like Pauli amplitudes, from which the five-fold differential cross section and its transverse, longitudinal, and interference response functions follow directly. Although these matrices are not all linearly independent, they follow directly from the covariant decomposition of the current, so the amplitudes can be read off from the Feynman diagrams and the observables take a simpler, more symmetric form. As a first application, we calculate the $\gamma p\to\pi^0\pi^0p$ and $\gamma p\to\pi^+\pi^0n$ reactions near threshold from tree-level nucleon Born terms. This example shows explicitly how the Dirac amplitudes are read off from Feynman diagrams and how gauge invariance, which is violated by the individual diagrams, is restored in the total amplitude and preserved when hadronic form factors are included. The formalism thus provides a versatile basis for future theoretical and phenomenological studies of double-meson photo- and electroproduction. A more complete abstract is provided in the paper.

核合成中的非平衡稳态

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

稳态行为是许多核合成过程的基础，在这些过程中，尽管核反应迅速进行，原子核的粒子数仍可近似保持不变。现有理论描述主要关注已经建立的化学平衡，如核统计平衡（NSE）和准平衡（QSE），而非平衡稳态（NESSs）可以在特定于过程的近似中形成，例如经典 $\beta$-流 $r$-过程。我们建立了描述核反应网络中 NESSs 的一般框架，将稳态条件表述为相关网络子集的约化线性描述中反应流之间的平衡。我们将该框架应用于涵盖不同稳态区制的四种代表性核合成情景：富 $\alpha$ 冻结期间核子与 $\alpha$ 粒子之间的非平衡、经典 $\beta$-流稳态、中微子辅助的 $\nu r$-过程，以及 $r$-过程的裂变循环阶段。特别是，裂变循环情景由准稳态（QSS）近似描述，该近似在不守恒原子核总数的同时维持两个原子核之间的粒子数比。该框架再现了每种情形的特征丰度模式，同时对其底层稳态行为给出了统一解释。本征值谱揭示了网络结构，并表征丰度分布的弛豫时间尺度。这些结果建立了一种分析超越化学平衡条件的核合成的一般方法，并为将基于图的方法扩展到复杂核反应网络奠定了基础。

查看原文标题与摘要

Non-Equilibrium Steady States in Nucleosynthesis

Steady-state behavior underlies many nucleosynthesis processes in which the population number of nuclei can remain nearly constant despite the rapid nuclear reactions. While existing theoretical descriptions largely focus on established chemical equilibrium, as in nuclear statistical equilibrium (NSE) and quasi equilibrium (QSE), non-equilibrium steady states (NESSs) can form in process-specific approximations such as classical $\beta$-flow $r$-process. We develop a general framework for describing NESSs in nuclear reaction networks by formulating the steady-state condition as a balance of reaction flows within a reduced linear description of the relevant network subset. We apply the framework to four representative nucleosynthesis scenarios spanning different steady-state regimes: disequilibrium between nucleons and $\alpha$ particles during $\alpha$-rich freeze-out, the classical $\beta$-flow steady state, the neutrino-assisted $\nu r$-process, and the fission-cycling phase of the $r$-process. In particular, the fission-cycling scenario is described by a quasi-steady-state (QSS) approximation maintaining the population ratios between two nuclei while not conserving the total number of nuclei. The framework reproduces the characteristic abundance pattern for each case while providing a unified interpretation of the underlying steady-state behavior. The eigenvalue spectrum reveals network structure and characterizes the relaxation timescales of abundance distributions. These results establish a general approach for analyzing nucleosynthesis beyond the condition of chemical equilibrium and provide a foundation for extending graph-based methods to complicated nuclear reaction networks.

High-rate accumulation of ${8.9\times10^9}$ positrons using a linac-driven buffer-gas trap combined with a SiC remoderator

We report the accumulation of $(8.9 \pm 0.9)\times10^9$ positrons in less than 35 minutes in a Penning--Malmberg trap connected to the GBAR electron-linac-based positron source. To our knowledge, this exceeds the largest previously reported trapped-positron population by more than a factor of two and was achieved at an 18-fold higher average accumulation rate of $4.5\times10^6$~e$^+$/s. The accumulated positron clouds are intended for the creation of dense positronium targets to produce keV-antihydrogen beams by charge exchange with antiprotons delivered by the CERN AD/ELENA facility. The system combines a low-energy electron linac, a modified Surko-type buffer-gas trap enhanced by a silicon carbide remoderator, and a high-field Penning--Malmberg trap operated as a positron accumulator. Time-resolved annihilation measurements provide insight into the capture and cooling dynamics, while comparisons of CO$_2$ and SF$_6$ show that the trapping signal reaches saturation at lower gas pressures with SF$_6$. Measurements show that the SiC-remoderator-based trapping scheme more than doubles the trapping efficiency, contributing to a more than threefold increase in the positron accumulation rate compared with the original configuration.

迈向利用古老矿物探测中微子和暗物质：橄榄石中重离子径迹的 TEM 研究

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

数十亿年来，太阳中微子、超新星中微子和大气中微子，以及可能存在的弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）暗物质，一直在我们脚下的地球内部发生相互作用。“古探测器”技术旨在探测并表征这些事件诱发的晶体缺陷，尤其是高能核反冲产生的缺陷；在某些矿物中，这些缺陷能够在这样的时间尺度上保存下来。此类缺陷可表现为核反冲径迹，其宽度为数纳米量级、长度可延伸至数百微米，并可通过具有纳米尺度分辨率的显微镜进行探测。为了检验古探测器技术的可行性，并研究橄榄石等有前景的候选矿物中径迹缺陷的形成和形貌，我们使用离子辐照人为植入径迹，以有效模拟天体物理粒子相互作用。我们使用扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM），研究了经 15 MeV Au$^{+5}$ 辐照的橄榄石晶体中重离子径迹宽度随深度的变化，并将其与离子能量相关联。与以往在受辐照样品表面测量径迹的研究不同，我们通过对受辐照样品进行聚焦离子束切片，在不同靶深处进行测量。未使用蚀刻技术来增强径迹。此外，我们还将结果与使用 SRIM 软件所得的模拟预测以及采用多种离子种类和能量的先前结果进行了比较。值得注意的是，我们发现，在所研究的能量范围（0.4-12.9 MeV）内，径迹连续性的显著变化表明电子阻止本领占主导与核阻止本领占主导之间发生了跃迁，这与模拟预测一致。总体而言，橄榄石中产生的径迹表明，该矿物是古探测的一种很有吸引力的候选材料，在 MeV 尺度下能够稳定形成径迹。

Toward Neutrino and Dark Matter Detection with Ancient Minerals: TEM Study of Heavy-Ion Tracks in Olivine

Solar, supernova, and atmospheric neutrinos, and possibly weakly interacting massive particle (WIMP) dark matter, have been interacting in the Earth beneath our feet for billions of years. The ''paleo-detector'' technique seeks to detect and characterize the induced crystalline defects from these events, in particular from energetic nuclear recoils, which in some minerals can be preserved on these timescales. Such defects can manifest as nuclear recoil tracks, on the order of a few nanometers wide and extending up to hundreds of microns in length, which can be detected with nanoscale-resolution microscopy. In order to test the feasibility of the paleo-detector technique and to study the formation and morphology of track defects in promising mineral candidates like olivine, we use ion irradiation to artificially implant tracks to effectively mimic astrophysical particle interactions. We present a study of heavy-ion track width as a function of depth, which we relate to ion energy, in an olivine crystal irradiated with 15 MeV Au$^{+5}$ using scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM). Unlike previous studies, which measure tracks at the surface of the irradiated sample, we instead take measurements at various target depths via focused ion-beam sectioning of the irradiated sample. No etching techniques are used to enhance the tracks. In addition, we provide a comparison to predictions from simulations using SRIM software and previous results with a variety of ion species and energies. Notably, we find that a significant change in track continuity across the energy range studied (0.4-12.9 MeV) is indicative of the transition between electronic and nuclear stopping power dominance, consistent with the simulations' predictions. Overall, the tracks produced in olivine indicate that this mineral is an attractive candidate for paleo-detection, with robust track creation at the MeV scale.
