FGPA-based advanced muon trigger algorithms for long-lived particle detection at the High-Luminosity LHC

The CMS Level-1 trigger must reconstruct muons in real time under stringent latency and hardware resource constraints. Conventional algorithms lose efficiency for high-energy muons undergoing radiative energy losses and can be suboptimal for displaced trajectories, limiting sensitivity to exotic signatures such as long-lived particles (LLPs). The Phase-2 upgrade introduces advanced FPGAs that enable more sophisticated reconstruction algorithms. This work presents two complementary developments addressing these challenges. In the barrel region, a dedicated algorithm detects showers from Drift Tube hit patterns at the first processing stage. At the subsequent filtering stage, shower primitives are correlated with conventional trigger primitives, currently targeting radiatively showering high-momentum muons, with LLP signatures as a broader objective. Simulation studies yield approximately $80\%$ shower-classification accuracy and an estimated matching efficiency above $80\%$ for radiating muons. Firmware prototypes demonstrate a shower-detection latency of approximately $20\,\mathrm{ns}$ and sub-bunch-crossing latency for preliminary matching logic. In parallel, a hardware-oriented optimization methodology is investigated for future graph neural network based displaced-muon reconstruction in the barrel--endcap overlap region. Using a GraphSAGE proxy on a benchmark dataset, a workflow combining integer quantization, power-of-two scaling, and data-driven bit-width optimization achieves $(75.0 \pm 1.1)\%$ accuracy and a deterministic latency of $19$ cycles ($52.8\,\mathrm{ns}$ at $360\,\mathrm{MHz}$), with only $20\%$ digital signal processor utilization. These results establish a basis for implementation studies using CMS muon data.

Tritium breeding lifetime assessment of a ceramic-based blanket in a fusion tokamak device

Abstract Achieving tritium self-sufficiency is a critical requirement for the viability of future fusion power plants. Here, a comprehensive assessment of the operational lifetimes of ceramic breeder materials under neutron irradiation within a tokamak environment is presented, focusing on the evolution of the tritium breeding ratio (TBR) over reactor-relevant timescales. This is defined here as the operational timeframe at which the TBR is maintained above a critical threshold of ≈ 1.10. Using the depletion capabilities within the OpenMC neutronics code, both conventional ceramic breeders (Li 2 TiO 3 , Li 4 SiO 4 , and advanced ceramic breeder—ACB) and emerging hybrid breeder-multiplier ceramics (Li 8 PbO 6 , Li 2 Be 2 O 3 ) are assessed for operational lifetime strategies. Initial optimisation identifies breeder-to-multiplier volumetric ratios that maximise TBR at time zero, followed by time-dependent simulations to quantify TBR degradation due to lithium depletion during service. It is shown that while higher initial multiplier fractions enhance TBR, they also accelerate lithium depletion, reducing blanket lifetime. Conversely, hybrid ceramics with intrinsic neutron multiplication show reduced sensitivity to additional multipliers. A novel layer-wise optimisation approach, varying breeder and multiplier distributions radially and poloidally, is shown to extend blanket lifetimes and maintain TBR above the critical threshold for longer periods demonstrating lifetime limitations may be elsewhere within the breeder blanket. Verification against FISPACT-II confirms the accuracy of the OpenMC depletion methodology utilised. The results highlight the importance of spatially optimised material distributions and lithium inventory management in maximising breeder blanket longevity. This work provides a robust framework for the design of next-generation solid breeder blankets, offering practical strategies to minimise replacement frequency and support sustainable fusion energy.

从p+p到Pb+Pb的奇异性、熵和化学非平衡：系统尺寸轴上的$K^+/\pi^+$峰

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

中心Pb+Pb碰撞中$\langle K^+\rangle/\langle\pi^+\rangle$激发函数在$\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 7.6$ GeV附近的极大值（“峰”），被解释为退禁闭开始时奇异性产生与增长更快的熵产生之间的竞争。Letessier、Rafelski及其合作者开展的统计强子化分析表明，只有将轻夸克相空间占有率$\gamma_q$设为自由参数，才能定量描述该峰，并且从30$A$ GeV起，$\gamma_q$接近π介子凝聚极限。我们沿着NA61/SHINE（p+p、Be+Be、Ar+Sc、初步Xe+La）和NA49（Pb+Pb）所提供的系统尺寸轴开展这一分析。据我们所知，我们的主要新结果是对2021和2024年发表的Be+Be和Ar+Sc $4\pi$多重数进行的首次非平衡分析：在Ar+Sc中，轻夸克的化学平衡（$\gamma_q = 1$）并不受支持，但这是由$K^+/K^-$不对称性而非π介子产额所致，而Be+Be数据无法作出区分。对于中心Pb+Pb，我们对NA49最终数据的拟合证实了早先的结果，其中在30$A$-80$A$ GeV下最为稳健；这种倾向基于每个净重子所产生强子数的过量以及π介子的Bose-Einstein增强。通过将$\langle K^+\rangle/\langle\pi^+\rangle$分解为每个受伤核子的奇异性产额和π介子产额，我们发现，Be+Be与Ar+Sc之间的阶跃源于每个参与者的奇异性变为原来的两倍，而该峰仅出现在Pb+Pb中，其中每个参与者的π介子产额在20$A$-40$A$ GeV下受到抑制。部分子-强子输运（PHSD）能够重现Pb+Pb中的峰，但也预测Ar+Sc和Xe+La中存在峰，而观测中并未发现。

Strangeness, entropy, and chemical non-equilibrium from p+p to Pb+Pb: the $K^+/\pi^+$ horn on the system-size axis

The maximum ("horn") of the $\langle K^+\rangle/\langle\pi^+\rangle$ excitation function in central Pb+Pb collisions near $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 7.6$ GeV is interpreted as the competition between strangeness production and the faster rise of entropy production at the onset of deconfinement. Statistical hadronization analyses by Letessier, Rafelski, and collaborators have shown that the horn is described quantitatively only if the light-quark phase-space occupancy $\gamma_q$ is free, with $\gamma_q$ near the pion condensation limit from 30$A$ GeV upward. We carry this analysis along the system-size axis provided by NA61/SHINE (p+p, Be+Be, Ar+Sc, preliminary Xe+La) and NA49 (Pb+Pb). Our main new result is the first non-equilibrium analysis, to our knowledge, of the Be+Be and Ar+Sc $4\pi$ multiplicities, published in 2021 and 2024: in Ar+Sc, chemical equilibrium of light quarks ($\gamma_q = 1$) is disfavored, but through the $K^+/K^-$ asymmetry rather than the pion yields, while the Be+Be data do not discriminate. For central Pb+Pb, our fits to the final NA49 data confirm the earlier result, most robustly at 30$A$-80$A$ GeV; the preference rests on the excess of produced hadrons per net baryon and on the Bose-Einstein enhancement of pions. Decomposing $\langle K^+\rangle/\langle\pi^+\rangle$ into strangeness and pion yields per wounded nucleon, we find that the step between Be+Be and Ar+Sc is carried by a doubling of strangeness per participant, whereas the horn appears only in Pb+Pb, where the pion yield per participant is suppressed at 20$A$-40$A$ GeV. Parton-hadron transport (PHSD) reproduces the Pb+Pb horn but predicts one also in Ar+Sc and Xe+La, where none is observed.

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

摘要 采用回旋动理学粒子模拟和理论分析，研究了热等离子体非线性导致的环形 Alfv{'e}n 本征模（TAE）非线性饱和。数值模拟基于国际托卡马克物理活动（ITPA）参数进行，重点考察单一-$n$ TAE 在单独存在或伴随 $n=0$ 带状场（ZF）时的演化与饱和，从而排除具有不同环向模数的 TAE 之间的交叉耦合。这里，$n$ 为环向模数。在滤除 ZF 的模拟中，我们发现，对于 $\gamma_{\rm L}/\omega_n>0.47\%$，TAE 的饱和水平由热等离子体非线性控制，并且对线性增长率 $\gamma_{\rm L}$ 的依赖很弱，即饱和水平具有“刚性”。我们发现，TAE 的频率随其振幅增大而降低，这是由粒子模拟中普遍存在的热等离子体相空间带状结构（PSZS）引起的。当该模式并入连续谱时，TAE 最终可以达到饱和。随着这一过程的进行，可以观察到相邻极向谐波的分离以及模式向高能粒子模的跃迁。在包含 ZF 的模拟中，ZF 可以从根本上抵消热等离子体 PSZS 的影响，使 TAE 饱和水平相比无 ZF 情况提高到约 2 倍，这意味着在评估 TAE 饱和水平时有必要纳入 ZF。

On nonlinear saturation of toroidal Alfvén eigenmode due to thermal plasma nonlinearities

Abstract The nonlinear saturation of toroidal Alfv{'e}n eigenmode (TAE) due to thermal plasma nonlinearities is investigated using gyrokinetic particle-in-cell simulations and theoretical analysis. Numerical simulations are performed based on the International Tokamak Physics Activity (ITPA) parameters to focus on the evolution and saturation of single-$n$ TAE, either alone or with $n=0$ zonal fields (ZFs), thereby excluding the cross coupling between TAEs with different toroidal mode numbers. Here, $n$ is the toroidal mode number. In the simulations with ZFs filtered out, we find that the saturation level of TAE is governed by thermal plasma nonlinearities for $\gamma_{\rm L}/\omega_n>0.47\%$, which has weak dependence on the linear growth rate $\gamma_{\rm L}$, i.e., ``stiffness'' in saturation level. We find that the frequency of TAE decreases as the amplitude of it increases, which is induced by the phase-space zonal structure (PSZS) of thermal plasmas universally existed in particle-in-cell simulations. The saturation of TAE can be finally reached when the mode merges into the continuum. Following this process, the separation of neighboring poloidal harmonics and mode transition to energetic particle modes can be observed. In simulations with ZFs, ZFs can essentially counteract the effects of PSZS of thermal plasmas, leading to roughly a factor of 2 enhancement of the TAE saturation level compared to the case without ZFs, implying the necessity of including ZFs in evaluating the saturation level of TAE.

Near scrape-off layer decay lengths in D, T and DT plasmas on JET

Abstract The near scrape-off layer (SOL) decay lengths of electron temperature (λ T ), electron density (λ n ), and electron pressure (λ p ) strongly influence the peak particle and heat fluxes to the divertor target, thereby constraining the operational space of a fusion reactor design through power-exhaust limitations. This study compares hydrogen isotopes (D, T and DT) to investigate the dependence of near-SOL decay lengths on the turbulence control parameter α t in ELMy and quasi-continuous exhaust (QCE) regimes on JET. Outboard decay lengths were measured using midplane Thomson scattering, and the separatrix position was determined self-consistently assuming Spitzer–Härm electron heat conduction. Results showed that both λ T and λ p increased with α t and did not contradict the Eich et al. scalings from ASDEX Upgrade (Eich et al. 2020 Nucl. Fusion 60 056016) within experimental uncertainty. These scalings include the dependences α t ∝1/√m i and, at constant α t , a linear scaling of the SOL width with the ion poloidal gyroradius (ρ s,pol ∝√m i ). The λ n measurements exhibited more scatter, indicating different particle and heat transport mechanisms. Trends for ELMy and QCE conditions were similar. Tritium pulses exhibited lower electron temperatures and higher electron densities at both the separatrix and pedestal top compared to their reference deuterium pulses. Divertor configuration affected decay length measurements, indicating that target recycling plays a role not captured by the α t framework. Separatrix quantities n e,sep and T e,sep were consistent with the separatrix operational space model (Eich and Manz 2021 Nucl. Fusion 61 086017).

Cross-machine density control experiments in DIII-D and KSTAR with relevance for ITER and FPPs

Abstract Cross-machine density-control experiments on DIII-D and KSTAR have demonstrated the ability to control the plasma density using an end-to-end, model-based control strategy relevant for ITER and fusion power plants (FPPs). This cross-machine work has proved the generality of density control based on particle-balance models. In addition, the density-control design is adaptive to handle uncertainties in the global particle confinement time and actuator fueling efficiency, and combines actuators with radically different time scales (i.e., fast deuterium pellet injection and emulated slow gas puffing). Prior to \mbox{DIII-D} and KSTAR experiments, simulation testing of the adaptive density controller has been performed for a variety of machine and plasma parameters to replicate the unknown conditions that would be found in experiments. As a result, robust experimental results have been obtained in both DIII-D and KSTAR despite using different plasma scenarios, varying wall conditions, and machine-specific gas and pellet injection systems. Moreover, the density-control models have been extended to a database of ITER-baseline discharges on \mbox{ASDEX-U}, TCV, and JET, highlighting the potential applicability of the modeling approach to future devices when using high-fidelity simulated data. This collaborative work has been carried out under the International Tokamak Physics and Engineering Activity framework, within the Integrated Operating Scenarios topical group.

An Innovative Photon-Driven Subcritical Reactor Concept Powered by Synchrotron Radiation Source

Most reactors are fuelled with enriched uranium, whose production is proliferation-sensitive and concentrated in a few suppliers, and discharge fuel that still contains fissile material and accumulates in storage. Accelerator-driven subcritical reactors relax the requirements on the fuel, but proton beams must travel in vacuum to a spallation target inside the core. Here we show that synchrotron radiation can drive a subcritical heavy-water reactor fuelled with natural uranium or with spent fuel loaded without reprocessing. A photon beam with a critical energy of 10 MeV, from a proposed 32 GeV electron storage ring, releases neutrons in a uranium converter at the centre of the core. Coupled photon-neutron Monte Carlo simulations show that the two cores, loading different fuels but being at a multiplication factor close to 0.985, reach the same gain: one 400 kW beamline drives each of them close to 4.4 MW of fission power, about 11 times the beam power. A storage ring with 50 beamlines could drive one core from each beamline, every core controlled through its own photon beam. These results make synchrotron radiation a candidate driver for thermal subcritical reactors that need neither enrichment nor reprocessing, complementary to proton-driven fast systems for transmutation.

Ultrabroadband, high-sensitivity time-resolved terahertz spectroscopy enabled by a Yb-based laser system

Ultrafast optical fields have introduced numerous novel light-induced states in quantum materials. Identifying these states and elucidating their formation mechanisms require comprehensive, quantitative access to nonequilibrium electrodynamics across relevant energy scales. Here, we develop a Yb-laser-based time-resolved terahertz spectroscopy (TRTS) platform combining multi-pass-cell pulse compression, spintronic terahertz generation, and air-biased coherent detection. A single time-domain waveform scan provides continuous phase-resolved spectral coverage to 25 THz ($\sim$100 meV) with a Fourier resolution of 0.1 THz ($\sim$0.4 meV). This capability enables the characterization of both low-frequency charge dynamics and higher-energy resonant excitations in quantum materials under a common temporal reference. The system achieves a single-scan time-domain signal-to-noise ratio of 72 dB with long-term waveform stability, while preserving most of the laser pulse energy for tunable excitation. Measurements on intrinsic GaAs resolve extremely weak phonon features above 23 THz and demonstrate quantitative retrieval of equilibrium and transient complex optical properties. The resulting platform can provide a more complete microscopic picture of light-induced states in quantum materials.

只提议，绝不指派：在机构配额下依据排序意愿公平分配实验粒子物理合作组的轮班

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

实验粒子物理合作组通过轮班为探测器运行配备人员，轮班由来自数十家机构的成员在控制室现场或远程承担。现行的轮班填补惯例是采用自行报名的积分制度：机构负有一定的积分配额，开放预订时，人员选择自己想要的席位。本文描述了一种方法：改为在机构配额下依据排序意愿分配席位，以邀约形式向每个人发布结果，由其接受或拒绝，并在发布任何内容之前由独立检查器重新核验每一条规则：平台只提议，绝不指派，并证明其遵守了规则。积分和配额模型继承自当前惯例。在这一核心基础上，本文增加了一项配额规则，使不可分割的轮班块对于在一个周期内所占份额小于一个轮班块的机构仍然公平；还增加了四项规则，使公平性不只体现在数量上而且清晰可见：凌晨、周末和节假日轮班块的份额；同时以实验室时钟和机构自身时钟显示的席位；无法出行的机构可参与的席位池；以及按余额确定的预订顺序。在一个由48家机构和256名符合资格的成员组成的合成合作组中，该方法通过两轮分配，在每个周期的72个席位中分配70至72个，平均排序满意度为84至95%，且没有违反任何规则；运行一年后，所有拥有四名或更多成员的机构与其累计份额的偏差均在13%以内，而对于最小的机构，由于算术原因一个轮班块便会使其超出份额，则通过两两合并使其也进入同一范围。该方法的陈述足够完整，可以重新实现；已有参考实现，并可依据许可提供给合作组。该方法可直接适用于任何在机构配额下以轮班块方式为设施配备人员的科学合作组。

Proposing, never assigning: fair allocation of shifts in experimental particle physics collaborations from ranked wishes under institutional quotas

Experimental particle physics collaborations staff the operation of their detectors with shifts, taken in the control room or remotely by members drawn from dozens of institutions. The established practice for filling them is a credit system with self sign-up: institutions owe a quota of credit, and people take the seats they want when booking opens. This paper describes a method in which seats are instead allocated from ranked wishes under institutional quotas, the result is issued as offers that each person accepts or declines, and an independent checker recomputes every rule before anything is published: the platform proposes, never assigns, and proves that it kept the rules. The credit and quota model is inherited from current practice. Around that core the paper adds a quota rule that keeps indivisible blocks fair to institutions whose share of a period is smaller than one block, and four rules that make fairness visible beyond the count: shares of owl, weekend and holiday blocks, seats shown in the laboratory's clock and the institution's own, reachable pools for institutions that cannot travel, and booking order by balance. On a synthetic collaboration of 48 institutions and 256 eligible members, the method allocates 70 to 72 of 72 seats per period in two rounds, with a mean rank satisfaction of 84 to 95% and no rule broken; after a year of running every institution of four members or more is within 13% of its cumulative share, and the smallest institutions, which one block overshoots by arithmetic, are brought within the same range by pooling in pairs. The method is stated completely enough to be reimplemented; a reference implementation exists and is available to collaborations under license. It applies as it stands to any scientific collaboration that staffs a facility in blocks of shifts under institutional quotas.

Probing the X ( 3700 ) through two-photon D D ¯ production in ultraperipheral collisions and at the EIC

We investigate near-threshold production of the scalar D D ¯ molecular candidate X ( 3700 ) through two-photon fusion in ultraperipheral collisions and at the EIC. The charged and neutral production amplitudes are dressed with a three-channel Bethe-Salpeter interaction in the { D + D − , D 0 D ¯ 0 , D s + D s − } basis. Including the virtual D s D ¯ s channel shifts the bound-state pole from 3.7162 to 3.7205 GeV while preserving the characteristic continuum pattern: final-state interactions enhance the charged channel and suppress the neutral one. Over 3.70 ≤ W ≤ 3.85 GeV , the central calculation gives a charged-channel enhancement of order 13 and a neutral-channel ratio of about 0.34 in both collision systems. Variations of the main model inputs produce sizeable, nonstatistical sensitivity ranges without changing this qualitative behavior. The most direct normalization-independent observable is the integrated charged-to-neutral ratio, which changes from about 0.043 at bare level to 1.70 after final-state interactions.

神经场热层析成像：用于无损评估的可微物理框架

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

刚性抛物型偏微分方程（PDEs）的反问题，例如逆热传导问题（IHCP），具有严重的不适定性：正向映射会在高频内部结构到达边界之前将其迅速衰减。软约束物理信息神经网络（PINNs）将 PDE 嵌入为残差惩罚；在其标准双网络反演表述中，这种网络使扩散率与表面数据解耦，而所评估的变体虽然拟合了边界测量结果，却使内部场基本未受影响。我们提出神经场热层析成像（NeFTY），这是一种用于无标签三维逆热传导的硬约束神经场框架。NeFTY 将未知扩散率表示为连续的基于坐标的神经网络，并在每个优化步骤中使候选场通过采用调和平均界面通量的可微隐式 Euler 热求解器，从而使模拟温度在求解器容差范围内满足离散化热方程。伴随梯度以求解器级别的内存成本将表面重建误差反向传播至网络权重，使单个 GPU 上的测试时反演切实可行。在各项合成 3D 基准测试中，对于无标签体积恢复，NeFTY 的表现优于软约束 PINN 变体、体素网格基线以及通过同一求解器进行的经典 L-BFGS 体素反演；它还可迁移至真实热成像数据，在缺陷分割和深度估计两方面均优于经典信号处理基线。更多详情见 https://cab-lab-princeton.github.io/nefty/

Neural Field Thermal Tomography: A Differentiable Physics Framework for Non-Destructive Evaluation

Inverse problems for stiff parabolic partial differential equations (PDEs), such as the inverse heat conduction problem (IHCP), are severely ill-posed: the forward map rapidly damps high-frequency interior structure before it reaches the boundary. Soft-constrained physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), which embed the PDE as a residual penalty, decouple the diffusivity from the surface data in their standard two-network inverse formulation, and the evaluated variants fit boundary measurements while leaving the interior field essentially untouched. We propose Neural Field Thermal Tomography (NeFTY), a hard-constrained neural field framework for label-free three-dimensional inverse heat conduction. NeFTY represents the unknown diffusivity as a continuous coordinate-based neural network, and at every optimization step passes the candidate field through a differentiable implicit-Euler heat solver with harmonic-mean interface flux, so that the simulated temperature satisfies the discretized heat equation up to the solver tolerance. Adjoint gradients propagate the surface reconstruction error back to the network weights at solver-level memory cost, making test-time inversion tractable on a single GPU. Across synthetic 3D benchmarks, NeFTY outperforms soft-constrained PINN variants, a voxel-grid baseline, and classical L-BFGS voxel inversion through the same solver on label-free volumetric recovery, and it transfers to real thermography data, surpassing classical signal-processing baselines in both defect segmentation and depth estimation. Additional details at https://cab-lab-princeton.github.io/nefty/

Nanohertz Loss in a Pendulum for Macroscopic Entanglement under Structural Damping

Pendulums are attractive for macroscopic quantum control because gravitational dissipation dilution lowers the modal loss, while the $1/f$ force noise of structural damping allows nearly lossless trapping to suppress the noise at an upshifted resonance. The same $1/f$ spectrum, however, produces a low-frequency tail that penalizes entanglement. To overcome this penalty, we realize a 7-mg pendulum suspended by a stepped fused-silica fiber. Ring-down measurements give an energy-decay rate $\Gamma/2\pi=361(39)$ nHz at the bare resonance $\omega_0/2\pi=2.63$ Hz. The reduction in $\omega_0\Gamma$ relative to the previous uniform suspension more than offsets the penalty. Although both suspensions support entanglement in the error-free model, only the stepped one allows robust verification in the multimode model with experimental uncertainties.

利用 Compton 散射约束对分段式 HPGe $\gamma$ 射线跟踪阵列进行无标签自校准

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

AGATA 等分段式 HPGe $\gamma$ 射线跟踪阵列中的脉冲形状分析依赖已知相互作用位置的参考信号，这些信号的测量成本高昂，且通过计算获得时仅为近似值。在包含 51 个 AGATA 晶体的模拟中，我们表明，可以在没有位置标签的情况下从脉冲中学习相互作用位置。对于一个在一个分段中发生 Compton 散射并在另一个分段中被吸收的 \Co\ 光子，散射角既可由沉积能量得出，也可由源的位置和两次相互作用的位置得出。所有晶体共享的一个神经网络为每个触发分段预测一个位置，并且仅通过使这两个角一致来进行训练；在训练期间，会修正两次相互作用的未测量次序以及相互作用对的选择。这些脉冲由 AGATAGeFEM 基数据合成，涉及从 $4\times10^{7}$ 次模拟衰变中选出的 $2.79\times10^{6}$ 个触发分段对，其能量之和等于一条 \Co\ 谱线；脉冲中包含噪声、串扰、触发时间抖动以及晶体之间的差异。对于留出相互作用对中的命中，三维位置误差的平均值从采用分段中心时的 10.4~mm 降至 1.65~mm，每个坐标上的 FWHM 为 1.0--1.3~mm；相比之下，同一网络在模拟位置上训练时为 1.07~mm。该约束对沿晶体轴线的位置约束较弱，并且在小散射角下会将低能量的第一次相互作用拉向源，这就是排除低于 200~keV 的能量沉积的原因。即使使用模拟位置，这两个角也存在差异，主要原因是包含多次相互作用的一个分段由单一位置和能量描述，而非 Doppler 展宽。

查看原文标题与摘要

Label-free self-calibration of a segmented HPGe $\gamma$-ray tracking array from the Compton scattering constraint

Pulse-shape analysis in segmented HPGe $\gamma$-ray tracking arrays such as AGATA relies on reference signals for known interaction positions, which are costly to measure and only approximate when calculated. In a simulation of 51 AGATA crystals, we show that the interaction positions can be learned from the pulses without position labels. For a \Co\ photon that is Compton scattered in one segment and absorbed in another, the scattering angle follows both from the deposited energies and from the positions of the source and of the two interactions. A neural network shared by all crystals predicts one position per fired segment and is trained only to make these two angles agree; the unmeasured order of the two interactions and the selection of the pairs are revised during training. The pulses, synthesised from AGATAGeFEM bases for $2.79\times10^{6}$ pairs of fired segments whose energies add up to a \Co\ line, selected from $4\times10^{7}$ simulated decays, include noise, cross-talk, trigger-time jitter and differences between crystals. For the hits of held-out pairs, the mean three-dimensional position error falls from 10.4~mm with the segment centres to 1.65~mm, with a FWHM of 1.0--1.3~mm in each coordinate, compared with 1.07~mm for the same network trained on the simulated positions. The constraint determines the positions along the crystal axes only weakly, and at small scattering angles it pulls low-energy first interactions towards the source, which is why deposits below 200~keV are excluded. Even with the simulated positions, the two angles differ, mainly because a segment with several interactions is described by a single position and energy rather than because of Doppler broadening.

In-pixel lock-in readout for wide-field nitrogen-vacancy radio-frequency spectrum analysis

The growing use of radio-frequency (RF) technologies requires compact, room-temperature sensors that can analyze a wide RF band in real time. Here we present a quantum RF spectrum analyzer based on the gradient-encoded electron spin resonance of a nitrogen-vacancy (NV) ensemble in diamond and read out with a $512\times542$-pixel lock-in camera that demodulates the signal in each pixel. A Novel Multi-Target Approach for simultaneous enhancement of brightness, intensity, and bi-spectral performance of cold neutron sources

Increasing the brightness of cold neutron beams is a central objective in modern cold neutron source development, since it largely governs the sample intensity for high-resolution neutron instruments. Raising accelerator beam power is constrained by the target heat-removal limit of 0.5-1 kW/cm2, and increasing the moderator length beyond about 20-30 cm becomes inefficient owing to the finite mean free path of cold neutrons. We propose reversing the conventional design logic: rather than tailoring the cryogenic moderator to a fixed target-premoderator source, we tailor the thermal-neutron source geometry to a high-performance moderator configuration. The concept is realized as a staircase moderator assembly of several shorter compressed moderators, each with its own target-premoderator unit, which distributes neutron production among multiple units and thus enables higher accelerator powers while preserving the brightness advantages of compressed moderators. This approach can provide additional brightness and intensity gains relative to a single low-dimensional or conventional voluminous moderator at the desired transverse beam size and may naturally generate bi-spectral neutron beams, thereby extending the dynamic range of neutron instruments.

A Camera-Based Single-Volume, Monolithic Compton Camera

A Compton camera measures two events in a detector from individual gamma-rays to reconstruct the radiation source. Conventional scintillator-based Compton cameras consist of two separate scintillator volumes or a single segmented scintillator, resulting in drawbacks regarding detector bulkiness and efficiency. Their scintillator readout techniques struggle to resolve two nearby events in one monolithic scintillator. Single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) cameras have recently been proposed for measuring scintillation events in various configurations. SPAD array sensors enable high-resolution photon-counting acquisition at high rates that is easily scalable to millions of pixels. Prior work has explored different optics and measurement arrangements as well as demonstrated measurement of single interaction locations. In this work, we extend these capabilities to demonstrate a practical radiation imaging device in the form of a Compton camera consisting of a SPAD camera and one compact, monolithic scintillator. Our Compton camera prototype successfully reconstructs a gamma-ray source over multiple locations that span a 4$\pi$ steradian field of view. Our approach is able to resolve two simultaneous events in one monolithic scintillator and enables Compton camera devices with compact, monolithic scintillator volumes for enhanced portability.

lawless: Normalization, Scaling, and Merging of Laue Data in the AIMLESS Framework

lawless, a new program for wavelength normalization, scaling, and merging of Laue data from both X-rays and neutrons, is presented. XTCP 是 Transmission Control Protocol (TCP) 在 field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) 上用于高数据率探测器读出的开源实现。其目标是实现接近 TCP 有效载荷极限的持续单连接吞吐量。在同一块 Kintex UltraScale+ KU5P 卡和 Linux 主机上分别进行的 10 Gigabit Ethernet 传输中，XTCP 和 SiTCP-XG 各自向主机提供了 9.49 Gbit/s 的应用层吞吐量中位数。向 FPGA 传输时，XTCP 的中位数为 9.49 Gbit/s，而受测 SiTCP-XG 配置的中位数为 5.76 Gbit/s。XTCP 原型使用了更多查找表和片上存储器。在跨越多次序列号回绕的 96 GiB 回显测试中，记录确认首次越过恢复边界的时刻，使原先的有符号比较会阻止的四次快速恢复得以实现。在一个 25 Gigabit Ethernet 比特流上，将 IP 最大传输单元从 1500 bytes 增大到 9000 bytes，使向主机的单向吞吐量中位数从 23.30 提高到 24.70 Gbit/s，并使向 FPGA 的单向吞吐量中位数从 22.09 提高到 24.08 Gbit/s。在 9000 bytes 时，这些速率分别达到 TCP 有效载荷极限的 99.7% 和 97.1%。在另一次回显测试中，主机验证了一个巨型帧原型通过单一连接在 3595 s 内返回的全部 8.77 TB 数据，每个方向的平均速率为 19.51 Gbit/s。

XTCP: an open-source FPGA TCP endpoint for readout over 10 and 25 Gigabit Ethernet

XTCP is an open-source implementation of the Transmission Control Protocol (TCP) on field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) for high-rate detector readout. It targets sustained single-connection throughput near the TCP payload limit. In separate 10 Gigabit Ethernet transfers on the same Kintex UltraScale+ KU5P card and Linux host, XTCP and SiTCP-XG each delivered a median application-level throughput of 9.49 Gbit/s towards the host. Towards the FPGA, the medians were 9.49 Gbit/s for XTCP and 5.76 Gbit/s for the tested SiTCP-XG configuration. The XTCP prototype used more lookup tables and on-chip memory. In a 96 GiB echo test spanning repeated sequence-number wraps, recording when an acknowledgement first crossed the recovery boundary allowed four fast recoveries that the earlier signed comparison would have blocked. On one 25 Gigabit Ethernet bitstream, increasing the IP maximum transmission unit from 1500 to 9000 bytes raised median one-way throughput from 23.30 to 24.70 Gbit/s towards the host and from 22.09 to 24.08 Gbit/s towards the FPGA. At 9000 bytes these rates reached 99.7% and 97.1% of the TCP payload limit, respectively. In a separate echo test, the host verified all 8.77 TB returned by a jumbo-frame prototype over one connection in 3595 s, averaging 19.51 Gbit/s per direction.

用于存在数据缺口的 LISA 信号的小波域流匹配后验估计

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) 数据流将包含计划内和计划外的缺口，这些缺口会破坏仪器噪声的平稳性，并使标准频域似然失效。摊销式 simulation-based inference (SBI) 避免了显式似然求值，但将其应用于长达数月的观测，需要在保留源参数信息的同时压缩高维、存在缺口的时间序列。我们开发了一套端到端摊销式后验估计流程：将存在缺口的数据映射为经噪声标度化的 Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) 小波系数，并由卷积-Transformer 网络进行概括；该网络与 flow-matching (FM) 后验估计器联合训练，并可选择进行重要性采样校正。对于观测了 3 months 的类 LISA 信号，摊销式 FM 后验在模拟观测上得到了良好校准，而这些观测的波形和缺口在训练中均未出现。FM 后验与基准——parallel-tempered Markov chain Monte Carlo (PTMCMC) 得到的后验——高度吻合，尽管其离散程度略大。重要性采样能够高效地对 FM 后验重新加权，以适度的额外成本实现与基准的高度吻合。

Wavelet-domain flow-matching posterior estimation for LISA signals with data gaps

The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) data stream will contain scheduled and unscheduled gaps, which break the stationarity of the instrumental noise and invalidate the standard frequency-domain likelihood. Amortized simulation-based inference (SBI) avoids explicit likelihood evaluations, but its application to months-long observations requires compressing high-dimensional, gapped time series while preserving information about source parameters. We develop an end-to-end amortized posterior estimation pipeline: the gapped data are mapped to noise-scaled Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) wavelet coefficients and summarized by a convolutional-Transformer network, trained jointly with a flow-matching (FM) posterior estimator, with optional importance-sampling correction. For a LISA-like signal observed for 3 months, the amortized FM posteriors are well calibrated on simulated observations whose waveforms and gaps were not seen in training. The FM posteriors agree closely with the benchmark, the posteriors from parallel-tempered Markov chain Monte Carlo (PTMCMC), although they exhibit moderately greater dispersion. Importance sampling efficiently reweights the FM posterior, achieving close agreement with the benchmark at modest additional cost.

次领头阶核子的夸克和胶子引力形状因子

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们在 $\alpha_s$ 次领头阶的三点 QCD 求和规则中，计算了核子的夸克和胶子引力形状因子 $A$、$J$ 和 $D$；对于夸克和胶子能动量张量 (EMT)，在 $\overline{\rm MS}$ 方案中按相同阶数处理。核子 EMT 也检验了该方法，表明求和规则在哪里可靠以及为何可靠。$D$ 和迹形状因子 $\bar c$ 的受保护估计量需要反对称 Borel 矩，因为仅使用相等 Borel 质量时没有任何估计量受到保护。$\mathcal{O}(\alpha_s)$ 修正使 $A_{\rm tot}$ 降低 $32$--$40\%$，使 $J_{\rm tot}$ 降低 $22$--$27\%$，并使 $|D_{\rm tot}|$ 降低 $21$--$27\%$，这是因为两点函数中的微扰部分占比大得多。总形状因子在 $1\le Q^2\le2.5$~GeV$^2$ 范围内与格点 QCD 一致。在 $Q^2=1.5$~GeV$^2$ 时，$A_{\rm tot}=0.337\pm0.113$、$J_{\rm tot}=0.206\pm0.100$ 且 $D_{\rm tot}=-0.206\pm0.121$。$D$ 是最可靠的结果：其关联函数主要是微扰性的，且其夸克和胶子部分与格点结果一致。在 $A$ 和 $J$ 中，一个不随 $Q^2$ 下降的局域胶子凝聚项最大，而且它们的胶子占比远低于格点结果，这可能是因为在此阶胶子 EMT 仅通过胶子交换和凝聚与其耦合。可行区域仅存在于 $\beta=1$ 附近，并且在 Borel 窗口内无法分离跃迁的常数部分。各个守恒恒等式在一点处偏离两个标准差，这与 $g_{\mu\nu}$ 结构中缺少一个有限项相符，因此 $\bar c$ 以包含凝聚项的领头阶结果给出。

Quark and gluon gravitational form factors of the nucleon at next-to-leading order

We compute the quark and gluon gravitational form factors $A$, $J$ and $D$ of the nucleon in three-point QCD sum rules at next-to-leading order in $\alpha_s$ for the quark and gluon energy-momentum tensors (EMT), treating both at the same order in the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme. The nucleon EMT also tests the method, showing where the sum rules are reliable and why. Protected estimators of $D$ and of the trace form factor $\bar c$ require antisymmetric Borel moments, since none with equal Borel masses alone is protected. The $\mathcal{O}(\alpha_s)$ corrections reduce $A_{\rm tot}$ by $32$--$40\%$, $J_{\rm tot}$ by $22$--$27\%$ and $|D_{\rm tot}|$ by $21$--$27\%$, since the perturbative share of the two-point function is much larger. The total form factors agree with lattice QCD for $1\le Q^2\le2.5$~GeV$^2$. At $Q^2=1.5$~GeV$^2$, $A_{\rm tot}=0.337\pm0.113$, $J_{\rm tot}=0.206\pm0.100$ and $D_{\rm tot}=-0.206\pm0.121$. $D$ is the most reliable result: its correlator is mostly perturbative, and its quark and gluon parts agree with the lattice. In $A$ and $J$, a local gluon-condensate term that does not fall with $Q^2$ is the largest, and their gluon shares are well below the lattice, possibly because the gluon EMT couples at this order only through gluon exchange and the condensate. A working region exists only near $\beta=1$, and the constant part of transitions cannot be separated within the Borel windows. The individual conservation identities deviate at one point by two standard deviations, consistent with a missing finite term in the $g_{\mu\nu}$ structure, and $\bar c$ is therefore given at leading order with condensates.

具有手征不平衡的磁化夸克物质中的中性和带电π介子模式

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们使用平均场近似和单通道随机相位近似（RPA），研究了存在均匀磁场和给定手征化学势$\mu_5$时，有限温度下双味Nambu--Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型中的中性和带电π介子关联。π介子质量由相应逆传播子实部所选取的正能量零点定义。我们证明，在外部空间动量为零时，中性通道中的完整Dirac迹消除了混合分支贡献，从而排除了有限能量Landau割。相比之下，带电通道允许非对角Landau能级跃迁，并同时呈现对产生和热散射贡献。随着$\mu_5$增大，中性π介子质量在低温下仅有微弱变化，而质量的快速下降向更高温度移动。在所选解区段上，带电π介子质量随$\mu_5$增大而减小。这种行为可能有助于解释格点QCD结果。在零温和固定$\mu_5$下，增大磁场会降低中性π介子质量，但会提高带电π介子质量。

查看原文标题与摘要

Neutral and Charged Pion Modes in Magnetized Quark Matter with Chiral Imbalance

We investigate neutral and charged pion correlations in a two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model at finite temperature in the presence of a uniform magnetic field and a prescribed chiral chemical potential $\mu_5$, using the mean-field approximation and the single-channel random phase approximation (RPA). The pion masses are defined by the selected positive-energy zeros of the real parts of the corresponding inverse propagators. We demonstrate that, at zero external spatial momentum, the complete Dirac trace in the neutral channel eliminates mixed-branch contributions, thereby precluding a finite-energy Landau cut. In contrast, the charged channel allows non-diagonal Landau-level transitions and exhibits both pair-production and thermal-scattering contributions. With increasing $\mu_5$, the neutral-pion mass exhibits only a weak variation at low temperature, while the rapid decrease of the mass shifts toward higher temperatures. The charged-pion mass decreases with increasing $\mu_5$ over the selected solution segments. This behavior may help account for the lattice QCD results. At zero temperature and fixed $\mu_5$, increasing the magnetic field lowers the neutral-pion mass but raises the charged-pion mass.

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

Machine-learning-assisted photon pairing for neutral-pion reconstruction at future Higgs factories

Neutral-pion ($\pi^0$) reconstruction is important for precision measurements at high-energy colliders, particularly in flavor, $\tau$, and Higgs physics. We study $\pi^0$ candidate selection in four benchmark physics processes at electron--positron colliders, comparing an optimized diphoton mass-window (MW) baseline with four machine-learning methods: a boosted decision tree (BDT), a multilayer perceptron (MLP), ParticleNet (PN), and Particle Transformer (ParT). Performance is evaluated using the product of selection efficiency and purity. At the reference ECAL energy resolution, all four machine-learning methods outperform MW. PN and ParT achieve relative improvements of 53%--95% and 60%--128%, respectively, primarily through better rejection of incorrectly matched photon pairs. Their performance advantage is maintained across the investigated ECAL energy resolutions. Training-sample-size scans reveal two periods of rapid performance improvement separated by slower growth in most PN and ParT learning curves. The improved candidate selection provides a starting point for further studies of $\rho$ and hadronic $\tau$ reconstruction.

级联GEM结构中的离子反馈抑制与吸收动力学

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

离子反馈（IBF）是气体电子倍增器（GEM）探测器中长期存在的一项挑战：漂移回灵敏体积的正离子会扭曲漂移场，并降低空间分辨率和能量分辨率。我们使用ANSYS和Garfield$^{++}$，研究了采用Ar/CO$_2$（70:30）气体混合物的单层至五层GEM探测器的离子吸收特性。我们建立了一种逐层分辨的形式体系，用以区分有条件的箔层级吸收与探测器层级吸收度量，后者对每个放大阶段产生的离子进行正确加权。对于传统双锥形几何结构，级联箔仅使总离子吸收比例得到适度提高，从单层GEM的47.83\%提高到五层GEM的59.06\%。在相同的层数范围内，用单锥形轮廓取代双锥形孔可将吸收比例提高到75\%-87\%，并使IBF/Gain比降至原来的二分之一至三分之一；这一改进超过了增加四层额外双锥形箔所带来的增益。作为在更现实条件下的次要测试，三层GEM探测器的第二个传输间隙长度从1.2变到3.5~mm。增益呈现非单调依赖关系，在约2.0-2.5~mm处达到最大值，而IBF/Gain比随间隙长度增加而单调降低；单锥形几何结构在整个范围内均保持其两倍优势。这些发现表明，级联GEM结构中的离子抑制不仅受箔层数量支配，更主要取决于每层箔的局部离子收集特性，并且单锥形孔是改善高计数率探测器应用性能的一条有前景的途径。

Ion Backflow Suppression and Absorption Dynamics in Cascaded GEM Structures

Ion backflow (IBF) is a persistent challenge in Gas Electron Multiplier (GEM) detectors: positive ions drifting back into the sensitive volume distort the drift field and degrade both spatial and energy resolution. We investigate the ion-absorption characteristics of single- to quintuple-GEM detectors using ANSYS and Garfield$^{++}$ for an Ar/CO$_2$ (70:30) gas mixture. A layer-resolved formalism is developed that distinguishes conditional foil-level absorption from a detector-level absorption metric that correctly weights ion production at every amplification stage. For the conventional biconical geometry, cascading foils improves the overall ion-absorption fraction only moderately, from 47.83\% for a single GEM to 59.06\% for a quintuple GEM. Replacing the biconical holes with a single-conical profile raises the absorption fraction to 75\%-87\% across the same range of multiplicities and reduces the IBF/Gain ratio by a factor of two to three an improvement that exceeds what is gained by adding four additional biconical foils. As a secondary test under more realistic conditions, the second transfer-gap length of a triple-GEM detector is varied from 1.2 to 3.5~mm. The gain shows a non-monotonic dependence with a maximum near 2.0-2.5~mm, while the IBF/Gain ratio decreases monotonically with increasing gap length; the single-conical geometry retains its factor-of-two advantage throughout. These findings establish that ion suppression in cascaded GEM structures is governed not only by the number of foils but primarily by the local ion-collection properties of each foil, and that single-conical holes represent a promising route to improved performance in high-rate detector applications.

用于星间激光链路的类HEP 1550 nm宽视场像素化光接收机：面向地球、月球和地月场景的基于模拟的概念验证

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我提出了一种用于捕获星间激光链路的1550 nm宽视场穹顶式光接收机（视场大于一个半球），并通过模拟对其进行了评估。该探测器是一种约1 Mpixel的InGaAs像素混合探测器，采用事件驱动的带时间戳读出，其灵感来自高能物理的像素探测器。通过滑动像素块中m个击中的符合来探测脉冲信标，并通过聚类和质心计算估计到达角（AoA），随后将其传递给窄视场数据通道。在550 km地球星座中，采用5 mrad信标和10 mm孔径时，探测耗时为12 $\mu$s至2 ms（使用光学系统所允许的6-9 mm时最长为116 ms），全帧误警率低于0.01/s，AoA误差为0.13-0.23 mrad。冷却至约233 K时，对于每年最高约$2\times10^{9}$ p/cm$^2$的注量，使用寿命可超过5年。还研究了该接收机在月球周围及地月链路上的应用，其中它仅能在高轨道之间工作。按各阶段与已发表的基于扫描的捕获方法比较，搜索阶段（使用10 mm时为3-18 ms）缩短至原来的约$10^{-2}$至$10^{-5}$；在端到端情况下，计入假定的指向和环路闭合时间后，耗时为0.16-1.5 s，与采用经校准开环指向的终端相当，但无需接收机具备这种指向能力；合作方的信标仍必须以约1.25 mrad的精度（5 mrad信标）指向该接收机。所有数值均为使用理想化光学系统和目前尚不存在的1 Mpixel探测器得到的模拟结果；没有任何数值是测量结果。

HEP-like 1550 nm wide-field pixelated optical receiver for inter-satellite laser links: a simulation-based proof of concept for Earth, lunar and Earth-Moon scenarios

I propose and evaluate by simulation a wide-field dome optical receiver (a field of view larger than a hemisphere) at 1550 nm for the acquisition of inter-satellite laser links. The detector is an InGaAs pixel hybrid of about 1 Mpixel with event-driven, time-stamped readout, inspired by the pixel detectors of high-energy physics. A pulsed beacon is detected by the coincidence of m hits in a sliding block of pixels, and the angle of arrival (AoA) is estimated by clustering and centroiding and handed to a narrow-field data channel. In a 550 km Earth constellation, with a 5 mrad beacon and a 10 mm aperture, detection takes 12 $\mu$s to 2 ms (up to 116 ms with the 6-9 mm that the optics allow), with a whole-frame false-alarm rate below 0.01/s, and the AoA error is 0.13-0.23 mrad. Cooling to about 233 K gives a service life above 5 years for fluences up to about $2\times10^{9}$ p/cm$^2$ per year. The receiver is also studied around the Moon and on the Earth-Moon link, where it works only between high orbits. Compared phase by phase with published scan-based acquisition, the search phase (3-18 ms with 10 mm) is shorter by a factor of about $10^{2}$ to $10^{5}$; end to end, with assumed pointing and loop-closure times, it takes 0.16-1.5 s, comparable to a terminal with calibrated open-loop pointing but without requiring that pointing at the receiver; the partner's beacon must still be pointed at it to about 1.25 mrad (5 mrad beacon). All values are simulation results, obtained with idealised optics and a 1 Mpixel detector that does not exist today; none is a measurement.

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

Finite Temperature Isoscalar Instabilities and Moat Regimes in Relativistic Nuclear Matter

We study the finite-temperature isoscalar collective response of symmetric nuclear matter in three relativistic mean-field descriptions: the linear Walecka model, its nonlinear extension, and the quark-meson coupling (QMC) model. The Walecka models are formulated in terms of nucle- onic degrees of freedom, whereas QMC incorporates quark degrees of freedom confined inside the nucleon. These models differ in their treatment of scalar interactions at finite density, affecting instability boundaries. Focusing on the coupled scalar-vector channel, we track how the boundaries of homogeneous instabilities evolve with temperature. In all three models, we find a nonmonotonic momentum dependence of the static response, characterized by a minimum at finite momentum, a moat regime that selects a preferred length scale for density fluctuations. Our results show that this moat structure is robust against thermal effects across these relativistic nuclear matter models, providing motivation for future studies of inhomogeneous phases in hot and dense astrophysical matter.

Efficient phase-only wavefront shaping at 250 kHz for multimode fiber endomicroscopy

Wavefront shaping through multimode fibers (MMFs) enables diffraction-limited fluorescence imaging with hair-thin probes, at a speed and light budget bounded by the wavefront modulator. Existing modulators provide at most two of three key characteristics: high refresh rate, (high) modal dimensionality, and light efficiency. Here, we demonstrate wavefront control through an MMF using a tailored phase-only microelectromechanical modulator that combines all three, with 8,192 elements arranged in a 32 x 256 array, 7-bit phase control, and a 250 kHz full-frame refresh rate. Direct on-axis phase modulation delivers 70+/-5% of the incident light into the modulated field and eliminates both off-axis encoding optical losses and hologram computation. The generated foci at the distal end of the fiber remain isotropic with high fidelity. We use singular-value decomposition analysis of the measured transmission to identify the effective degrees of freedom of our system. By scanning the focus at multiple depths, we demonstrate volumetric fluorescence imaging. These results open a new operating regime for adaptive in vivo multimode-fiber endoscopy.

Development of a Hybrid Scintillator Strip-Drift Tube Muon Detector for the FCC-ee

The Future Circular Collider (FCC-ee) is a proposed high-luminosity $e^+e^-$ collider for precision measurements at the electroweak, Higgs, and top-quark energy scales. A high-performance, cost-effective muon system is essential for muon identification, triggering, momentum measurement, and long-lived particle detection. We present the R&D of a hybrid muon detector concept combining fast extruded plastic scintillator strips with precision drift tubes. The scintillator strips were produced at Fermilab, while detector prototypes with SiPM readout and pressurized drift tubes were designed, constructed, and integrated at the University of Michigan. The hybrid detector was tested at the CERN test beam facility in 2025 and 2026. The scintillator strips achieved a position resolution of 1.6 mm and a time resolution of approximately 350 ps, while the drift tubes provided a single-hit spatial resolution better than 100 $\mu$m. These results demonstrate the feasibility of a high-performance hybrid muon spectrometer for the FCC-ee.

我们介绍了一种定制的顶部装载式探杆，无需更换现有氦流变温插入件的低温或磁场基础设施，即可为其增加振动样品磁强测量功能。该模块化仪器结合了可拆卸密封驱动头、工作频率接近 44 Hz 的音圈谐振器、振动峰值振幅的连续监测、电动轴向对中装置以及紧凑的一阶拾取线圈梯度计。对于数种振动振幅，数值磁通链计算结果在乘以共同因子 1.08 后，复现了所测轴向响应的位置和形状。对 Ni 参考样品的测量证明，该仪器可低至 5 K 工作，并得到介于 24.0 V s A$^{-1}$ m$^{-3}$ 和 31.4 V s A$^{-1}$ m$^{-3}$ 之间的有效几何因子。在固定磁场下，观测到的电压离散对应于 16.5 nA m$^{2}$ 至 27.8 nA m$^{2}$ 的磁矩等效单标准差重复性。对于在 250 K 下测量的 31.7 nm Ni 薄膜，在三种振动振幅下获得的磁化曲线经振幅校正后大致重合，并在扣除线性高场背景后得到约 409 kA m$^{-1}$ 的饱和磁化强度。结果证明了一种实用的改装方法，可为具有类似孔径和法兰约束的 VTI 增加块体和薄膜磁强测量功能。

Design and performance of a top-loading vibrating-sample magnetometer for a helium-flow variable-temperature insert

We present a custom-built top-loading probe that adds vibrating-sample magnetometry to an existing helium-flow variable-temperature insert without replacing its cryogenic or magnetic-field infrastructure. The modular instrument combines a detachable sealed drive head, a voice-coil resonator operating near 44 Hz, continuous monitoring of the peak vibration amplitude, motorized axial centering, and a compact first-order pickup-coil gradiometer. A numerical flux-linkage calculation reproduces the position and shape of the measured axial response for several vibration amplitudes after multiplication by a common factor of 1.08. Measurements on Ni reference samples demonstrate operation down to 5 K and yield effective geometrical factors between 24.0 V s A$^{-1}$ m$^{-3}$ and 31.4 V s A$^{-1}$ m$^{-3}$. At fixed field, the observed voltage scatter corresponds to moment-equivalent one-standard-deviation repeatabilities of 16.5 nA m$^{2}$ to 27.8 nA m$^{2}$. For a 31.7 nm Ni film measured at 250 K, magnetization curves obtained at three vibration amplitudes approximately collapse after amplitude correction and yield a saturation magnetization of approximately 409 kA m$^{-1}$ after subtraction of the linear high-field background. The results demonstrate a practical retrofit approach for adding bulk and thin-film magnetometry to VTIs with comparable bore and flange constraints.

Data handling, production, and simulation in the Muon g-2 Experiment at Fermilab

This paper details the computing strategies of the Muon g-2 Experiment at Fermilab, which measured the muon's anomalous magnetic moment with a precision of 127 ppb by processing over 10 petabytes of detector and simulation data. This paper discusses the computational and operational challenges of data production, data handling, and data analysis of such large datasets. These challenges were overcome by using Fermilab-developed software infrastructure and production tools, making efficient use of resources, and improving the collaboration-developed reconstruction workflow. By overcoming these challenges, the experiment improved the data throughput capacity and reduced the analysis turnaround time by an order of magnitude over the course of its lifetime.

Complete virtual unwrapping and reading of a rolled Herculaneum papyrus

The carbonized papyrus scrolls from Herculaneum preserve the only large-scale library to survive from classical antiquity. Many scrolls have remained unread because physical unrolling risks irreversible damage. Non-invasive imaging offers a route to their texts, yet previous work on sealed Herculaneum scrolls has recovered only localized readings. Here, by combining high-resolution X-ray computed microtomography ($\mu$CT) data acquired on the BM18 beamline at the ESRF with machine-learning-assisted virtual unwrapping, we study three still-closed scrolls. For PHerc. 1667, a remnant of earlier attempts at physical unrolling, we achieve the complete unwrapping and reading of the preserved writing surface, making it the first Herculaneum papyrus read completely by virtual unwrapping. The text was transcribed and cross-verified by nine papyrologists. In PHerc. Paris. 4, the source of the Vesuvius Challenge 2023 Grand Prize text, the high-resolution scan makes ink directly visible in the tomographic volume, allowing 3D ink segmentation and comparison of recovered text with visible deposits. In PHerc. 139, we recover title and author-attribution evidence identifying the scroll as Philodemus, On Gods, Book 8. These results establish a basis for the ongoing, systematic recovery and scholarly study of texts preserved within the still-closed Herculaneum library.

Characterization and Monte Carlo Simulation of Wavelength-Shifting Absorption Length in Plastic Optical Fibers

We report an experimental characterization of the wavelength-shifting (WLS) absorption length of the EJ-160I fiber core material from Eljen Technology. Fiber-core samples of different thicknesses were employed, and the transmitted intensity of incident LED light was measured to extract the WLS absorption length at 405.7 nm. The resulting value, (0.823 +/- 0.038) mm, was used to normalize the WLS absorption length spectrum derived from the absorption spectrum of the core material. An independent measurement at 392.5 nm yielded a WLS absorption length of (1.404 +/- 0.074) mm, differing from the value predicted by the reconstructed WLS absorption length spectrum by 3.16%, corresponding to 0.43 sigma. The resulting spectrum was implemented as an optical input in a Geant4-based optical Monte Carlo simulation of the EJ-160I fiber to examine the role of the WLS absorption length in light output, light attenuation, and the emission spectrum.

CHARMS250V1: A Cryogenic Front-End ASIC for Low-Noise Readout of Charge or Light Signals in Noble Liquid Detectors

This work presents the design and measurement results of CHARMS250V1, a next-generation cryogenic front-end application specific integrated circuit (ASIC) developed using a 65 nm CMOS process. It is designed for low-noise readout of charge or light signals generated in noble liquid detectors. CHARMS250V1 is a single channel prototype whose design in the future will be integrated within a 16-channel ASIC. It comprises pre-amplification and pulse shaping stages that provide programmable charge gain between 4.3 mV/fC and 25 mV/fC, peaking time programmable between 250 ns and 2 us, and programmable baseline of either 200 mV or 900 mV for processing of unipolar and bipolar signals, respectively.

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

Ballistic propagation-mediated super-resolution light-element imaging: in-situ tracking dynamic hydrogen migration

Transmission electrons - with few-picometer wavelengths - can theoretically resolve all atoms in solids. However, constrained by factors such as lens aberrations, Z-contrast dependence and poor temporal resolution, the scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) fails to tackle the long-standing challenge of hydrogen dynamics on atomic scale. Here, our experiment-guided simulations reveal that unlike heavy-atom imaging - governed by wave-based imaging process - ballistic propagation of particle-like electrons in Cs-corrected TEM enables super-resolution imaging (SRI) of light atoms, achieving a resolution down to 14 pm and a robust hydrogen contrast of ~24%. Our in-situ SRI study not only uncovers polymetric hydrogen aggregation in TiHx and H-LaAlO3, but also inter-site migration, rotational displacement of hydrogen species and collective hydrogen tunneling-induced polarization surge in the oxide. By exploiting the novel electron-matter interactions, our findings offer an unparalleled approach to probe hydrogen dynamics and establish accurate structure-property relationships in diverse functional materials.

一种具有 AC 和 DC 耦合前端及透明双 DDR 事件存储器的八通道 RFSoC 波形数字化仪

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

An eight-channel RFSoC waveform digitizer with AC- and DC-coupled front ends and transparent dual-DDR event memory

We present Transparent Dual-DDR Event Memory (TDEM) for the triggered readout of an eight-channel radio-frequency system-on-chip (RFSoC) digitizer. With either an AC- or a DC-coupled front end, each accepted trigger records 4096 samples per channel at 5 GSa/s. Two pulses 100 ns apart produced separate, overlapping records. On one DC-coupled input, interleaving calibration reduced an eight-sample periodic residual by a factor of 29 at +100 mV, but not at zero input. After a power cycle, the digitizer restored the coefficients for all eight DC-coupled inputs without a training tone. TDEM assigns complete events to 64- and 32-bit DDR4 memory interfaces in a 2:1 ratio. With this allocation alone, host page requests still determined which memory served each read. In two runs of approximately 6 s at 160 kHz, the peak receiver backlog fell from 20 140 events with the earlier readout to 1457 with event-level prefetch. Prefetch raised the sustained rate of the DC-coupled system from 150 to 170 kHz under repeated host readout pauses. With the same receiver design and a revised host buffer allocation, the AC-coupled system passed three 30 min tests at 175 kHz, each with 48 readout pauses and no rejected triggers. Without imposed pauses or background load, the host verified all 323 452 631 events in 30 min at 179.7 kHz. At 180 kHz, triggers were first rejected at the digitizer, consistent with a modelled transmitter capacity of 179.730 kHz for this event format.

A Theoretical Investigation of Magnetic Susceptibility Measurement of Diamagnetic Liquids Using a Michelson Interferometer

We examine the theoretical use of a Michelson interferometer to infer the magnetic susceptibility of a diamagnetic liquid from a magnetically induced surface deformation. A ring magnet changes the liquid thickness along the sample beam, producing a signed phase shift relative to a fixed optical reference. For a double traversal of the sample, the fringe displacement is $m = 2(n_{\ell} - n_a)\Delta z/\lambda$. We derive the susceptibility inversion both in a local gravity approximation and with surface tension included through the linearized Young-Laplace equation. The treatment distinguishes a finite reference field from a zero-field reference, specifies the displacement sign and susceptibility contrast, and identifies calibration and uncertainty requirements. Reproducible illustrative calculations use a uniformly magnetized annulus. The results establish a theoretical measurement relation and its assumptions; they do not constitute an experimental demonstration or a verified accuracy claim.

查看原文标题与摘要

Selection-aware inference of supernova kicks

Observed compact objects (isolated or in binaries) used to infer natal kicks are selected by class membership, which depends on disruption, subsequent evolution, and discovery. Ignoring this selection can bias the inferred kicks. To demonstrate this bias in a comprehensive inference framework, we construct a differentiable model for key components of binary evolution, including correlated remnant-mass and kick prescriptions as well as survival probabilities. As initial conditions prior to the first SN, we use a fiducial COSMIC analysis. Using conventional statistical methods to assess bias due to omitted selection factors in a likelihood, we demonstrate this bias concretely. Reliable natal inference requires a forward prediction for both the observed properties and the probability of observing them. Our framework provides a novel method to enable robust neutron star population inference combining heterogeneous observations of multiple NS-pertinent observations.

Leveraging Industrial Foundation Models at the Edge of Particle Physics Detectors via Distillation Learning and Hardware Co-design

Data acquisition (DAQ) systems at future particle physics experiments stand to benefit from the extremes of AI/ML development: large-scale foundation models can enhance the performance of feature extraction algorithms, and small-scale on-detector deployments can enable real-time intelligent data handling. This work provides the first tokenizer fine-tuning of an industrial foundation model for particle physics DAQ. Starting from the backbone of Google Research's TimesFM (Time Series Foundation Model), we address real-time regression tasks for drift chamber trackers and dual-readout calorimeters by jointly training the pretrained tokenizer and a newly initialized residual adapter, then fitting task-specific linear heads while keeping the transformer backbone frozen. Furthermore, the resulting TimesFM teachers can be distilled into students and co-designed with FPGA implementation to support real-time operation at future colliders. The distilled and compressed models achieve competitive performance, while distillation reduces reliance on labeled training data. Further, the pipeline of distillation and model compression from TimesFM is generic and can be easily adapted to a variety of 1D waveform tasks across domains.

原中子星中的有限温度夸克动力学：最大重子质量与径向振荡

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

即使冷物态方程保持不变，有限温度夸克动力学也能显著改变原中子星的演化。我们通过比较纯强子基准模型与两个混合模型来量化这种敏感性；这两个混合模型通过 Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (PNJL) 框架及其纠缠扩展 (EPNJL) 引入夸克物质，从而分离出有限温度手征-退禁闭耦合的作用。我们对核液-气相共存和强子-夸克相共存均采用体相 Gibbs 构造，计算热中微子俘获阶段、热无中微子阶段和冷阶段的平衡构型，并计算所选热无中微子恒星的线性径向振荡。引入夸克会降低每个阶段的最大重子质量，并逆转其演化次序：在纯强子模型中，最大重子质量朝冷极限增大，而在两个混合模型中，它均逐渐减小，从而为延迟坍缩打开质量窗口。关键的是，尽管具有相同的冷极限，PNJL 和 EPNJL 对混合核心跨阶段存活给出了不同的重子质量区间：只有 EPNJL 允许构型在两个热阶段均保留混合核心，而只有 PNJL 允许热无中微子混合核心恒星具有对应的冷平衡构型。所有被检验的热无中微子混合核心恒星均保持线性稳定，但它们的基频径向振荡频率存在显著差异。这些发现表明，除冷物质描述之外，有限温度夸克动力学还会在原中子星上留下不同的演化约束和脉动特征。

Finite-temperature quark dynamics in proto-neutron stars: Maximum baryonic mass and radial oscillations

Finite-temperature quark dynamics can significantly alter proto-neutron star evolution even when the cold equation of state remains unchanged. We quantify this sensitivity by comparing a purely hadronic baseline with two hybrid models incorporating quark matter via the Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (PNJL) framework and its entanglement extension (EPNJL), which isolates the role of finite-temperature chiral-deconfinement coupling. Employing bulk Gibbs constructions for both nuclear liquid-gas and hadron-quark phase coexistence, we compute equilibrium configurations across hot neutrino-trapped, hot neutrino-free, and cold stages, alongside linear radial oscillations of selected hot neutrino-free stars. Introducing quarks lowers the maximum baryonic mass at every stage and reverses its evolutionary ordering: whereas the maximum baryonic mass increases toward the cold limit in the purely hadronic model, it decreases progressively in both hybrid models, opening mass windows for delayed collapse. Crucially, despite sharing the same cold limit, PNJL and EPNJL yield distinct baryonic-mass intervals for mixed-core survival across stages: only EPNJL permits configurations that retain mixed cores across both hot stages, whereas only PNJL allows hot neutrino-free mixed-core stars to possess cold equilibrium counterparts. All tested hot neutrino-free mixed-core stars remain linearly stable, yet their fundamental radial frequencies differ substantially. These findings demonstrate that finite-temperature quark dynamics imprints distinct evolutionary constraints and pulsation signatures on proto-neutron stars beyond cold-matter descriptions.

爱因斯坦望远镜几何构型比较报告的科学章节

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

这是爱因斯坦望远镜 (ET) 几何构型比较报告的科学附加价值章节，该报告由 ET 政府代表委员会委托 ET 组织编写。我们比较三种构型：单台址 10-km 三角形 ($\Delta$)，以及位于相距很远的台址、近乎对齐或未对齐的两台 15-km L 形探测器 (2L)。我们使用代表 ET 科学目标的指标：致密双星总体、致密物质、宇宙测绘学、随机背景、多信使天文学、基础物理学，以及连续源和暴发源。所有三种构型都能实现 ET 科学目标，但各自在不同领域表现突出。对于大多数由灵敏度驱动的科学研究，2L 构型的表现优于 $\Delta$。未对齐的 2L 最适合对单个源进行定位和表征、多信使天文学以及亮标准汽笛宇宙学。近乎对齐的 2L 最适合随机背景研究。$\Delta$ 对源的定位优于对齐的 2L，并且对于连续源和暴发源以及在千赫兹频率下，其表现与两种 2L 构型相当。$30^\circ$ 的 2L 取向是随机背景灵敏度与定位能力之间的良好折中。对于 $\sim550$ 个双黑洞和中子星-黑洞信号，我们通过与贝叶斯推断比较来验证 Fisher 信息矩阵 (FIM) 的预测。尽管存在显著差异，FIM 对于内禀参数并未显示出对任一构型的系统性偏好。对于距离和天空定位，FIM 无法刻画多模态性和长程相关性，因而导致更大的差异，但这并不影响排序。将 Cosmic Explorer 加入网络可提升所有三种 ET 构型的探测范围，尤其是在体积定位和高红移源表征方面。不同几何构型之间的差异有所缩小，但排序基本不变。

The Science Chapter of the Einstein Telescope Geometry Comparison Report

This is the Scientific Added Value Chapter of the Einstein Telescope (ET) Geometry Comparison Report, commissioned from the ET Organization by the ET Board of Governmental Representatives. We compare three configurations: a single-site 10-km triangle ($\Delta$) and two 15-km L-shaped detectors (2L) at widely separated sites, either nearly aligned or misaligned. We use metrics representative of the ET science case: compact-binary populations, dense matter, cosmography, stochastic backgrounds, multimessenger astronomy, fundamental physics, and continuous and burst sources. All three configurations deliver the ET science case, but they excel in different areas. For most sensitivity-driven science, the 2L configurations outperform the $\Delta$. The misaligned 2L is best for localizing and characterizing individual sources, multimessenger astronomy, and bright-siren cosmology. The nearly aligned 2L is best for stochastic backgrounds. The $\Delta$ localizes sources better than the aligned 2L and performs comparably to both 2L configurations for continuous and burst sources and at kilohertz frequencies. A 2L orientation of $30^\circ$ is a good compromise between stochastic-background sensitivity and localization. We validate Fisher information matrix (FIM) forecasts against Bayesian inference for $\sim550$ binary black hole and neutron star-black hole signals. Despite substantial differences, the FIM shows no systematic preference for either configuration for intrinsic parameters. For distance and sky localization, the FIM cannot capture multimodality and long-range correlations, leading to larger discrepancies, which however do not affect the ranking. Adding Cosmic Explorer to the network improves the reach of all three ET configurations, especially in volume localization and characterization of high-redshift sources. Differences between geometries shrink, but the ranking is largely unchanged.

On extracting Science information from a large data set of RXTE X-ray binary spectra using KDE technique

Machine Learning/Neural Networks have been successively and extensively used in Astronomy (and other subjects) for class identification of objects based on large data sets. However, it remains difficult to extract other science information, such as what the characteristics of the class are that allow for definite identification, and whether there are objects in the sample that are unique and hence of possible science interest. An alternate approach based on rank ordering of variables and Kernel Density Estimation (KDE) may be used for class identification and to infer such science information. We test the method on a previously analyzed data set of RXTE spectra of low-mass X-ray binaries and find that black hole and neutron star systems can be distinguished at an accuracy level of ~93%, which is comparable to deep learning techniques. The soft state spectra of black hole systems are distinct from neutron star ones. For the whole sample, observations of the pulsar 4U 1907+097 (which was inadvertently introduced into the data set) were clearly found to be unique. The previously reported hyper-soft states of GRO J1655-40 and XTE J1550-564 are found to be unique among black hole spectra. For the rest of the neutron stars, the method identifies some unreported spectra of EXO 1745-248 as unique because they seem to be relativistic reflection-dominated spectra. We discuss the potential and scalability of this method.

混有费米子暗物质的奇异夸克星的早期演化

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们研究了混有费米子暗物质（DM）的奇异夸克星在其早期演化的三个准静态阶段：中微子俘获阶段、去轻子化后的加热阶段和冷阶段。夸克物质采用有限温度下的 MIT 袋模型描述，其中包含热胶子、排斥性矢量相互作用，并且在第一阶段包含被俘获的中微子。DM 被建模为质量为 $m_\chi = 0.3$、$1$ 和 $3$ GeV 的冷非对称费米子气体，分别考虑有无排斥性自相互作用的情况，并且仅通过引力与夸克物质相互作用。利用双流体 Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff 方程，我们计算了 DM 质量分数 $F_D$ 高至 $5\%$ 时各阶段恒星的结构、最大质量和潮汐形变率，并在重子数和 DM 粒子数固定的条件下跟踪各颗恒星。DM 的效应在三个阶段中几乎相同：对于 $m_\chi = 1$ GeV 和 $F_D = 5\%$，DM 在每个阶段都使最大质量降低约 $8\%$，并使一颗 $1.4\,M_\odot$ 恒星的潮汐形变率降低约 $26\%$，而阶段本身对这些量的改变仅为百分之几。重 DM 形成致密核心；对于无自相互作用的 $m_\chi = 3$ GeV 情形，该核心的质量极限可以决定恒星的最大质量。轻 DM 可以形成扩展晕，但对于所考虑的分数，这不会增大最大质量。对于 $m_\chi = 1$ GeV 和 $F_D = 5\%$，DM 使新生恒星的最大重子质量降低约 $0.23\,M_\odot$，但在所考虑的所有情形中，这一极限从中微子俘获阶段到冷阶段均有所增大，因此 DM 不会导致延迟坍缩成黑洞。在我们的模型中，质量约为 $2\,M_\odot$ 的脉冲星要求夸克物质具有硬状态方程，并将 DM 分数限制在百分之几以内，除非 DM 很轻。

Early Evolution of Strange Quark Stars Admixed with Fermionic Dark Matter

We study strange quark stars admixed with fermionic dark matter (DM) in three quasistatic stages of their early evolution: the neutrino-trapped stage, the heated stage after deleptonization, and the cold stage. Quark matter is described by the MIT bag model at finite temperature with thermal gluons, a repulsive vector interaction, and, in the first stage, trapped neutrinos. The DM is modeled as a cold, asymmetric gas of fermions with masses $m_\chi = 0.3$, $1$, and $3$ GeV, with and without a repulsive self-interaction, and interacts with the quark matter only through gravity. Using the two-fluid Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations, we compute the structure, the maximum masses, and the tidal deformability of the stars in each stage for DM mass fractions $F_D$ up to $5\%$, and we follow individual stars at fixed numbers of baryons and DM particles. The effect of DM is nearly the same in the three stages: for $m_\chi = 1$ GeV and $F_D = 5\%$, DM lowers the maximum mass by about $8\%$ and the tidal deformability of a $1.4\,M_\odot$ star by about $26\%$ in each stage, whereas the stage itself changes these quantities by only a few percent. Heavy DM forms a compact core; for $m_\chi = 3$ GeV without self-interaction, the mass limit of this core can set the maximum mass of the star. Light DM can form an extended halo, which does not increase the maximum mass for the fractions considered. For $m_\chi = 1$ GeV and $F_D = 5\%$, DM lowers the largest baryon mass of a newborn star by about $0.23\,M_\odot$, but this limit increases from the neutrino-trapped to the cold stage in all cases considered, so that DM does not cause a delayed collapse to a black hole. In our model, pulsars of about $2\,M_\odot$ require a stiff quark equation of state and restrict the DM fraction to a few percent unless the DM is light.

我们在色玻璃凝聚（CGC）有效场论框架内，计算了大型强子对撞机（LHC）超外周铅-铅和质子-铅碰撞（UPCs）中相干衍射 $D^0$ 光生过程的截面。我们同时纳入粲-反粲夸克对的独占产生和伴随一个额外半硬胶子的衍射产生，后者采用衍射横动量依赖（TMD）因子化描述。我们表明，衍射道尽管受到强耦合的抑制，却在大横动量处占主导地位，而独占道在该区域受到色透明效应的抑制。此外，我们还利用固定光子能量下的核修正因子 $R_{pA}$，在部分子层次量化核效应；该因子显示衍射道受到轻微抑制，而独占道则出现随横动量增大而增强的效应，最高约为原来的2倍。我们还使用衍射截面修正我们的包容 $D^0$ 预言，以符合要求靶原子核破裂的 CMS 事例选择，并将其与数据进行比较。最后，我们给出了质子-铅 UPCs 中包容 $D^0$ 光生过程的预言。

Diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral collisions at the LHC

We compute the cross section for coherent diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral lead-lead and proton-lead collisions (UPCs) at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) within the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective field theory. We include both the exclusive production of a charm-anticharm pair and diffractive production with an additional semi-hard gluon, described in diffractive transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorization. We show that the diffractive channel, although suppressed by the strong coupling, dominates at large transverse momentum, where the exclusive channel is suppressed by color transparency. Furthermore, we quantify nuclear effects at the parton level with a nuclear modification factor $R_{pA}$ at fixed photon energy, which shows a mild suppression for the diffractive channel and an enhancement growing with transverse momentum up to about a factor of 2 for the exclusive one. We also use the diffractive cross section to correct our inclusive $D^0$ predictions to the CMS event selection, which requires the target nucleus to break up, and compare them with the data. Finally, we provide predictions for inclusive $D^0$ photoproduction in proton-lead UPCs.

迈向储存环中的自动离子鉴别

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

共振肖特基探测器能够对储存在重离子储存环中的离子种类的回旋频率和强度进行无损、单离子灵敏的测量。这些高-\(Q\)腔允许分析回旋频率的高阶谐波，以放大绝对频率间隔。然而，相邻的谐波范围可能在探测器频带内重叠，使得仅凭频率不足以确定离子的质荷比 $(m/q)$。我们提出了一种在肖特基质谱法与等时性质谱法相结合的情况下，利用谱形实现自动谐波去重叠的概念验证方法。其经验性假设是，对于固定的离子光学设置，每个与具有给定 $m/q$ 比的特定离子种类相对应的峰都具有特征轮廓，该轮廓随 $m/q$ 的变化而平滑演化。因此，在不同谐波处出现的相同局部结构经频率重标度后应保持几乎完全相同的形状。将该方法应用于在 GSI 实验储存环中由 11\,452 次注入累积得到的 245\,MHz 频谱后，该方法将探测到的 32 个结构中的 16 个归入五个候选族，并重建了它们的基频表示。将复杂的混合谐波频谱缩减为这一较小的候选集合，解决了寻找离子鉴别初始锚点这一具有挑战性的任务。

Towards Automatic Ion Identification in Storage Rings

Resonant Schottky detectors provide non-destructive, single-ion-sensitive measurements of revolution frequencies and intensities of ion species stored in a heavy-ion storage ring. These high-\(Q\) cavities allow analysis of high-order harmonics of the revolution frequencies to magnify absolute frequency separations. Adjacent harmonic ranges can, however, overlap within the detector band, making frequency alone insufficient for determining the ion's mass-to-charge ratio $(m/q)$. We present a proof-of-concept for an automated harmonic de-overlapping method using spectral shapes in combined Schottky and Isochronous Mass Spectrometry. The empirical ansatz is that, for a fixed ion-optical setting, each peak, corresponding to a specific ion species with given $m/q$ ratio, has a characteristic profile that evolves smoothly with changing $m/q$. Occurrences of the same local structure at different harmonics should therefore retain almost identical shapes after frequency rescaling. Applied to a 245\,MHz spectrum accumulated from 11\,452 injections at the Experimental Storage Ring at GSI, the method organizes 16 of 32 detected structures into five candidate families and reconstructs their fundamental-frequency representation. Reducing the complex mixed-harmonic spectrum to this smaller candidate set solves the challenging task of finding initial anchors for the ion identification.

中子星中体黏度的潮汐效应

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

中子星物质中的体黏度提供了一种依赖温度的潮汐耗散机制。在本研究中，我们考察体黏度对由中子、质子和电子组成的中子星的耗散性潮汐响应的影响。采用通过动量依赖相互作用模型得到的冷态状态方程，我们研究由直接 Urca 过程产生的体黏度，该过程用于恢复化学平衡。通过弱相互作用速率引入温度依赖性，使我们能够探索温度高达数 MeV 时中微子透明区间内体黏度的行为。为计及中子星内部体黏度的空间分布，我们引入有效体黏度，即在直接 Urca 过程发生区域内进行体积平均所得的量，并建立其与体黏度的联系。具体而言，我们研究其对潮汐量的影响，包括滞后时间和耗散性形变率，并着重说明它们对温度和恒星致密度的依赖性。我们发现，耗散性潮汐效应在某一温度下达到最大值，该温度近似与直接 Urca 开始发生时体黏度达到最大值的温度一致。此外，与最大质量的中子星相比，这些效应在中等质量的中子星中更为显著。最后，对于标准的 $1.4\;M_\odot$ 和最大质量中子星构型，我们推导出这些量的基于因果性的上限，为评估双中子星旋近产生的引力波信号中体黏滞效应的潜在可观测性提供理论基准。

Tidal effects of bulk viscosity in neutron stars

Bulk viscosity in neutron-star matter provides a temperature-dependent mechanism for tidal dissipation. In this study, we investigate the impact of bulk viscosity on the dissipative tidal response of neutron stars composed of neutrons, protons, and electrons. Employing cold equations of state derived through the momentum-dependent interaction model, we examine the bulk viscosity arising from the direct Urca process, which acts to restore chemical equilibrium. The temperature dependence is incorporated through the weak-interaction rate, allowing us to explore the behavior of bulk viscosity in the neutrino-transparent regime at temperatures up to a few MeV. To account for the spatial distribution of bulk viscosity within neutron stars, we introduce the effective bulk viscosity, a volume-averaged quantity over the region where direct Urca process operates, and establish a connection with the bulk viscosity. Specifically, we investigate its implications for tidal quantities, including the lag time and dissipative deformability, highlighting their dependence on temperature and stellar compactness. We find that the dissipative tidal effects become maximal at a temperature approximately coinciding with the one of maximum bulk viscosity on the onset of direct Urca. Additionally, these effects are more pronounced in intermediate neutron star masses compared to maximum ones. Finally, for both canonical $1.4\;M_\odot$ and maximum-mass neutron star configurations, we derive causality-based upper bounds on these quantities, providing theoretical benchmarks for assessing the potential observability of bulk-viscous effects in gravitational-wave signals from binary neutron star inspirals.

EuXFEL 超导 TESLA 腔压电运行的优化

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

具有高-Q 因子的超导腔需要精确调谐以匹配 RF 频率，从而确保电磁场稳定并最大限度降低 RF 功耗。在欧洲 XFEL（EuXFEL）加速器中，TESLA 腔采用慢速调谐器（步进电机）进行粗调，并采用快速调谐器（压电致动器）进行精调以及补偿洛伦兹力失谐（LFD）等扰动。压电致动器对于该系统至关重要，需要高电压（高达 100V）和大电流（高达 1A）驱动信号才能有效补偿 LFD。然而，它们容易受到过电压、过电流和过热的损害，并且由于在完全组装好的模块中更换损坏的压电元件不可行，对其进行保护至关重要。此外，压电元件引发的振动会影响机器的稳定性。在确保有效补偿 LFD 的同时，通过降低电压、电流和电流斜率来优化压电激励，可同等改善效率、可靠性和机器稳定性。本文探讨了 EuXFEL 压电激励的优化，详细介绍了同样适用于其他超导腔设施的方法和结果。

查看原文标题与摘要

Optimization of Piezo Operation for Superconducting TESLA Cavities at EuXFEL

Superconducting cavities with high-Q factor require precise tuning to match the RF frequency, ensuring stable electromagnetic fields and minimizing RF power consumption. At the European XFEL (EuXFEL) accelerator, TESLA cavities are tuned using slow tuners (step motors) for coarse adjustments and fast tuners (piezoelectric actuators) for fine-tuning and compensating disturbances such as Lorentz Force Detuning (LFD). Piezo actuators, critical to this system, require high-voltage (up to 100V) and high-current (up to 1A) driving signals for effective LFD compensation. However, they are vulnerable to overvoltage, overcurrent, and overheating, and their protection is crucial since replacing damaged piezos in fully assembled modules is infeasible. Additionally, piezo-induced vibrations can affect the machine's stability. Optimizing piezo excitation, by reducing voltage, current, and current slope while ensuring effective LFD compensation, improves equally efficiency, reliability and machine stability. This paper explores the optimization of piezo excitation at EuXFEL, detailing methods and results applicable to other facilities with superconducting cavities as well.

质子驱动等离子体尾场的精确1D非线性解

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

对相对论性质子束驱动的等离子体尾场进行解析建模，是优化先进等离子体加速方案的一个要素。在本工作中，我们提出了一个准静态近似下的1D非线性流体框架，用于描述由带正电的质子驱动束激发的尾场势；该驱动束将背景电子吸引至密度压缩区，而不是将其排出。我们将经典1D非线性相对论等离子体尾场范式加以扩展，以在共动坐标近似下评估强相对论质子束列驱动的流体分布。采用双束团方案，我们将分段事件积分模型与冷流体解的独立相空间求积进行比较，以高数值精度（$\sim 10^{-11}~\mathrm{GV/m}$）验证了该模型，证实界面边界拐角代表势的二阶导数中真实的阶跃不连续。将其扩展至具有固定$2\pi$间距的$N=100$渐升微束团列后，我们通过推导分段解析标度模型，解析出宏观相位累积及随后的非线性失谐。该表述刻画了初始未衰减的二次相干增长以及束列之后平坦的渐近残余尾场平台，后者由在最终束团列出口状态处计算的守恒真空哈密顿量不变量精确确定。此外，该框架估算了非线性相位滑移的起始点，这种相位滑移会使增长停止，并导致瞬态尾场过冲朝残余极限衰减。这一计算成本低廉的工具可直接提供1D解析基准，为验证1D流体代码和多维粒子网格（PIC）代码提供可复现的基线。它还为探索与变压器比优化相关的非对称定制形状驱动束分布提供了一个易于处理的起点。

查看原文标题与摘要

Exact 1D Nonlinear Solutions for Proton-Driven Plasma Wakefields

The analytical modeling of a plasma wakefield driven by a relativistic proton beam is an element in optimizing advanced plasma-based acceleration schemes. In this work, we present a 1D nonlinear fluid framework under the quasi-static approximation to describe the wake potential excited by a positively charged proton driver which attracts background electrons into a density compression rather than expelling them. We extend the classical 1D nonlinear relativistic plasma wakefield paradigm to evaluate the fluid profiles driven by intense relativistic proton trains under the comoving coordinate approximation. Using a two-bunch scheme, our piecewise-event integration model is validated to high numerical accuracy ($\sim 10^{-11}~\mathrm{GV/m}$) against independent phase-space quadrature of cold-fluid solutions, confirming that interface boundary corners represent physical step discontinuities in the second potential derivative. Scaling to an $N=100$ ramped micro-bunch train with fixed $2\pi$ spacing, we resolve macroscopic phase-accumulation and subsequent nonlinear detuning by deriving a piecewise analytical scaling model. This formulation maps the initial unattenuated quadratic coherent growth alongside the flat asymptotic post-train residual wake plateau, which is determined exactly by the conserved vacuum Hamiltonian invariant evaluated at the final bunch-train exit state. Furthermore, the framework estimates the onset of nonlinear phase-slippage that arrests growth and causes the transient wakefield overshoot to decay toward the residual horizon. Providing immediate 1D analytical benchmarks, this computationally inexpensive tool offers a reproducible baseline for verifying 1D fluid and multi-dimensional particle-in-cell (PIC) codes. It also provides a tractable starting point for exploring asymmetric, custom-shaped driver profiles relevant to transformer-ratio optimization.

暗物质和标量截断势对中子星宏观性质的影响：手征夸克平均场方法

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们研究费米子暗物质（DM）和标量截断势（$U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$）对中子星状态方程（EOS）及宏观性质的影响。重子物质由处于 $\beta$-平衡下的中子、质子、电子和μ子组成，并在手征 SU(3) 夸克平均场（CQMF）模型内进行描述。在此框架中，夸克被禁闭在重子内部，重子的性质通过交换标量场 $\sigma$、$\zeta$ 和 $\delta$ 以及矢量场 $\omega$ 和 $\rho$ 而发生改变。DM 被建模为具有标量 $\varphi_D$ 和矢量 $V_D^\mu$ 媒介子的自相互作用费米流体。重子流体和 DM 流体仅通过引力相互作用，并在双流体 Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff 形式体系内处理。我们考察所得的质量-半径关系和潮汐形变率以及引力红移，以研究 DM 对中子星结构和可观测性质的影响。引入 $U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$ 会使 EOS 变硬，而增加 DM 比例通常会使 EOS 变软，并改变质量-半径关系、潮汐形变率和引力红移。这些改变的幅度和性质取决于 DM 粒子质量和媒介子相互作用，它们决定了所得构型呈现致密的 DM 核还是延展的 DM 晕。
