Effect of dark matter and scalar-cut potential on the macroscopic properties of neutron stars: A chiral quark mean field approach

We investigate the effects of fermionic dark matter (DM) and scalar-cut potential ($U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$) on the equation of state (EOS) and macroscopic properties of neutron stars. The baryonic matter consists of neutrons, protons, electrons, and muons under the $\beta$-equilibrium and is described within the chiral SU(3) quark mean-field (CQMF) model. In this framework, quarks are confined within baryons, whose properties are modified through the exchange of scalar fields $\sigma$, $\zeta$, and $\delta$, and vector fields $\omega$ and $\rho$. The DM is modelled as a self-interacting fermionic fluid with scalar $\varphi_D$ and vector $V_D^\mu$ mediators. The baryonic and DM fluids interact only through gravity and are treated within a two-fluid Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff formalism. We examine the resulting mass-radius relation and tidal deformability, along with the gravitational redshift, to investigate the effects of DM on neutron star structure and observable properties. The inclusion of $U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$ stiffens the EOS, whereas increasing the DM fraction generally softens the EOS and modifies the mass-radius relation, tidal deformability, and gravitational redshift. The magnitude and nature of these modifications depend on the DM particle mass and mediator interactions, which determine whether the resulting configurations exhibit compact DM cores or extended DM halos.

突破纳安极限：用于$^{11}$B(p,$\alpha$)2$\alpha$反应中超低能共振的厚靶方法

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

Bypassing the Nanoampere Limit: A Thick-Target Methodology for Ultra-Low-Energy Resonances in the $^{11}$B(p,$\alpha$)2$\alpha$ Reaction

Precise measurements of the low-energy cross section for the $^{11}$B(p,$\alpha$)2$\alpha$ reaction is critical for nuclear astrophysics and fusion energy research, yet such measurements are experimentally demanding due to the reaction's extremely small cross section. Conventional thin-target experiments are fundamentally limited to nanoampere beam currents, which restrict count rates to impractical levels at ultra-low proton energies. To overcome this limitation, we report a correction-free thick-target methodology capable of operating at beam currents of 20-60 microamperes, corresponding to a gain of over three orders of magnitude. Monte Carlo simulations of the full reaction kinematics are employed to determine the absolute reaction yield, eliminating the requirement for empirical normalization factors. At the well-known 148~keV (c.m.) resonance peak, our extracted resonance energy ($E_{\mathrm{r}} = 148.0$~keV) and level width ($\Gamma_{\mathrm{cm}} = 5.3$~keV) are demonstrated in good agreement with published data. A scheme via comparing the measured reaction probability with a theoretical extrapolation result is furtherly presented to probe low-energy resonances below 100~keV region. Preliminary measurements at proton energies of 75~keV and 100~keV reveal no evidence for additional low-energy resonances. This work provides a robust new methodology to measure cross section and resonance parameters for the p$^{11}$B reaction and offers a highly efficient tool for systematic resonance searches in the ultra-low-energy regime.

A muonium laser ionization system for muon acceleration studies at J-PARC

This paper reports the development and commissioning of a muonium ionization laser system installed in the H2 area of the J-PARC Materials and Life Science Experimental Facility for muon accelerator studies. In this accelerator scheme, ultraslow muons generated by laser ionization of thermal muonium atoms serve as the particle source for subsequent radio-frequency acceleration. The laser system generates coherent 122-nm pulses for muonium excitation and 355-nm pulses for photoionization of the excited muonium. A vacuum-ultraviolet (VUV) pulse transport system and VUV pulse diagnostics were also developed. Muonium ionization was confirmed using a 5.7~keV muon transport line, and a muon rate of approximately 6~s$^{-1}$ was observed. These results establish operation of the ionization system and provide a platform for subsequent muon accelerator studies.

高能碰撞中的少胶子相互作用

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

在RHIC计划之前形成的一种关于高能重离子碰撞的传统叙事认为，强子产生由热而致密的QGP形成、膨胀和冻结主导。所提出的诊断方法包括：通过关联和能谱分析探测椭圆流与径向流，以及通过重标度能谱比探测致密介质中的喷注淬火。近年来，随着在小碰撞系统的数据中探测到类似特征，这一叙事的有效性受到质疑。作为回应，近期若干观测结果暗示需要对该叙事作出重大修正。对p-Pb能谱的研究揭示，使用Glauber蒙特卡洛方法估算A-B碰撞几何是相当误导的。相反，应通过反演系综平均$\bar p_t$数据来推断碰撞几何。已经开发出一种将$v_2(p_t)$数据转换为四极能谱$\bar \rho_2(p_t)$的方法，该方法为真实的关联机制提供了新见解。对p-p碰撞中$p_t$积分的$v_2$随$n_{ch}$变化进行的精确测量揭示了一种三次趋势，表明三胶子相互作用才是真实机制。p-p的$v_2(n_{ch})$趋势已被推广，以使用广延量$V_2^2 \equiv \bar \rho_0^2 v_2^2$（关联对的数量）统一描述所有A-B碰撞系统。类似的研究方案已应用于能谱和角关联中与喷注有关的贡献，并得到相似结果，即如双胶子相互作用所预期的那样，呈现关于$n_{ch}$的二次趋势。这些新结果表明，相关数据特征实际上代表了低密度系统内少胶子相互作用产生的多极（色偶极和四极）辐射。

查看原文标题与摘要

Few-gluon interactions in high-energy collisions

A conventional narrative developed for high energy heavy ion collisions prior to the RHIC program assumed hadron production dominated by hot and dense QGP formation, expansion and freezeout. Proposed diagnostics included detection of elliptic and radial flows via correlation and spectrum analysis and jet quenching in a dense medium via rescaled spectrum ratios. The validity of that narrative has come into question in recent years with detection of similar features in data from small collision systems. In response, several recent observations hint at a strongly modified narrative. Study of p-Pb spectra has revealed that the Glauber Monte Carlo estimation of A-B collision geometry is quite misleading. Collision geometry should instead be inferred by inverting ensemble-mean $\bar p_t$ data. A method has been developed to transform $v_2(p_t)$ data to quadrupole spectra $\bar \rho_2(p_t)$ that provide new insights into the true correlation mechanism. Accurate $p_t$-integrated $v_2$ vs $n_{ch}$ measurements for p-p collisions reveal a cubic trend suggesting a three-gluon interaction as the true mechanism. The p-p $v_2(n_{ch})$ trend has been generalized to describe all A-B collision systems in common in terms of an extensive measure $V_2^2 \equiv \bar \rho_0^2 v_2^2$ (number of correlated pairs). A similar program has been applied to jet-related contributions to spectra and angular correlations with a comparable result, a quadratic trend on $n_{ch}$ as expected for two-gluon interactions. Those new results suggest that the relevant data features actually represent multipole (color dipole and quadrupole) radiation from few-gluon interactions within a low-density system.

Event-by-Event Multiplicity Cumulants as Probes of Partonic Energy Loss and Phase Structure in Au+Au Collisions within a Modified HIJING Model

Event-by-event fluctuations of charged hadron multiplicity, net-proton, and net-electric charge are investigated in central Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} =$ 20-200 GeV using a modified HIJING Monte Carlo generator. The model incorporates QCD-inspired partonic energy-loss formalisms in hot (quark--gluon plasma) and cold media, reducing reliance on the default HIJING energy-loss parameter. At energies above 100 GeV/nucleon, multiplicity and net-charge fluctuations show sensitivity to the assumed medium properties, while net-proton results provide an additional comparison with available STAR measurements. By introducing a probabilistic distribution for a first-order phase transition, we find within the present modified-HIJING framework that multiplicity cumulant ratios ($\sigma^2/M$ and $S\sigma$) show a pronounced non-linear response to the adopted phase-allocation ansatz near the selected binodal energy, whereas net-charge and net-proton fluctuations remain relatively insensitive. Furthermore, expanding the kinematic acceptance enhances the sensitivity of multiplicity fluctuations to the selected transition region in the present phenomenological ansatz.

从多探针数据集快速推断状态方程

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

要完全约束中子星状态方程，需要结合探测不同密度区间的各种渠道的观测。我们使用双中子星并合的引力波观测、NICER对X射线脉冲星的质量-半径测量，以及射电脉冲星计时得到的大质量脉冲星质量测量，对状态方程进行联合多探针推断。我们约束参数化状态方程，并在一组候选状态方程之间进行贝叶斯模型选择。联合分析得到，一颗标准$1.4 \, M_\odot$中子星的半径为$R_{1.4} = 11.93^{+1.02}_{-0.83} \, \rm km$，非旋转中子星的最大质量为$M_{\rm max} = 2.31^{+0.23}_{-0.23} \, M_\odot$，可信区间为$90\%$。引力波和NICER观测约束低至中等密度下的状态方程，而大质量脉冲星观测通过要求状态方程能够支撑观测到的中子星质量，对高密度下的状态方程进行约束。我们发现，在不同观测探针中，状态方程的刚度与贝叶斯证据之间不存在简单相关性。

Rapid Equation of State Inference from Multiprobe Datasets

Fully constraining the neutron star equation of state requires combining observations from various channels that probe different density regimes. We perform a joint multiprobe inference of the equation of state using gravitational-wave observations from binary neutron star mergers, NICER mass-radius measurements of X-ray pulsars, and radio timing mass measurements of massive pulsars. We constrain the parametrized equation of state and perform Bayesian model selection among a set of candidate equations of state. The joint analysis yields a radius of $R_{1.4} = 11.93^{+1.02}_{-0.83} \, \rm km$ for a canonical $1.4 \, M_\odot$ neutron star, and a maximum non-rotating neutron star mass of $M_{\rm max} = 2.31^{+0.23}_{-0.23} \, M_\odot$ with $90\%$ credible intervals. Gravitational-wave and NICER observations constrain the equation of state at low to intermediate densities, while massive pulsar observations constrain it at high densities by requiring the equation of state to support the observed neutron star masses. We find no simple correlation between the stiffness of an equation of state and Bayesian evidence across different observational probes.

瞬态相对论流体动力学的 Einstein-Israel-Stewart 理论的非线性因果性与强双曲性

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们首次对一类非常普遍的具有体黏滞和剪切黏滞的相对论瞬态流体动力学理论进行了非线性因果性和局部适定性的完整分析，该类理论将以下理论作为特定子类涵盖在内：(i) 从熵流论证推导出的原始 Israel-Stewart 理论，(ii) 从动理学理论推导出的方法，以及 (iii) 基于重求和梯度展开的表述。我们的工作首次在完整非线性范围内，同时建立了因果性的代数充要条件以及保证强双曲性的充分条件。对于与具有动态度规的 Einstein 方程耦合的系统以及固定背景上的系统，无论有无宇宙学常数，我们都严格证明了这些结果，并且这些结果包括重子守恒（不存在热流/扩散流时）。这些条件是纯代数的，不需要对时空对称性作任何简化假设，也不需要特定的状态方程，并允许所有输运系数依赖于耗散流。我们还证明了动力学变量所满足的归一化、正交性、对称性和无迹性物理约束在解的整个存在期间均得到正确传播。我们的结果提供了一套可直接使用的工具，可用来在重离子碰撞和天体物理学的数值与唯象研究中考察相对论黏性流体动力学的适用范围。

查看原文标题与摘要

Nonlinear Causality and Strong Hyperbolicity of Einstein-Israel-Stewart Theories of Transient Relativistic Fluid Dynamics

We present the first complete analysis of nonlinear causality and local well-posedness for a very general class of bulk and shear viscous theories of relativistic transient fluid dynamics, which encompasses (i) the original Israel-Stewart theory derived from entropy-current arguments, (ii) approaches derived from kinetic theory, and (iii) resummed gradient-expansion based formulations as particular subcases. Our work establishes, for the first time, simultaneously necessary and sufficient algebraic conditions for causality, alongside sufficient conditions guaranteeing strong hyperbolicity, in the full nonlinear regime. These results are rigorously proven for both systems coupled to Einstein's equations featuring a dynamic metric and on a fixed background, with or without a cosmological constant, and include baryon conservation (in the absence of heat/diffusion currents). The conditions are purely algebraic, require no simplifying spacetime symmetry assumptions or a specific equation of state, and allow all transport coefficients to depend on the dissipative currents. We also demonstrate that the normalization, orthogonality, symmetry, and tracelessness physical constraints on the dynamical variables are properly propagated during the lifetime of the solutions. Our results provide a readily usable toolset with which one can investigate the domain of applicability of relativistic viscous fluid dynamics in numerical and phenomenological studies in heavy-ion collisions and astrophysics.

M67 中的富锂恒星：通过紫外观测搜寻热伴星

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

富锂（Li-rich）恒星是一类罕见天体，其特征是大气中的 Li 丰度异常高。尽管已提出包括双星相互作用在内的若干解释，但其起源仍不确定。我们的目标是搜寻 M67 中已知富锂恒星的热伴星，表征这些恒星，并评估双星假说能否作为其 Li 增丰的一种可能解释。我们将来自 UVIT/AstroSat、GALEX 和 Swift/UVOT 的档案紫外测光数据与光学和红外测光数据相结合，构建多波段光谱能量分布（SEDs）并搜寻热伴星。我们还使用档案中的高分辨率 UVES 光谱来寻找过去双星相互作用的化学特征。M67 中两颗已知的富锂恒星 WOCS6008 和 WOCS2003，相对于单星 SED 拟合均显示出显著的远紫外过量，前者归因于伴星，后者归因于恒星活动。因此，我们报告了首次在 M67 中探测到富锂恒星 WOCS6008 的热伴星，这为双星相互作用在其 Li 增丰中所起的作用提供了直接证据。研究发现该伴星是一颗质量为 $\sim 0.16\pm0.01 M_{\odot}$ 的极低质量（ELM）白矮星（WD），与其运动学质量一致，年龄为 2.5 Gyr。我们在 WOCS6008 中未发现 Na 或 s-过程元素增丰，也未发现 C 或 Mg 亏损，这表明不太可能在渐近巨星支或红巨星支阶段发生过质量转移。其推导丰度支持这样一种情景：该恒星是一个经历了早期 Case B 质量转移的双星系统中的吸积星。我们的结果支持此前的观点，即恒星自转是该相互作用后系统中 Li 增丰最受青睐的机制。总体而言，现有证据支持双星相互作用是形成富锂恒星的途径之一。

Lithium rich stars in M67: Searching for hot companions through UV observations

Lithium-rich (Li-rich) stars are a rare class of objects characterized by an abnormally high Li abundance in their atmospheres. Although several explanations, including binary interactions, have been proposed, their origin remains uncertain. We aim to search for hot companions to the known Li-rich stars in M67, characterize these stars, and evaluate the binary hypothesis as a possible explanation for their Li enhancement. We combined archival ultraviolet photometry from UVIT/AstroSat, GALEX, and Swift/UVOT with optical and infrared photometry to create multiwavelength spectral energy distributions (SEDs) and search for hot companions. We also used high-resolution archival UVES spectra to look for chemical signatures of past binary interactions. Two known Li-rich stars in M67, WOCS6008 and WOCS2003, show a significant far-UV excess relative to a single-star SED fit, attributed to a companion in the former and to stellar activity in the latter. We thus report the first detection of a hot companion to the Li-rich star, WOCS6008, in M67, offering direct evidence of the role of binary interactions in its Li enrichment. The companion is found to be an extremely low-mass (ELM) white dwarf (WD) of $\sim 0.16\pm0.01 M_{\odot}$, consistent with its kinematic mass, and an age of 2.5 Gyr. We find no enhancement of Na or s-process elements and no depletion of C or Mg in WOCS6008, suggesting that mass transfer during the asymptotic giant branch or red giant branch stage is unlikely. Its derived abundances support a scenario in which this star is the accretor in a binary system that underwent early Case B mass transfer. Our results support previous suggestions that stellar rotation is the most favored mechanism for the Li enrichment in this post-interaction system. Overall, the available evidence supports the case for binary interactions as one of the channels to form Li-rich stars.

EuCu 9 Sn 4 中没有显著反常 Hall 特征的大磁矩铁磁性

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

反常 Hall 效应通常被视为金属铁磁性的特征性标志，但原则上，稳健的铁磁有序并不一定意味着存在易于区分的反常 Hall 输运。在此，我们利用磁化强度、热容、电输运、中子衍射和电子结构计算研究了稀土金属间化合物 EuCu 9 Sn 4。我们表明，EuCu 9 Sn 4 在 T c = 9.5 K 处发生磁跃迁，进入磁矩沿晶体学 c 方向排列的共线铁磁态。尽管存在大磁矩铁磁有序，T c 以下的 Hall 响应对磁场仅呈弱非线性，且没有可明确分辨的反常贡献。温差分析得到依赖于处理程序的实验灵敏度尺度 ρ x y sens ≃ 0.0093 µ Ω cm 和 σ x y sens ≃ 157 Ω − 1 cm − 1。电子结构计算将已占据的 Eu- 4 f 流形定位在 Fermi 能级以下约 1 eV 处，并表明 Fermi 能级附近的投影态主要具有杂化的 Sn- p / Cu − d 特征。这种主导轨道特征的差异为输运观测提供了合理的电子结构背景。综合来看，这些结果确立了 EuCu 9 Sn 4 这一经过充分表征的平台，可用于研究大的局域磁矩如何与横向电荷输运耦合，并凸显出仅有稳健的铁磁有序并不能确保出现易于区分的反常 Hall 响应。

阅读原文 ↗
查看原文标题与摘要

Large-moment ferromagnetism without a distinct anomalous Hall signature in EuCu 9 Sn 4

The anomalous Hall effect is often regarded as a characteristic signature of metallic ferromagnetism, yet in principle, robust ferromagnetic order does not necessarily imply readily distinguishable anomalous Hall transport. Here, we investigate the rare-earth intermetallic EuCu 9 Sn 4 using magnetization, heat-capacity, electrical-transport, neutron diffraction, and electronic-structure calculations. We show that EuCu 9 Sn 4 undergoes a magnetic transition at T c = 9.5 K into a collinear ferromagnetic state with moments aligned along the crystallographic c direction. Despite the large-moment ferromagnetic order, the Hall response below T c is weakly nonlinear in field, with no uniquely resolvable anomalous contribution. A temperature-difference analysis yields procedure-dependent experimental sensitivity scales of ρ x y sens ≃ 0.0093 µ Ω cm and σ x y sens ≃ 157 Ω − 1 cm − 1 . Electronic-structure calculations place the occupied Eu- 4 f manifold approximately 1 eV below the Fermi level and show that the projected states near the Fermi level have predominantly hybridized Sn- p / Cu − d character. This difference in dominant orbital character provides plausible electronic-structure context for the transport observations. Together, these results establish EuCu 9 Sn 4 as a well-characterized setting for examining how large localized magnetic moments couple to transverse charge transport, highlighting that robust ferromagnetic order alone does not ensure a readily distinguishable anomalous Hall response.

通过双中子星系统中的潮汐共振增强偏心率

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

双中子星系统的轨道偏心率是揭示其形成历史的关键参数。由于孤立形成的双星预计早在并合之前就会圆化，因此可测量的偏心率表明其在致密恒星环境中动力学形成。然而，利用数值相对论模拟，我们表明，由于其中一颗星的 $f$-模共振，偏心率可以在初始准圆双星的晚期旋近阶段产生。我们模拟了两颗高速自旋中子星的并合，其中一颗星顺行自旋，另一颗星逆行自旋。我们观测到与逆行自旋星的 $f$ 模发生持续的潮汐共振，并伴随其自旋因轨道向其转移角动量而逐渐变化。一个考虑了依赖自旋的 $f$-模频率偏移的简单共振锁定模型，可以很好地解释这种自旋变化。这些发现揭示了双中子星中一种与其形成历史无关的偏心率起源，并为建立含高速自旋星的双中子星动力学模型提供了重要信息。

查看原文标题与摘要

Enhancing eccentricity through tidal resonance in binary neutron star systems

Orbital eccentricity of binary neutron star systems is a key parameter to reveal their formation history. Since binaries formed in isolation are expected to circularize long before merger, measurable eccentricity suggests dynamical formation in dense stellar environments. However, using numerical-relativity simulations, we show that eccentricity can be introduced in the late inspiral of an initially quasicircular binary, due to the $f$-mode resonance of one of the stars. We simulate the merger of two highly spinning neutron stars, one star spinning prograde and the other retrograde. Persistent tidal resonance with the $f$ mode of the retrograde-spinning star is observed, accompanied by a gradual change of its spin due to angular momentum transfer from the orbit. This spin change can be well explained by a simple resonance locking model that accounts for the spin-dependent shift of the $f$-mode frequency. These findings reveal an origin of eccentricity in binary neutron stars that is independent of their formation history and provide important information for modeling binary neutron star dynamics with rapidly spinning stars.

迈向全局 CE$\nu$NS 图景

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们提出了一个从实验数据中提取并解释相干弹性中微子-核散射（CE$\nu$NS）微分截面的一般框架。该框架采用由广义中微子相互作用模型中反复出现的常见运动学项构建的广义参数化基，以数值方式约束所观测 CE$\nu$NS 响应的形状和归一化。使用贝叶斯推断将该框架应用于 COHERENT CsI 和液氩数据集，得到的归一化约束与合作组报告的结果相当，同时还提供了对相关谱形的额外约束。我们还进行了联合分析，利用两个数据集的互补灵敏度，约束任一数据集单独使用时都较难获取的微分截面特征。该框架已在开源 Julia 软件包 Newtrinos.jl 中实现，该软件包支持模块化正向建模、并行计算和自动微分。

Towards a Global CE$\nu$NS Picture

We present a general framework for extracting and interpreting the Coherent Elastic Neutrino-Nucleus Scattering (CE$\nu$NS) differential cross-section from experimental data. A generalized parametric basis, constructed from common kinematic terms recurring in generalized neutrino interaction models, is employed to numerically constrain the shape and normalization of the observed CE$\nu$NS response. The framework was applied to the COHERENT CsI and liquid argon datasets using Bayesian inference, yielding normalization constraints comparable to those reported by the Collaboration while providing additional constraints on the correlated spectral shape. A combined analysis was also performed, exploiting the complementary sensitivities of the two datasets to constrain features of the differential cross-section that are less accessible to either dataset individually. The framework has been implemented in the open-source Julia package Newtrinos.jl, which supports modular forward modeling, parallel computation, and automatic differentiation.

通过 DIII-D 公共和私营部门实验对聚变示范电站先进面向等离子体材料进行比较鉴定

Comparative qualification of advanced plasma-facing materials for fusion pilot plants through public- and private-sector experiments in DIII-D

A coordinated DIII-D campaign involving four private fusion developers and 12 participating institutions comparatively assessed 44 advanced plasma-facing materials, including the first DIII-D divertor exposure of neutron-irradiated W, to support fusion pilot plant wall and divertor material qualification. Samples were exposed using the Divertor Materials Evaluation System (DiMES) under Ohmic, L-mode, and H-mode conditions with edge-localized modes, at 0.2-2.5 MW m$^{-2}$ on flush geometries and 10-15 MW m$^{-2}$ on 10$^\circ$ angled geometries. Engineered tungsten architectures retained integrity; long-fiber Wf/W showed the clearest crack-arrest behavior. W-Re and K-doped W showed near-ITER-W-like responses, while additively manufactured W-Ta showed heat-flux-sensitive mass losses of 0.64 mg for the flat sample and 2.19-2.87 mg for angled samples. After irradiation to 0.3 dpa at 550$^\circ$C, neutron-irradiated ITER-grade W retained 2.8 times more deuterium than pristine W, while TiB$2$ showed the lowest D$2$ release in the Ohmic set. VTaHfMo was the most stable refractory multi-principal-element alloy. NbC and (Nb${0.5}$Ta${0.5}$)C retained integrity with 0.02-0.03 mg mass loss, whereas ZrC lost 7 mg. CVD SiC retained macroscopic integrity but exhibited an effective Si erosion yield of 0.5, about 5-10 times above prior DIII-D trends. Renewable boron pebble rods underwent plasma-driven recession; 13% of released boron was ionized near the outer strike point and up to 50% was recovered locally. Initial in-situ Cr gross-erosion measurements yielded effective erosion yields of order $10^{-2}$. Together, these results provide cross-material benchmarks for fusion pilot plant down-selection and future AI/ML-assisted plasma-facing-material development.

Precision measurement of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering at a nuclear reactor by CONUS+

We report the most precise measurement to date of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$\nu$NS) from reactor antineutrinos, using the CONUS+ germanium detectors at the Leibstadt nuclear power plant. With an exposure of 1296 kg$\cdot$days reactor-on, a blind likelihood analysis yields a signal with high statistical significance, corresponding to $1565 \pm 155$ CE$\nu$NS events, in agreement with the Standard Model expectation of $1701 \pm 198$ events within 0.5 $\sigma$. The flux-averaged cross section is $(0.57 \pm 0.09)\times10^{-40}$ cm$^{2}$, and the weak mixing angle at a momentum transfer of about 10 MeV is $\sin^2\theta_W = 0.226 \pm 0.022$.

将单个邻近射电星系建模为超高能宇宙线加速器

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

邻近射电星系是超高能宇宙线（UHECRs）加速最有希望的候选源之一。在这项工作中，我们建立了一个具有物理动机、分辨到源的框架，以量化三个最近的 FR-I 射电星系$-$半人马座 A、室女座 A 和天炉座 A 对皮埃尔·奥歇天文台测得的 UHECR 通量的贡献。将由详细喷流加速模型导出的加速谱与河外传播数值模拟相结合，同时将更遥远的射电星系种群视为连续背景。通过仅拟合测得的 UHECR 能谱，我们确定了每个源的相对贡献，并约束了转化为 UHECR 光度的喷流功率比例。我们发现，少数邻近射电星系能够以 $10^{-3}-10^{-2}$ 量级的加速效率解释最高能量 UHECR 通量，而背景贡献仍处于次要地位。所得情景给出的质量组成趋势与观测总体一致，并预测了不同水平的次级中微子通量。这些结果表明，对邻近射电星系进行具有物理基础且针对具体源的建模，为最高能量宇宙线的起源提供了一种可行且具有预测能力的解释。

Modeling individual nearby radio galaxies as ultra-high-energy cosmic-ray accelerators

Nearby radio galaxies are among the most promising candidates for the acceleration of ultra-high-energy cosmic rays (UHECRs). In this work, we develop a physically motivated, source-resolved framework to quantify the contribution of the three nearest FR-I radio galaxies$-$Centaurus A, Virgo A, and Fornax A to the UHECR flux measured by the Pierre Auger Observatory. Acceleration spectra derived from detailed jet-acceleration models are combined with numerical simulations of extragalactic propagation, while the more distant radio-galaxy population is treated as a continuous background. By fitting exclusively the measured UHECR energy spectrum, we determine the relative contribution of each source and constrain the fraction of jet power converted into UHECR luminosity. We find that a small number of nearby radio galaxies can account for the highest-energy UHECR flux with acceleration efficiencies of order $10^{-3}-10^{-2}$, while the background contribution remains subdominant. The resulting scenarios yield mass-composition trends broadly consistent with observations and predict distinct levels of secondary neutrino fluxes. These results demonstrate that physically grounded, source-specific modeling of nearby radio galaxies provides a viable and predictive explanation for the origin of the highest-energy cosmic rays.

一项与三轻子核子衰变搜索互补的 LHC 重子数破坏搜索提案

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们研究了 Super-Kamiokande（Super-K）实验对三轻子核子衰变的现有限制与 TeV 标度下重子数破坏（BNV）的相容性，并论证了利用 LHC 现有数据开展专门搜索的必要性。在有效场论框架内，我们推导了与保持 $B - L/3$ 的 BNV 结构相关的高维算符所受到的模型无关约束。我们证明，与这些算符诱导的树级粲重子衰变相比，Super-K 对圈级三轻子核子衰变的现有限制给出了显著更强的约束。这些约束转化为对 BNV 标度的 $O(1-30)$ TeV 界限，仍保留了在 LHC 搜索 $B - L/3$ 型 BNV 的可能性。受这种互补性的启发，我们研究了一种代表性紫外完备模型的对撞机唯象学，该模型具有一个 $s$-道双夸克共振，它衰变为一个轻子夸克—双轻子对，产生一种独特的末态，其中包含一个反顶夸克、两个带电反轻子以及横向缺失能量。我们的结果表明，尽管 ATLAS 和 CMS 的同号双轻子搜索已经对该模型施加了间接约束，但基于我们所制定选择截断的专门分析显著扩展了对所选基准点的探测范围。特别是，我们证明，仅使用 Run 2 数据即可获得与 Super-K 实验所设定的三轻子核子衰变搜索限制以及 Hyper-Kamiokande 的预期探测范围互补的探测能力。这些选择截断也适用于我们的代表性模型之外的情形。

查看原文标题与摘要

A proposal for an LHC search for baryon number violation in complementarity with trilepton nucleon decay searches

We investigate the compatibility of existing limits on trilepton nucleon decay by the Super-Kamiokande (Super-K) experiment with Baryon Number Violation (BNV) at the TeV scale and motivate a dedicated search using existing data at the LHC. Working within an effective field theory framework, we derive model-independent constraints on higher-dimensional operators associated with $B - L/3$ conserving texture of BNV. We demonstrate that existing Super-K limits on loop-level trilepton nucleon decay provide significantly stronger constraints compared to tree-level charm-baryon decays induced by these operators. These constraints translate into bounds of $O(1-30)$ TeV on the BNV scale, leaving open the possibility of an LHC search for $B - L/3$ type BNV. Motivated by this complementarity, we investigate the collider phenomenology of a representative UV completion featuring an $s$-channel diquark resonance that decays into a leptoquark--dilepton pair, producing a distinctive final state containing an anti-top quark, two charged anti-leptons, and missing transverse energy. Our results indicate that, although same-sign dilepton searches by ATLAS and CMS already place indirect constraints on this model, a dedicated analysis based on the selection cuts we develop significantly extends the reach for our chosen benchmarks. In particular, using only Run 2 data, we demonstrate complementary reach to limits on trilepton nucleon decay searches set by the Super-K experiment and to the projected reach of Hyper-Kamiokande. The selection cuts are applicable beyond our representative model.

查看原文标题与摘要

Making Reconnection Cooler: A relativistic resistive magnetohydrodynamics model for radiative collisionless reconnection

Relativistic magnetic reconnection around astrophysical compact objects is a likely site for particle energization and multiwavelength emission. Kinetic theory has substantially modelled such collisionless radiative relativistic reconnection using a radiative drag force countering the acceleration of the particles. Here, we systematically derive an energy-momentum sink term in relativistic resistive magnetohydrodynamics (RRMHD), informed by kinetic theory and coupled with a charge-starved resistivity prescription which reproduces the fast reconnection rate seen in kinetic calculations, to model radiative losses. We show that our radiative loss term accurately captures important macroscopic properties -- including the temperature in the reconnection layer and the bulk motion energy distribution of the out-flowing plasma -- for inverse Compton cooling applied to two-dimensional RRMHD simulations of a reconnection layer and benchmarked to kinetic simulations. Our results hold in various cooling regimes with a one-to-one mapping to kinetic theory. Our radiative model lays a foundation for future RRMHD simulations of plasma in the relativistic radiative regime, such as in reconnection sites in a local turbulent flow or globally in the magnetospheres of black holes and neutron stars.

具有并合后转动轮廓的混合星

原始发布日期：2026-11-24

我们研究了差分转动对混合星的影响，这类混合星具有从强子物质到色超导夸克物质的一级退禁闭相变。差分转动通过一种现实的四参数唯象转动定律引入，在该定律中，转动构型的最大角速度位置偏离中心。我们聚焦于两类差分转动解，即准环形（C 型）和准球形（A 型），并研究相变的存在所导致的恒星整体性质和角速度轮廓的变化。我们表明，当角动量 $J$ 增加时，$J=const$ 序列的转折点向较低能量密度移动，从而显著缩小了存在具有相变的差分转动中子星的区域。有趣的是，对于 A 型和 C 型解，尽管能量密度不连续，角速度轮廓在整个恒星内仍然连续。此外，我们表明，对于所研究的差分转动定律，在不同状态方程的质量-半径曲线相交的交点处，尽管能量密度轮廓差异很大，各解的转动轮廓却非常接近。这揭示了具有相变和不具有相变的模型在并合后遗迹性质方面可能存在简并性，强调了需要互补的多信使观测量来区分它们。

查看原文标题与摘要

Hybrid Stars with Post-Merger Rotation Profiles

We study the effect of differential rotation on hybrid stars with the first-order deconfinement phase transition from hadronic to color superconducting quark matter. The differential rotation is introduced within a realistic, four-parameter phenomenological rotation law, in which the maximum angular velocity of the rotating configuration is shifted away from the center. We focus on two classes of differentially rotating solutions, namely quasi-toroidal (type C) and quasi-spherical (type A), and study the changes in the star global properties and angular velocity profiles due to the presence of a phase transition. We show that when increasing the angular momentum $J$ the turning points of the $J=const$ sequences shift towards lower energy densities, shrinking considerably the region where differentially rotating neutron stars with phase transitions exists. Interestingly, for both type A and type C solutions, the angular velocity profile is continuous throughout the star despite the discontinuity in the energy density. Moreover, we show that for the differential rotation law we study, at the crossing points where the mass-radius curves for different equations of state intersect, the rotational profiles of the solutions are very close despite large differences in the energy density profiles. This reveals a possible degeneracy between the post-merger remnant properties for models with and without phase transitions, emphasizing the need for complementary multi-messenger observables to distinguish between them.

Forward deuteron breakup on carbon and hydrogen: Coulomb effects and the high-momentum excess

We investigate whether recoil and the Coulomb interaction can explain the excess of protons at large longitudinal momenta in $^{12}$C(d,p)X. We calculate the spectrum in the Glauber--Sitenko model, retaining longitudinal momentum transfer and the transverse momentum of the detected proton. At a beam momentum of 9.1 GeV/c and a longitudinal proton momentum of 0.325 GeV/c in the rest frame of the incident deuteron, the invariant differential cross section with recoil is 2.8--3.6 mb/GeV$^2$, against 18.5 mb/GeV$^2$ in the published spectrum. The Coulomb correction to the channel sum is small in this region. Near the main peak at zero proton angle, the coherent cross section rises by 4--12%, while the sum of coherent breakup and neutron removal changes by less than 0.2% in the transverse approximation. At 3.5 GeV/c we compare the calculated carbon and hydrogen spectra with Sitnik's measurements for several transverse cuts. The effects considered here do not account for the high-momentum excess. Further observables are needed to establish whether it has a quark origin.

Dissipative Tidal Love Numbers of Neutron Stars from Viscous Heating

We compute the leading low-frequency dissipative tidal response of nonrotating relativistic neutron stars. We formulate the response in a worldline effective field theory. We determine the linear dissipative Love number in two complementary ways: from the redshifted viscous entropy production inside the star and from the imaginary, frequency-odd part of the exterior metric. The first method reduces the calculation to an integral over the static equilibrium tide; the second provides an independent check. We apply our approach to publicly available equations of state and assess the corresponding correction to the gravitational-wave phase of neutron-star binaries. For an equal-mass $1.4+1.4M_\odot$ binary with both stars at a redshifted temperature $T_\infty=10^5\,\mathrm K$ and a standard electron shear viscosity, tidal dissipation shifts the gravitational-wave phase by less than $10^{-3}$ cycles between $20\,{\rm Hz}$ and $1\,{\rm kHz}$ for the four equations of state considered. Including the resulting rise in the stars' temperature in a simple thermal model reduces the shift below $10^{-6}$ cycles. For the viscosity model considered here, shear-viscous tidal dissipation in cold neutron stars is negligible for gravitational-wave phasing. This suggests that a measurable dissipative tidal signature would have to come from a different mechanism.

Bayesian Inference of the Landau Parameter G 0 ′ from Joint Gamow-Teller Measurements

$G'_0$ is the leading-order Landau-Migdal parameter that characterizes the spin-isospin dependent nucleon-nucleon interaction. Consequently, the $G'_0$ is closely related to the Gamow-Teller resonance (GTR), the beta and double-beta decay rates of finite nuclei, the response of hot and dense nucleonic matter that modifies the neutrino-nucleon absorption rates in core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe) and binary neutron star (BNS) mergers, and finally the critical density for pion condensation in neutron stars. In this letter, for the first time, we report the $G'_0$ with quantified uncertainty in the framework of Bayesian inference, using a self-consistent standard Skyrme Random Phase Approximation (RPA) model and joint constraints from experimental GTR measurements on $^{208}\mathrm{Pb}$, $^{132}\mathrm{Sn}$, $^{90}\mathrm{Zr}$. Our extracted $G_0'$ at saturation density is $0.48\pm0.034$, which is close to the prediction of a few existing Skyrme models that consider spin-isospin observables but smaller than the traditional ones extracted from pion-exchange models. The reasons for this deviation, like the value of the nucleon effective mass $\frac{m^*}{m}$ and the density dependence of $G'_0$, are investigated. The density-dependent $G_0'$ values extracted in this work may guide the construction of new energy density functionals that aims to self-consistently describe the dense matter properties in the spin-isospin channel.

Evolution of collectivity and emergence of octupole correlations in Br 77

The high-spin states of 77Br have been investigated using the heavy-ion fusion evaporation reaction 65Cu(19F, αp2n)77Br at the beam energy of 80 MeV. The level scheme has been upgraded by adding a new band and electric dipole (E1 ) transitions between the yrast positive- and negative-parity bands. Lifetimes of excited states have been measured using the Doppler-shift attenuation method to extract the transition strengths and transitional quadrupole moment (Qt). The deduced transitional quadrupole moment decreases with the increasing spin for both positive- and negative-parity bands, which is supported by the triaxial projected shell model calculations. The enhanced B (E1 ) strength along with B (E1 )/B (E 2) branching ratio suggests the presence of octupole correlations in 77Br.

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

摘要 电荷交换为在反应堆尺度下预期出现的、原本可能被视为“不可穿透的刮削层”中的加料提供了一条途径。来自DIII-D无源主离子电荷交换复合光谱（pMICER）系统的光谱分辨\Baa~测量证实了高能（$>\mathrm{1keV}$）电荷交换原子中性粒子群的存在。 这些测量为使用FIDASIM代码进行的中性粒子输运模拟提供了强有力的实验约束；模拟表明，在没有辅助弹丸加料的典型DIII-D H模等离子体中，在pMICER测量位置（低场侧中平面），体积积分主离子源的很大一部分位于台基顶部以内的半径处。 我们使用一种几何“加料效率”度量评估了加料的极向变化，该度量考虑了磁通膨胀和表面积等因素。我们发现，低场侧中平面是最高效的加料位置。总体而言，在假设边缘中性粒子密度均匀分布时，偏滤器位形H模等离子体中来自主室的加料效率约为x点附近的十倍。这一几何优势抵消了通常在x点附近发现的较高中性粒子密度。 多种等离子体参数会影响这些CX中性粒子为等离子体加料的效率。随着离子温度升高，粒子源变宽；但随着密度、\Zeff和同位素质量增大，粒子源变窄，这是由于这些参数会影响电荷交换的相对概率以及所产生中性粒子的能量。此外，对于Alcator C-Mod、ITER以及SPARC等其他高场设计，从分离面出发的这些中性粒子的有效性也降低到相近程度。对于ITER，空间尺度和电子密度的增大共同降低了加料；而对于紧凑型高场装置，这一效应主要源于已经达到或根据更大的Greenwald密度极限预测会达到的显著更高的电子密度。

查看原文标题与摘要

Plasma fueling due to thermal charge exchange neutrals on DIII-D and future reactors

Abstract Charge exchange provides a pathway for fueling in what may otherwise be considered ``opaque scrape off layers'' that are expected at reactor scale. Spectrally resolved \Baa~measurements from the DIII-D passive main-ion charge exchange recombination spectroscopy (pMICER) system confirm the presence of a population of high energy ($>\mathrm{1keV}$) charge exchange atomic neutrals. These measurements serve as strong experimental constraints for neutral transport simulations using the FIDASIM code which demonstrate that a significant fraction of the volume integrated main ion source is located at radii inside the pedestal top at the pMICER measurement location (low field side midplane) of typical DIII-D H-mode plasmas without auxiliary pellet fueling. We evaluated the poloidal variation of fueling using a geometric “fueling efficiency” metric, which accounts for factors like flux expansion and surface area. We found that the low-field side midplane is the most efficient fueling location. Overall, fueling efficiency from the main chamber is approximately ten times greater than near the x-point in diverted H-mode plasmas when assuming a uniformly distributed edge neutral density. This geometric advantage offsets the higher neutral densities typically found near the x-point. A variety of plasma parameters affect the efficiency with which these CX neutrals fuel the plasma. The particle source broadens with increased ion temperature, but narrows with increased density, \Zeff, and isotope mass due to the effects these parameters have on the relative probability of charge exchange and the energy of the resulting neutrals. Additionally, for Alcator C-Mod, ITER, and other high field designs such as SPARC, the effectiveness of these neutrals starting from the separatrix is reduced to a similar extent. For ITER, the combination of increased spatial scales and electron density reduce the fueling, while for the compact high field devices the effect is dominantly due to substantially higher electron densities that were achieved or are predicted based on the larger Greenwald density limits.

我们分析了手征有效场论中次次次次领头阶（N4LO）的次领头阶三核子接触相互作用项。我们对T = 1 / 2和T = 3 / 2态进行同位旋投影，发现13个算符中只有11个在T = 1 / 2道中有贡献。通过投影到动量空间中N − d散射态渐近构型的分波上，我们发现了十种低能常数组合的贡献。这些结果为在少体计算中纳入N4LO三体力以及通过对数据进行数值拟合来约束这些三体力提供了有用的基础。

Partial-wave decomposition of isospin-projected subleading three-nucleon contact interactions

We analyze the subleading three-nucleon contact interaction terms at next-to-next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order (N4LO) in chiral effective field theory. We perform the isospin projection into T = 1 / 2 and T = 3 / 2 states, and find that only 11 of the 13 operators contribute in the T = 1 / 2 channel. By projection on the partial waves of the asymptotic configuration of N − d scattering states in momentum space, we find contributions from ten combinations of low-energy constants. These results provide a useful basis for including N4LO three-body forces in few-body calculations and for constraining them through numerical fits to data.

EAST上电子加热等离子体中离子温度钳制效应的实验研究

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

摘要 在EAST上系统研究了离子温度（Ti）钳制效应，该效应严重限制了电子加热等离子体中能量约束的改善。在等离子体电流Ip ≤ 550 kA的电子回旋共振加热（ECRH）和低杂波（LHW）情景下，芯部离子温度普遍被钳制在约1.25 keV。实验结果表明，在高等离子体电流（600 kA）和高密度条件下，能量约束时间超过电子-离子碰撞时间，从而实现高效的碰撞离子加热，并打破钳制效应，使芯部离子温度最高达到2 keV。相比之下，在Ip ≤ 550 kA时，即使能量约束时间变得与电子-离子碰撞时间相当，钳制效应仍然存在，这表明除简单的时间尺度匹配之外还存在其他机制。此外，在400 kA下进行脉冲氩（Ar）杂质注入，可有效地将芯部离子温度提高约80%至1.8 keV，超过钳制阈值。这一增强伴随着离子热扩散率的显著降低，以及主导湍流不稳定性模由TEM和ETG占主导跃迁为ITG和ETG占主导，表明离子通道约束得到显著改善。这些发现为理解电子加热等离子体中的离子加热和能量约束物理提供了重要实验证据，并支持为未来聚变装置开发先进运行情景。

查看原文标题与摘要

Experimental investigation of ion temperature clamping effect in electron-heated plasmas on EAST

Abstract The ion temperature (Ti) clamping effect, which severely constrains the improvement of energy confinement in electron-heated plasmas, has been systematically investigated in EAST. The core ion temperature is universally clamped at approximately 1.25 keV under electron cyclotron resonance heating (ECRH) and lower hybrid wave (LHW) scenarios with plasma current Ip ≤ 550 kA. Experimental results show that under high plasma current (600 kA) and high density conditions, the energy confinement time exceeds the electron-ion collision time, enabling efficient collisional ion heating and breaking the clamping effect with a maximum core ion temperature up to 2 keV. In contrast, the clamping effect persists at Ip ≤ 550 kA even when the energy confinement time becomes comparable to the electron-ion collision time, indicating additional mechanisms beyond simple time-scale matching. Furthermore, pulsed argon (Ar) impurity injection at 400 kA effectively elevates the core ion temperature by approximately 80% to 1.8 keV, exceeding the clamping threshold. This enhancement is accompanied by a significant reduction in ion thermal diffusivity and a transition of the dominant turbulent instability modes from TEM and ETG dominance to ITG and ETG dominance, demonstrating a pronounced improvement in ion channel confinement. These findings provide important experimental evidence for understanding ion heating and energy confinement physics in electron-heated plasmas, and support the development of advanced operational scenarios for future fusion devices.

奇异夸克星与中子星的共存：原中子星中的亚稳性与成核

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

如果奇异夸克物质（SQM）是绝对稳定的，那么强子中子星（NSs）和奇异夸克星（QSs）可能在所谓的双族情景中共存。一个关键问题是如何解释强子NSs为何能够作为长寿命亚稳态天体存续，而不是迅速转化为稳定的QSs。由于所有NSs诞生时都是炽热的原中子星（PNSs），它们存在的一个必要条件是，并非每颗PNS中都能达到由首个临界SQM液滴成核所触发的转化条件。我们研究了典型PNS条件下临界SQM液滴的热成核，并通过抑制小于双夸克相干长度的液滴中的配对，计及味组成涨落以及有限尺寸效应对色超导性的影响。我们将成核条件定义为这样一种热力学状态：在该状态下，预期临界SQM液滴会以统计方式在系统的特征动力学时间尺度内成核。在这些条件下，约化成核势垒近乎普适，W * / T ≃ 167，偏差在几个单位以内。我们探索了类袋模型的SQM参数空间，并采用与近期双族情景贝叶斯分析相容的固定强子物态方程；我们找到了能使一颗标准的1.4 M ⊙ NS存续度过PNS阶段的参数化和表面张力，这是双族情景的一个必要条件。对于强子模型与夸克模型的这一特定组合，要求这种存续会为强子-夸克表面张力设定下限，即σ ≳ 67 MeV fm − 2。

查看原文标题与摘要

Coexistence of strange quark stars and neutron stars: Metastability and nucleation in protoneutron stars

If strange quark matter (SQM) is absolutely stable, hadronic neutron stars (NSs) and strange quark stars (QSs) may coexist in the so-called two-families scenario. A key issue is explaining how hadronic NSs can survive as long-lived metastable objects, rather than promptly converting into stable QSs. Since all NSs are born as hot protoneutron stars (PNSs), a necessary condition for their existence is that the conditions for conversion, triggered by the nucleation of the first critical SQM droplet, are not reached in every PNS. We investigate the thermal nucleation of critical SQM droplets under representative PNS conditions, accounting for flavor-composition fluctuations and finite-size effects on color superconductivity by suppressing pairing in droplets smaller than the diquark coherence length. We define the nucleation conditions as the thermodynamic state at which a critical SQM droplet is expected to nucleate statistically within the characteristic dynamical timescale of the system. Under these conditions, the reduced nucleation barrier is nearly universal, with W * / T ≃ 167 to within a few units. Exploring the SQM parameter space of a baglike model, with a fixed hadronic equation of state compatible with a recent Bayesian analysis of the two-families scenario, we find parametrizations and surface tensions for which a canonical 1.4 M ⊙ NS survives the PNS stage, a necessary condition for the two-families scenario. For this specific combination of hadronic and quark models, requiring such survival sets a lower bound on the hadron-quark surface tension, σ ≳ 67 MeV fm − 2 .

中国锦屏地下实验室的山体缪子成像

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

中国锦屏地下实验室（CJPL）位于锦屏山下方∼ 2 400 m处，是世界上最深、规模最大（∼ 300 , 000 m 3）的地下设施之一，开展暗物质、核天体物理和中微子实验。我们报告了在这一非凡深度开展的首次缪子射线成像（缪子成像）。利用为锦屏中微子实验研制的一吨级原型机探测到的宇宙线缪子，在3 km横向范围内进行了无侵入式地下密度测绘。直径1.3 m的探测器提供近乎各向同性的接收度以及∼ 4.5 °的角分辨率。通过将预测的地表缪子通量分布与地下测量结果相关联，我们重建了一个方向不透明度图，该图约束了上覆岩层的密度结构，并与卫星导出的地形模型高度吻合。这项工作证明了在具有千米尺度上覆岩层的极深环境下开展缪子成像的可行性，并为未来的地球物理应用和大型设施（如完整的锦屏中微子实验）建立了稳健的方法。基于这一经过验证的上覆岩层模型，我们进一步预测了CJPL-II中八个实验大厅的总缪子通量，为其物理研究计划提供了关键输入。

Mountain muography for the China Jinping Underground Laboratory

The China Jinping Underground Laboratory (CJPL), located ∼ 2 400 m beneath Jinping Mountain, is one of the world’s deepest and largest ( ∼ 300 , 000 m 3 ) underground facilities, hosting dark matter, nuclear astrophysics, and neutrino experiments. We report the first muon radiography (muography) conducted at this extraordinary depth. Cosmic muons detected by a one-ton prototype developed for the Jinping Neutrino Experiment were used to perform noninvasive subsurface density mapping over a 3 km lateral range. The 1.3 m diameter detector provides nearly isotropic acceptance and an angular resolution of ∼ 4.5 ° . By correlating the predicted surface muon flux distributions with the underground measurements, we reconstruct a directional opacity map that constrains the density structure of the overburden and shows excellent agreement with satellite-derived terrain models. This work demonstrates the feasibility of muography at extreme depths with kilometer-scale overburden and establishes a robust methodology for future geophysical applications and large-scale facilities, such as the full Jinping Neutrino Experiment. Based on this validated overburden model, we further predict the total muon fluxes for the eight experimental halls in CJPL-II, providing essential input for their physics programs.

事例形状工程下的D 0 − D s +椭圆流劈裂：对粲强子序贯强子化的探测

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

近期工作提出了夸克-胶子等离子体中开放粲强子的序贯强子化，其中结合更紧密的粒子种类（如D s +）较早在接近1.2 T c时形成，而D 0较晚在T c时形成。该工作表明，相对于传统的同时强子化基线，这一机制会自然反转D 0 − D s +椭圆流劈裂Δ v 2 ≡ v 2 ( D 0 ) − v 2 ( D s + )的符号。在本工作中，我们证明，与仅进行无偏测量相比，事例形状工程（ESE）能够更明确地区分这两种图景。通过在s NN = 5.02 TeV的Pb–Pb碰撞中，在0–10%和30–50%中心度区间选择大q 2和小q 2事例，我们表明，几何驱动的粲介子v 2增强可以与强子化时间响应分离：序贯情景下中等p T处的正Δ v 2 ( D 0 − D s + )随q 2系统性增大，而相应的响应斜率χ呈现出依赖粒子种类的层级关系χ ( D 0 ) > χ ( D s + )，该关系不受整体流归一化的影响，且在同时强子化基线中不存在。在同时强子化情形下，劈裂接近零或为负，并且不遵循相同的几何标度。值得注意的是，半中心的30–50%区间成为最佳窗口，因为夸克-胶子等离子体寿命与初始偏心率之间的非单调相互作用使晚期流转换最大化。D s + / D 0产额比的q 2比值仍接近一，这支持该劈裂是一种动力学流效应，而非化学产额变化。这些结果表明，ESE下的Δ v 2 ( D 0 − D s + )和响应斜率χ可作为QCD跃迁温度附近粲强子化时空结构的互补微分探针。

查看原文标题与摘要

D 0 − D s + elliptic-flow splitting under event-shape engineering: A probe of sequential charm hadronization

Recent work has proposed sequential hadronization of open-charm hadrons in the quark-gluon plasma, wherein more tightly bound species such as D s + form earlier near 1.2 T c and D 0 forms later at T c . That work showed that this mechanism naturally reverses the sign of the D 0 − D s + elliptic-flow splitting Δ v 2 ≡ v 2 ( D 0 ) − v 2 ( D s + ) relative to the conventional simultaneous baseline. In this work, we demonstrate that event-shape engineering (ESE) provides a sharper discrimination between the two pictures than inclusive measurements alone. By selecting large- q 2 and small- q 2 events in 0–10% and 30–50% centrality classes in Pb–Pb collisions at s NN = 5.02 TeV , we show that the geometry-driven enhancement of charm-meson v 2 can be separated from the hadronization-time response: the positive Δ v 2 ( D 0 − D s + ) at intermediate p T in the sequential scenario grows systematically with q 2 , while the corresponding response slope χ reveals a species-dependent hierarchy χ ( D 0 ) > χ ( D s + ) that is robust against the overall flow normalization and absent in the simultaneous baseline. In the simultaneous case, the splitting is near zero or negative and does not follow the same geometry scaling. Notably, the semicentral 30–50% class emerges as the optimal window, because the nonmonotonic interplay between quark-gluon plasma lifetime and initial eccentricity maximizes the late-stage flow conversion. The q 2 ratios of the D s + / D 0 yield ratio remain close to unity, supporting that the splitting is a dynamical flow effect rather than a chemical yield modification. These results suggest Δ v 2 ( D 0 − D s + ) and the response slope χ under ESE as complementary differential probes of the space-time structure of charm hadronization near the QCD transition temperature.

GRS 1915+105中指向翘曲吸积盘的毫角秒尺度喷流重新定向

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

黑洞X射线双星GRS 1915+105在被发现后表现出持续超过26年的X射线活动，随后于2018年进入长期的低X射线流量状态，同时在射电波段仍然明亮。多波段观测表明，这种低X射线状态源于一层复杂吸收物质的出现，其确切性质仍然未知。由于X射线发射被遮蔽，射电喷流观测为观察与这一阶段相关的物理变化提供了无遮挡的视野。最近发表的甚大阵列（VLA）和东亚VLBI网（EAVN）观测揭示了喷流位置角（PA）的显著变化。在此，我们给出更高分辨率的VLBI观测，以研究遮蔽事件与靠近发射区的喷流行为之间的联系。我们在跨越2019--2021年的四个历元中，使用甚长基线阵列（VLBA）和欧洲VLBI网（EVN）观测了处于早期X射线遮蔽阶段的GRS 1915+105，对多种喷流和吸积行为进行了采样。本文重点研究四个历元中的毫角秒尺度喷流形态和PA变化，并结合已报道的X射线行为加以讨论。2018年之后测得的许多PA均显著偏离GRS 1915+105遮蔽前的喷流轴，该喷流轴在毫角秒尺度上的加权平均值为142^ ̧irc ± 7^ ̧irc。我们发现相对于该轴的最大偏移为57^ ̧irc ± 8^ ̧irc，这是迄今为止针对GRS 1915+105报道的最大此类偏移。我们的四个历元揭示了多种多样的喷流行为，包括具有较大PA偏移的稳定致密源、具有相似较大偏移的明亮耀发源、PA更接近遮蔽前喷流轴的延展喷流，最后则是未探测到。各个八小时历元内均无可测量的PA变化，这排除了内盘快速Lense-Thirring进动是喷流重新定向直接驱动机制的可能性。相反，考虑到所涉及的时间尺度、系统的几何构型以及与X射线遮蔽事件的明显联系，这些因素支持辐射驱动的翘曲吸积盘；这种构型可以自然地解释持续遮蔽。

Milliarcsecond-scale jet reorientations in GRS 1915+105 pointing towards a warped accretion disc

The black hole X-ray binary GRS 1915+105 exhibited persistent X-ray activity for more than 26 years after its discovery, prior to entering a prolonged low X-ray flux state in 2018, while remaining bright at radio wavelengths. Multi-wavelength observations suggest that this low X-ray state results from the emergence of a complex layer of absorbing material, whose exact nature remains unknown. Since the X-ray emission is obscured, radio jet observations provide an unobstructed view of the physical changes associated with this phase. Recently published Very Large Array (VLA) and East Asian VLBI Network (EAVN) observations have revealed substantial changes in the jet position angle (PA). Here, we present higher-resolution VLBI observations to investigate the connection between the obscuration event and the jet behaviour close to the launch region. We observed GRS 1915+105 with the Very Long Baseline Array (VLBA) and European VLBI Network (EVN) during the early X-ray obscuration phase across four epochs spanning 2019--2021, sampling a diverse range of jet and accretion behaviours. In this paper, we focus on the milliarcsecond jet morphology and PA changes across the four epochs and contextualize these in relation to the reported X-ray behaviour. Many of the PAs measured post-2018 are significantly offset from GRS 1915+105's pre-obscuration jet axis, which has a weighted mean of 142^ ̧irc ± 7^ ̧irc on milliarcsecond scales. We find a maximum offset of 57^ ̧irc ± 8^ ̧irc from this axis, the largest such offset reported for GRS 1915+105 to date. Our four epochs reveal a broad variety of jet behaviours, ranging from a stable, compact source with a large PA offset, to a bright, flaring source with a similarly large offset, to an extended jet with a PA that is closer to the pre-obscuration axis, and finally to a non-detection. The lack of measurable PA changes within individual eight-hour epochs rules out rapid Lense-Thirring precession of the inner disc as the direct driver of the jet reorientation. Instead, given the timescales involved, coupled with the system's geometry and the apparent connection to the X-ray obscuration event, favour a radiation-driven warped accretion disc, in a configuration that could naturally explain the persistent obscuration.

Gamow Teller共振的信息含量：采用自洽Skyrme模型的贝叶斯推断

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

许多重要的电荷交换弱过程，例如β衰变、双β衰变、中微子-原子核反应和中微子-核子反应，都与依赖自旋和同位旋的核力密切相关，然而其中很少有过程能够在地面实验中得到精确测量，甚至被观测到。有限原子核中的Gamow-Teller共振（GTR）是这种自旋-同位旋力为数不多的实验探针之一。在一篇姊妹快报中，我们利用贝叶斯推断，从GTR测量中获得了表征自旋-同位旋力主要部分的Landau-Migdal参数$G'_0$的概率分布。在这项后续工作中，我们定量研究了这一贝叶斯分析所产生的相关性。根据后验分布，我们确定了两种标准Skyrme参数化（nuSky*），它们以统一方式再现了有限原子核的基态可观测量、GTR的显著特征以及中子星物质的体性质。由于有限原子核和中子星物质中的集体激发相互关联，我们使用从后验分布中采样的Skyrme相互作用，约束炽热致密的致密星物质中轴向流、带电流中微子-核子反应率，作为本研究的首个天体物理应用。由GTR提供信息的计算大幅减小了中微子不透明度的离散范围；尽管如此，由于亚饱和密度下的对称能约束较差，仍然存在显著不确定性。在受到良好约束的自旋-同位旋相互作用基础上进一步约束这一物理量，将为系统确定致密物质中的中微子相互作用铺平道路。

Information content of the Gamow Teller resonance: Bayesian inference with self-consistent Skyrme models

Many important charge-exchange weak processes, such as beta decays, double beta decays, and neutrino-nucleus and neutrino-nucleon reactions, are closely related to the spin- and isospin-dependent nuclear force, yet few of them can be precisely measured, or even observed, in terrestrial experiments. The Gamow-Teller resonance (GTR) in finite nuclei is one of the few experimental probes of this spin-isospin force. In a companion letter, we obtained the probability distribution of the Landau-Migdal parameter $G'_0$, which characterizes the main part of the spin-isospin force, from GTR measurements using Bayesian inference. In this follow-up work, we quantitatively study the correlations emerging from this Bayesian analysis. From the posterior, we identify two standard Skyrme parameterizations (nuSky*) that reproduce, in a unified manner, ground-state observables of finite nuclei, the salient features of the GTR, and the bulk properties of neutron-star matter. Since collective excitations in finite nuclei and in neutron-star matter are related, we use the Skyrme interactions sampled from the posterior to constrain the axial-current, charged-current neutrino-nucleon reaction rates in hot and dense compact-star matter, as the first astrophysical application of this study. The GTR-informed calculations substantially reduce the spread of the neutrino opacity; nevertheless, significant uncertainties remain due to the poorly constrained symmetry energy at sub-saturation densities. Constraining this quantity on top of a well-constrained spin-isospin interaction would pave the way towards a systematic determination of neutrino interactions in dense matter.

旋转 p-11B 等离子体中多流体平衡的参数扫描：对聚变功率和轫致辐射损失的影响

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

摘要 我们研究了温度、参考密度、燃料离子比和旋转如何影响给定剖面的 p-¹¹B 平衡的聚变功率和轫致辐射损失。多流体平衡求解器 VEQ-MF（Veloce 平衡–多流体）将广义玻尔兹曼密度响应和准中性静电极化与谱 Grad–Shafranov 求解器耦合，并使用漂移麦克斯韦分布聚变反应性和相对论性轫致辐射模型评估所得场。对于具有相同给定输入的四种情形，有限差分结果与 VEQ-MF 结果在总储能和两种功率方面的差异均在 0.49% 以内。EHL-2 和 EHL-3B 几何构型中的参数扫描表明，离子温度和电子温度以不同方式影响反应率响应和辐射响应，同时二者都会改变平衡密度分布。提高参考密度会使两种功率均近似按平方增大，而其比值的变化要小得多。在参考电子密度固定时，改变燃料离子比 f = N_B0/N_p0 会使聚变功率与轫致辐射功率之比在 f = 0.08 附近出现一个宽峰值，这是在本文考察的等旋转情形下得到的。当质子和硼离子以相同角速度沿环向旋转时，更快的旋转会促进硼在外侧积聚并增强轫致辐射。在保持质子旋转固定的同时降低硼的角速度，会削弱静电极化并降低辐射。相对环向流动在本文考察的范围内提高了有效反应率系数，而该系数与质子–硼重叠之间的竞争决定了总聚变功率的响应。

Parameter scan of multi-fluid equilibria in rotating p-11B plasmas: Effects on fusion power and Bremsstrahlung losses

Abstract We investigate how temperature, reference density, fuel-ion ratio and rotation affect the fusion power and bremsstrahlung losses of prescribed-profile p-¹¹B equilibria. The multi-fluid equilibrium solver VEQ-MF (Veloce Equilibrium–Multi-Fluid) couples generalized Boltzmann density responses and quasineutral electrostatic polarization to a spectral Grad–Shafranov solver, and evaluates the resulting fields with a drift-Maxwellian fusion reactivity and a relativistic bremsstrahlung model. For four cases with the same prescribed inputs, the finite-difference and VEQ-MF results agree to within 0.49% in total stored energy and both powers. Parameter scans in the EHL-2 and EHL-3B geometries show that the ion and electron temperatures affect the reaction-rate and radiation responses differently, while both modify the equilibrium density distribution. Increasing the reference density raises both powers almost quadratically and changes their ratio much less. At fixed reference electron density, varying the fuel-ion ratio f = N_B0/N_p0 produces a broad maximum in the fusion-to-bremsstrahlung ratio near f = 0.08 in the equal-rotation case examined here. When protons and boron ions rotate toroidally at the same angular velocity, faster rotation promotes outboard boron accumulation and increases bremsstrahlung. Reducing the boron angular velocity while holding the proton rotation fixed weakens the electrostatic polarization and lowers the radiation. Relative toroidal flow raises the effective reaction-rate coefficient over the range examined here, and the competition between this coefficient and the proton–boron overlap determines the total fusion-power response.

用于 EAST 破裂预测的分层多模态到单模态知识蒸馏

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

摘要 等离子体破裂是对托卡马克安全的严重威胁。现有的数据驱动预测器主要依赖时间序列诊断信号，而可见光图像可提供互补的空间线索，包括等离子体形变、局部增亮和辐射结构演化。尽管图像模态提高了模型的判别能力，但也显著增加了推理期间的计算成本。为解决这一问题，我们在同步的 EAST 多模态数据集上提出了一种用于破裂预测的分层多模态到单模态知识蒸馏框架。在训练期间，使用可见光图像和时间序列信号训练多模态教师模型，该模型通过基于 Transformer 的编码器和原型引导的时空超图模块学习等离子体表征。在推理期间，仅保留时间序列学生模型，并通过图结构级、表征级和决策级蒸馏来迁移多模态知识。在包含 1307 次放电的 EAST 数据集上，在 10 ms 有效预警约束下，当 FPR 分别为 10% 和 5% 时，多模态教师模型表现优异，TPR 分别达到 98.77% 和 97.53%，AUC 达到 99.15%。蒸馏后的单模态学生模型在推理期间依赖时间序列输入，当 FPR 分别为 10% 和 5% 时，TPR 仍分别达到 92.59% 和 81.48%，AUC 达到 95.77%。同时，其推理仅需 3.13 ms、26.50 G FLOPs 和 23.18M 个参数。与多模态教师模型相比，蒸馏后的学生模型实现了 2.59× 的推理加速，并将 FLOPs 和参数数量分别减少约 66.06% 和 47.15%。相似性分析证实，蒸馏后的学生模型继承了教师模型在潜在表征、决策边界和结构偏好方面的判别能力。这些结果表明，所提出的框架能够在显著降低推理成本的同时保留多模态学习的判别优势，并为 EAST 中的高效破裂预测提供了一条有效途径。

Hierarchical multi-to-single-modal knowledge distillation for disruption prediction in EAST

Abstract Plasma disruption is a critical threat to tokamak safety. Existing data-driven predictors mainly rely on time-series diagnostic signals, while visible images provide complementary spatial cues including plasma deformation, local brightening, and radiation-structure evolution. Although the image modality improves the model's discriminative capability, it also substantially increases the computational cost during inference. To address this issue, we propose a hierarchical multi-to-single-modal knowledge distillation framework for disruption prediction on a synchronized EAST multimodal dataset. During training, visible images and time-series signals are used to train a multimodal teacher, which learns plasma representations through Transformer-based encoders and a prototype-guided spatiotemporal hypergraph module. During inference, only the time-series student is retained, with multimodal knowledge transferred through graph-structure-level, representation-level, and decision-level distillation. On the 1307-discharge EAST dataset, at FPRs of 10% and 5% under a 10 ms valid-warning constraint, the multimodal teacher achieves strong performance, attaining TPRs of 98.77% and 97.53%, respectively, and an AUC of 99.15%. The distilled unimodal student, which relies on time-series inputs during inference, still achieves TPRs of 92.59% and 81.48% at FPRs of 10% and 5%, respectively, and an AUC of 95.77%. Meanwhile, it requires only 3.13 ms for inference, 26.50 G FLOPs, and 23.18M parameters. Compared with the multimodal teacher, the distilled student achieves a 2.59× inference speedup and reduces the FLOPs and parameter count by approximately 66.06% and 47.15%, respectively. The similarity analysis confirms that the distilled student inherits the teacher's discriminative capability in latent representation, decision boundary, and structural preference. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve the discriminative advantages of multimodal learning while substantially reducing inference cost, and provide an effective route for efficient disruption prediction in EAST.

136 · Nuclear Fusion · 11 月 23–24 日补发

强磁化等离子体中的削弱剖面

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

摘要 在强磁化电容耦合等离子体（CCP）中，离子诱导的电极削弱受沿磁力线的各向异性输运支配，从而导致空间上不均匀且通常缺乏充分表征的削弱模式。尤其是，离子能量分布和角分布、等离子体几何构型以及磁场取向对削弱的耦合影响仍未得到充分理解。 在本研究中，通过实验研究了磁场为 3 T 时运行于氩气 CCP 中的半圆形钼涂层铜电极的空间分辨削弱剖面。该实验配置能够独立改变若干关键参数，包括定义为 θ 的电极表面与磁场之间的夹角、通过对电极设置的等离子体柱至最近接地表面的连接长度、通过电极旋转改变的等离子体柱形状，以及通过受控自偏压改变的鞘层厚度。未直接测量等离子体参数，因为其测定超出了这项以削弱为重点的研究范围，并且在强磁场条件下仍具有实验挑战性。采用能量色散 X 射线光谱结合基于标准样品的多能量分析，对涂层削弱进行了定量测定。 结果揭示了削弱对 θ 的非单调依赖关系，反映了由磁预鞘层塑造的离子能量分布与角分布之间的相互作用。此外，结果表明，削弱剖面强烈依赖于等离子体几何构型：电极几何形状会在等离子体柱拓扑结构中留下印记，而连接长度的变化会导致沿磁力线的局部离子通量不对称。最后，增加鞘层厚度会扩大有效离子收集区域，从而导致离子通量重新分布，并使低角度处的削弱局部增强。这些发现表明，磁化 CCP 中的削弱受各向异性输运、鞘层动力学和等离子体柱几何构型的耦合作用支配，不能仅由离子通量变化来描述，并为强磁场下的等离子体–表面相互作用和材料削弱提供了新的认识。

Erosion profiles in strongly magnetised plasmas

Abstract Ion-induced erosion of an electrode in strongly magnetised Capacitively Coupled Plasmas (CCP) is governed by anisotropic transport along magnetic field lines, leading to spatially non-uniform and often poorly characterised erosion patterns. In particular, the coupled influence of ion energy and angular distributions, plasma geometry, and magnetic-field orientation on erosion remains insufficiently understood. In this work, spatially resolved erosion profiles of semi-circular molybdenum-coated copper electrodes are experimentally investigated in an argon CCP operated at a magnetic field of 3 T. The experimental configuration enables independent variation of key parameters, including the angle between the electrode surface and the magnetic field defined as θ, the plasma column connection length to the nearest grounded surface, set via a counter-electrode, the plasma column shape through electrode rotation, and the sheath thickness through controlled self-bias. Plasma parameters were not measured directly, as their determination was beyond the scope of this erosion-focused study and remains experimentally challenging under strong magnetic field conditions. Coating erosion was quantified using energy-dispersive X-ray spectroscopy combined with a multi-energy, standard-based analysis. The results reveal a non-monotonic dependence of erosion on θ, reflecting the interplay between ion energy and angular distributions shaped by the magnetic pre-sheath. In addition, the erosion profiles are shown to depend strongly on the plasma geometry: electrode geometry imprints the plasma column topology, while variations in connection length lead to local ion-flux asymmetries along magnetic field lines. Finally, increasing sheath thickness expands the effective ion collection region, resulting in a redistribution of ion flux and a localised enhancement of erosion at low angles. These findings demonstrate that erosion in magnetised CCPs is governed by the coupled effects of anisotropic transport, sheath dynamics, and plasma-column geometry, and cannot be captured by ion-flux variations alone, providing new insight into plasma-surface interaction and material erosion under strong magnetic fields.

利用数据驱动的参数空间和降维对仿星器阿尔法粒子约束进行贝叶斯优化

原始发布日期：2026-11-23

摘要 现代仿星器通常通过优化等离子体边界面的形状来设计，其中参数取为傅里叶振幅。许多有前景的优化算法（如贝叶斯方法）要求对参数施加有界约束，并且当每个参数的缩放尺度与其他参数相近时效率最高。对于典型的傅里叶参数化，如何设置这些边界尚不明确：宽松约束会导致边界自相交以及MHD平衡计算频繁失败，而严格的有界约束则会限制表达能力。为解决这些问题，我们在此提出两种用于仿星器优化的新参数空间。两者均以现有仿星器边界的数据集为起点。在第一种方法中，对每个傅里叶自由度应用分位数变换，将数据分布映射为单位区间上的均匀分布。在第二种方法中，对边界上的点应用主成分分析（PCA），随后进行分位数变换。对于这两种方法，变换后的变量成为自由度，并自然地限制在[0, 1]内。PCA方法还具有降维这一额外优势，可以用少量参数实现高表达能力。通过以良好的阿尔法粒子约束为目标的贝叶斯优化对这些方法进行了演示，其中在优化循环内进行导引中心追踪，并采用异步并行化。这些优化得到的仿星器位形在可能远非准对称或准等动力学的场中具有优异的无碰撞快粒子约束。

Bayesian optimization of stellarator alpha-particle confinement using data-informed parameter spaces and dimensionality reduction

Abstract Modern stellarators are typically designed by optimizing the shape of the plasma boundary surface, with the parameters taken to be Fourier amplitudes. Many promising optimization algorithms such as Bayesian methods require bound constraints on the parameters and are most efficient when each parameter is scaled similarly to the others. With the typical Fourier parameterization, it is unclear how to set these bounds: wide constraints lead to self-intersecting boundaries and frequent failures of the MHD equilibrium calculation, while tight bound constraints limit expressiveness. To address these issues, here we propose two new parameter spaces for stellarator optimization. Both begin with a dataset of existing stellarator boundaries. In the first approach, a quantile transformation is applied to each Fourier degree of freedom, mapping the data distribution to a uniform distribution on the unit interval. In the second approach, principal component analysis (PCA) is applied to points on the boundaries, followed by a quantile transformation. For both approaches, the transformed variables become the degrees of freedom, naturally bounded to [0, 1]. The PCA method has the additional benefit of dimensionality reduction, with high expressiveness for a small number of parameters. The methods are demonstrated via Bayesian optimization for good alpha-particle confinement with guiding-center tracing inside the optimization loop, using asynchronous parallelization. These optimizations yield stellarator configurations with excellent collisionless fast-particle confinement in fields that can be far from quasisymmetric or quasi-isodynamic.

The resulting ANC value is [Formula: see text][Formula: see text]fm[Formula: see text]. Elastic scattering cross-section of 10B projectile on light, medium and heavy targets using different effective interactions

This work is a comprehensive and systematic investigation of the elastic scattering of the weakly bound projectile [Formula: see text] from a broad range of light, medium and heavy target nuclei over several incident energies within the double-folding framework. The real and imaginary components of the nuclear optical potential are constructed using the M3Y, R3Y and B3Y-Fetal effective nucleon–nucleon (NN) interactions combined with nuclear density distributions obtained from the two-parameter Fermi (2pF) and relativistic mean-field (RMF) models. The calculated elastic-scattering angular distributions are compared with available experimental data to assess the predictive capabilities of the different interactions and density parametrizations. The calculated angular distributions generally reproduce the main features of the measured elastic-scattering cross-sections, although the quality of agreement varies among the investigated systems and incident energies. Among the interactions considered, the B3Y-Fetal interaction generally provides the best overall agreement with the experimental data with calculations employing RMF densities using the NL2 parameter set, while the phenomenological 2pF densities show comparatively weaker performance in several cases. Based on these findings, empirical relations are proposed to predict suitable [Formula: see text] and [Formula: see text] values for elastic-scattering analyses involving [Formula: see text]B projectiles. Beyond the description of individual systems, this study establishes global trends in the optical-model parameters and their dependence on target mass and incident energy, thereby providing a unified framework for understanding the reaction dynamics of the weakly bound nucleus [Formula: see text].

Measurement of Sn 119 ( p , γ ) Sb 120 and Sn 116 ( p , γ ) Sb 117 reaction cross sections at energies of astrophysical relevance

The cross sections of the $^{119}\mathrm{Sn}{(p,\ensuremath{\gamma})}^{120}\text{Sb}$ and $^{116}\mathrm{Sn}{(p,\ensuremath{\gamma})}^{117}\text{Sb}$ reactions have been measured using the activation technique over the center-of-mass energy ranges of 2.43--7.06 and 2.43--4.92 MeV, respectively. The measured energy range substantially covers the Gamow window of 1.85--4.53 MeV corresponding to stellar temperatures of 2--3 GK. The present measurements extend the measured energy range of the cross section for the $^{119}\mathrm{Sn}(p\text{,}\ensuremath{\gamma})^{120}\mathrm{Sb}$ reaction and help to clarify the existing experimental discrepancies for $^{116}\mathrm{Sn}(p,\ensuremath{\gamma})^{117}\mathrm{Sb}$ reaction when compared with previous measurements. The extracted cross sections and the resulting astrophysical S factors, corrected for electron screening effects, were compared with predictions from the talys and non-smoker statistical models. The $^{116}\mathrm{Sn}(p,\ensuremath{\gamma})^{117}\mathrm{Sb}$ cross sections agree well with the talys predictions. For $^{119}\mathrm{Sn}(p,\ensuremath{\gamma})^{120}\mathrm{Sb}$, however, standard model combinations proved inadequate, necessitating a tailored parameter set to reproduce the data reasonably well. Utilizing these validated models, stellar reaction rates for both proton-capture reactions were calculated using talys and compared with the rates from the JINA REACLIB database.

Enhanced neutrino cooling from parity-doubled nucleons in neutron star cooling simulations

Although restoration of chiral symmetry is predicted by quantum chromodynamics to take place at high baryon density, most modeling of neutron star interiors disregards a chiral phase transition. We model neutron star cores with a parity doublet model, which allows for dynamical chiral symmetry restoration and predicts the appearance of the parity partners of nucleons and hyperons at large densities, as well as deconfined quark matter. We study the thermal evolution of neutron stars, focusing for the first time on the impact of Urca processes involving the parity partners in neutron star cooling simulations. We find that Urca processes for the parity partners of the nucleons significantly affect the thermal evolution of massive stars and allow for improved agreement with observed surface temperature and ages.

Development and performance of the ultrahigh-energy-resolution Dreamline beamline at Shanghai Synchrotron Radiation Facility

The ultrahigh-energy-resolution soft X-ray beamline, “Dreamline,” at the Shanghai Synchrotron Radiation Facility (SSRF), has been successfully constructed and is now fully operational for conducting angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and photoelectron emission microscopy (PEEM) experiments. Both branches of the beamline utilized a sophisticated plane-grating monochromator equipped with four variable-line-spacing gratings, which allowed it to span an energy range of 20–2000 eV. The beam spot size at the ARPES endstation was 60 $$\upmu$$ m (H) $$\times$$ 30 $$\upmu$$ m (V). The vertical size was crucial for determining the energy resolution of the beam, which was influenced by the exit slit. 使用高能 X 射线的 FLASH 放射治疗：基于紧凑型 LINAC 的 FLASH 辐射平台的关键剂量学设计与特性

原始发布日期：2026-10-24

与常规剂量率放射治疗（CONV-RT）相比，超高剂量率放射治疗（FLASH-RT）可在有效杀死癌细胞的同时减少对正常组织的损伤。为满足临床要求和医院安装要求，开发室温紧凑型 FLASH 直线加速器至关重要。由中国工程物理研究院应用电子学研究所和中玖闪光医疗科技有限公司建造的 FLASH-RT 平台可产生超高剂量率 X 射线。在本研究中，我们测量了实验平台中的剂量并验证了我们的设计。将 EBT4 胶片置于固体水中，以测量不同辐射野尺寸下的百分深度剂量（PDD）曲线和剂量分布剖面。基于实验平台和初始束流参数，使用蒙特卡罗 N 粒子输运程序 MCNP6（Version 6）建立蒙特卡罗模型，以优化转换靶厚度并测量 PDD 和束流剖面。为满足用户对不同剂量率的要求并在同一台机器上开展实验，使用蒙特卡罗模型分析了若干剂量率调节方法的可行性。结果表明，在 X 射线 FLASH-RT 实验平台上，当源到表面距离（SSD）为 50 cm 时，最大平均剂量率超过 330 Gy/s，瞬时剂量率约为 7500 Gy/s，远高于触发 FLASH 效应的条件（$$\ge$$ 40 Gy/s）。对于所有 PDD 曲线和剖面，蒙特卡罗模拟结果与实验数据之间的差异均小于 3%。此外，伽马分析（2 mm/2% 标准）显示通过率超过 95%，证实结果完全处于临床可接受的容差范围内。为优化转换靶材料的厚度，通过结合最大转换效率原理得到的平均剂量与连续慢化近似（CSDA）射程相比提高了约 12.7%。对于仅调节平均剂量率，建议采用改变脉冲频率的方法；同时改变平均剂量率和瞬时剂量率的方法则是在次级准直器前添加相应厚度的屏蔽材料。该剂量率范围满足在同一台机器上开展 CONV-RT 和 FLASH-RT 的要求，从而最大限度地减少实验生物学变异。

查看原文标题与摘要

FLASH radiotherapy using high-energy X-rays: critical dosimetry design and characteristics of FLASH radiation platform based on compact LINAC

Compared with conventional dose-rate radiotherapy (CONV-RT), ultrahigh-dose-rate radiotherapy (FLASH-RT) can reduce damage to normal tissues while effectively killing cancer cells. To meet clinical requirements and hospital installation requirements, developing a room-temperature compact FLASH linear accelerator is crucial. The FLASH-RT platform, built by the Institute of Applied Electronics, China Academy of Engineering Physics, and Zhongjiu Flash Medical Technology Co., Ltd., can produce ultrahigh-dose-rate X-rays. In this study, we measured the dose in an experimental platform and verified our design. EBT4 films were mounted in solid water to measure both the percentage depth dose (PDD) curves and dose distribution profiles for various radiation field sizes. Based on the experimental platform and initial beam parameters, a Monte Carlo model was established using the Monte Carlo N-particle transport code MCNP6 (Version 6) to optimize the conversion target thickness and measure the PDDs and beam profiles. To meet the requirements of users for different dose rates and perform the same machine experiment, the possibility of several dose-rate adjustment methods was analyzed using the Monte Carlo model. The results showed that the maximum mean dose rate was over 330 Gy/s and the instantaneous dose rate was approximately 7500 Gy/s at a source-to-surface distance (SSD) of 50 cm on the X-ray FLASH-RT experimental platform, which was much higher than the conditions for triggering the FLASH effect ( $$\ge$$ 40 Gy/s). The discrepancy between the Monte Carlo simulation results and the experimental data was less than 3% for all PDD curves and profiles. Furthermore, gamma analysis (2 mm/2% criteria) demonstrated pass rates exceeding 95%, confirming that the results were well within the clinically acceptable tolerances. To optimize the thickness of the conversion target material, the average dose obtained by combining the principle of maximum conversion efficiency was increased by approximately 12.7% compared to the continuous slowing-down approximation (CSDA) range. The method of adjusting only the average dose rate is recommended to change the pulse frequency, and the method of changing the average and instantaneous dose rates simultaneously involves adding the corresponding thickness of the shielding material before the secondary collimator. This dose-rate range meets the requirements for conducting CONV-RT and FLASH-RT on the same machine, minimizing experimental biological variability.

原始发布日期：2026-10-23

本文，我们给出了 s = 13 ~TeV 质子–质子碰撞中 -标记喷注的能量–能量关联函数（EEC）的首次测量。EEC 探测 QCD 辐射的结构，为涉及粲夸克的部分子簇射中的质量依赖效应提供独特检验，并为包括强子化过程在内的非微扰现象提供一种不同的观察视角。对横动量为 {} 的 -标记喷注和未作味标记的（包容）喷注测量了 EEC，在此横动量下粲夸克质量效应十分显著。我们观察到，与包容喷注相比，粲喷注中的 EEC 振幅受到显著压低，这与预期的大质量夸克辐射压低相一致——这是 QCD 的一项基本预言。尽管振幅存在显著差异，观测到的粲喷注与包容喷注 EEC 峰位相近，表明 QCD 部分子簇射中的 Casimir 因子（区分夸克喷注和胶子喷注）与夸克质量（死锥）效应以及随后的强子化效应之间存在复杂的相互作用。与次领头阶计算和多种 Monte Carlo 事例生成器的比较揭示了该观测量对部分子簇射中的质量效应和强子化过程的敏感性。这些结果为重夸克喷注的理论模型提供了新的约束，并增进了对 QCD 中部分子到强子跃迁的认识。

First measurement of energy-energy correlators in D 0 -tagged jets from proton-proton collisions at s = 13 TeV

In this letter, we present the first measurement of energy–energy correlators (EEC) in -tagged jets from proton–proton collisions at s = 13 ~TeV. EECs probe the structure of QCD radiation, providing a unique test of mass-dependent effects in parton showers involving a charm quark and offering a distinct view into non-perturbative phenomena, including the hadronization process. The EEC is measured for -tagged jets and flavor-untagged (inclusive) jets with transverse momenta of {}, where charm-quark mass effects are significant. We observe a significant suppression of the EEC amplitude in charm jets compared to inclusive ones, consistent with the expected suppression of radiation from massive quarks – a fundamental prediction of QCD. Despite the significant amplitude difference, the observed peak positions of the charm and inclusive-jet EEC are similar, indicating a complex interplay between Casimir factor (differentiating quark and gluon jets), and quark-mass (dead-cone) effects in the QCD parton shower as well as subsequent hadronization effects. Comparisons with next-to-leading order calculations and various Monte Carlo event generators reveal the sensitivity of this observable to mass effects in the parton shower and the hadronization process. These results provide new constraints on theoretical models of heavy-quark jets and offer insights into the parton-to-hadron transition in QCD.

以 E 0 跃迁强度作为约束模型参数的工具：在相互作用玻色子模型和偶偶 Xe 同位素中的应用

原始发布日期：2026-10-23

背景：在任何核结构理论框架中，对 $E0$ 跃迁率的定量描述仍是一项非平凡且持续存在的挑战。此外，这些观测量对这类模型的可行参数空间施加了严格约束。目的：本工作旨在系统研究所选 ${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ 值及其比值在具有单一组态的相互作用玻色子模型整个参数空间内的行为。作为一项具体应用，对偶偶 Xe 同位素链进行了详细分析。方法：将采用相互作用玻色子模型计算所选关键跃迁的 ${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ 值，并研究这些观测量在模型参数空间内的演化。结果：分析了若干关键的 ${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ 量，包括绝对值或比值，并在以 Casten 三角形表示的相互作用玻色子模型参数空间内生成了等高线图。随后，将偶偶 Xe 同位素的计算结果映射到这些等高线图上并与之比较。结论：本分析表明，${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ 值，尤其是特定比值，对相互作用玻色子模型的参数空间提供了严格约束。此外，该参数空间中存在一些区域，在这些区域内，${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ 值在对哈密顿量参数进行微调时实际上保持不变，因此 ${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ 不能用于约束这些参数。

E 0 transition strengths as a tool to constrain model parameters: Application to the interacting boson model and even-even Xe isotopes

Background: In any theoretical framework for nuclear structure, the quantitative description of $E0$ transition rates remains a nontrivial and persistent challenge. Moreover, these observables impose stringent constraints on the viable parameter space of such models.Purpose: This work aims to systematically investigate the behavior of selected ${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ values and their ratios across the entire parameter space of the interacting boson model with a single configuration. Furthermore, there exist regions of this parameter space in which the ${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ values are effectively invariant under fine tuning of the Hamiltonian parameters and, hence ${\ensuremath{\rho}}^{2}(E0)$ cannot be used to constrain the parameters.

中间区效应对跃迁辐射轨道角动量的影响

原始发布日期：2026-10-23

电子穿过真空介质界面时会产生跃迁辐射。该辐射体的形状能够影响发射的角分布，并赋予某种偏振。在本工作中，我们研究了在介电螺旋相位板 (SPP) 内部诱发并由以电子传播方向为中心的探测器记录的跃迁辐射能否携带轨道角动量 (OAM)。对超相对论电子在波区和中间区产生的 UV 跃迁辐射的估算表明，总角动量 (TAM) 在两个区域中均为零，因而可以利用自旋角动量 (SAM) 的估算值获得 OAM。进一步表明，尽管整个辐射的 OAM 在区域之间并未发生显著变化，但其密度分布在中间区可能会从较低极角向较高极角偏移。因此，相对于辐射体，当一个物体置于波区而另一个物体置于中间区时，两个具有相似有限角尺寸的物体所见的表观 OAM 可能不同。
